### [1] [Scale-Gest: Scalable Model-Space Synthesis and Runtime Selection for On-Device Gesture Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12506)
*Abdul Basit,Saim Rehman,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scale-Gest提出了一种运行时自适应的手势检测框架，通过扩展tiny-YOLO架构家族、创建设备校准的ACE（精度-复杂度-能耗）配置文件，并引入轻量级运行时控制器和运动感知ROI门控，在电池供电设备上实现4倍能效提升（6.9mJ降至1.6mJ），同时保持高手势检测性能（事件级F1=0.8-0.9）和低延迟（6ms）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在移动设备上实现基于ML的实时手势检测面临严苛的实时性、能耗和内存约束，尤其是电池电量波动时。现有EdgeAI部署通常依赖单一固定检测器，限制了优化空间，无法根据动态电池状态进行自适应调整。

Method: 1) 将检测器空间扩展为密集的tiny-YOLO架构家族；2) 通过分析不同的模型-分辨率-步长操作点，创建多个设备校准的ACE（精度-复杂度-能耗）配置文件；3) 设计轻量级运行时控制器，根据用户定义和电池约束选择合适的ACE模式；4) 引入运动感知的手势跟踪ROI门控，裁剪输入以降低检测复杂度；5) 提出时间标注的Driver Simulated Gesture (DSG-18)数据集用于真实驾驶场景评估。

Result: 在电池供电的笔记本电脑上运行手势流时，ACE控制器将每帧能耗降低4倍（从6.9mJ降至1.6mJ），同时保持高手势检测性能（事件级F1=0.8-0.9）和低平均延迟（6ms）。与单检测器方法相比，显著降低了能耗和延迟。

Conclusion: Scale-Gest通过运行时自适应框架有效解决了移动设备上手势检测的能耗与性能权衡问题，在保持高检测精度的同时实现了大幅能效提升，证明了动态ACE配置在EdgeAI部署中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Realizing on-device ML-based gesture detection under tight real-time performance, energy and memory constraints is challenging, especially when considering mobile devices with varying battery-power levels. Existing EdgeAI deployments typically rely on a single fixed detector, limiting optimization opportunities. We present Scale-Gest, a novel run-time adaptive gesture detection framework that expands the detector space into a dense family of tiny-YOLO architectures. We introduce multiple novel device-calibrated ACE (Accuracy-Complexity-Energy) profiles by analyzing different model-resolution-stride operating points. A lightweight run-time controller selects an appropriate ACE mode under user-defined and battery constraints, while a motion-aware hand-gesture-tracking ROI gate crops the input for reduced complexity detection. To evaluate performance of our system in real-world car driving scenarios, we introduce a temporally-annotated Driver Simulated Gesture (DSG-18) dataset. Scale-Gest maintains event-level F1 while significantly reducing energy and latency compared to single-detector approaches. On a battery-powered laptop running gesture streams, our ACE controller reduces per-frame energy by 4x (from 6.9 mJ to 1.6 mJ) while maintaining high gesture-detection performance (event-level F1 = 0.8-0.9) and low mean latency (6 ms).

中文摘要翻译: 在严格的实时性能、能耗和内存约束下实现基于机器学习的设备端手势检测具有挑战性，尤其是在考虑电池电量水平变化的移动设备时。现有的EdgeAI部署通常依赖单一固定检测器，限制了优化机会。我们提出Scale-Gest，一种新颖的运行时自适应手势检测框架，将检测器空间扩展为密集的tiny-YOLO架构家族。通过分析不同的模型-分辨率-步长操作点，我们引入了多个新颖的设备校准ACE（精度-复杂度-能耗）配置文件。一个轻量级运行时控制器根据用户定义和电池约束选择合适的ACE模式，同时一个运动感知的手势跟踪ROI门控裁剪输入以降低检测复杂度。为了评估我们系统在真实世界驾驶场景中的性能，我们引入了一个时间标注的驾驶员模拟手势（DSG-18）数据集。与单一检测器方法相比，Scale-Gest在显著降低能耗和延迟的同时保持了事件级F1。在运行手势流的电池供电笔记本电脑上，我们的ACE控制器将每帧能耗降低了4倍（从6.9mJ降至1.6mJ），同时保持高手势检测性能（事件级F1=0.8-0.9）和低平均延迟（6ms）。

</details>


### [2] [MorphOPC: Advancing Mask Optimization with Multi-scale Hierarchical Morphological Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12528)
*Yuting Hu,Lei Zhuang,Chen Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Hua Xiang,Gi-joon Nam,Jinjun Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MorphOPC，一种基于神经形态学操作的多尺度分层模型，用于解决光学邻近校正中几何变换捕获不足的问题，在保真度和制造成本上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于编码器-解码器架构的生成式掩模优化模型难以准确捕获从目标版图到掩模图案的几何变换，导致生成的掩模质量次优，需要一种能显式学习这些形态学变换的方法。

Method: 将掩模生成建模为对局部版图特征的一系列形态学操作序列，提出MorphOPC——一种包含神经形态学模块的多尺度分层模型，用于学习从目标版图到掩模图案的几何变换。

Result: 在基于边缘的OPC和ILT基准测试中，涵盖金属层和通孔层，MorphOPC始终优于最先进方法，实现了更高的打印保真度和更低的制造成本。

Conclusion: MorphOPC通过显式建模形态学操作有效提升了掩模优化质量，展现了在可扩展掩模优化方面的巨大潜力。

Original Abstract: As feature sizes shrink to the nanometer scale, accurately transferring circuit patterns from photomasks to silicon wafers becomes increasingly challenging. Optical proximity correction (OPC) is widely used to ensure pattern fidelity and manufacturability. Recent generative mask optimization models based on encoder-decoder architecture can synthesize near-optimal masks, serving as fast machine learning (ML) surrogates for traditional OPC. However, these models often fail to capture the geometric transformations from target layouts to mask patterns, leading to suboptimal quality. In this work, we formulate mask generation as a sequence of morphological operations on local layout features and propose \textit{MorphOPC}, a multi-scale hierarchical model with neural morphological modules to learn these transformations. Experiments on edge-based OPC and ILT benchmarks across metal and via layers show that \textit{MorphOPC} consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving higher printing fidelity and lower manufacturing cost, demonstrating strong potential for scalable mask optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 随着特征尺寸缩小至纳米尺度，将电路图案从光掩模精确转移到硅晶圆上变得日益困难。光学邻近校正（OPC）被广泛用于确保图案保真度和可制造性。最近基于编码器-解码器架构的生成式掩模优化模型能够合成接近最优的掩模，作为传统OPC的快速机器学习（ML）替代方案。然而，这些模型往往无法捕获从目标版图到掩模图案的几何变换，导致质量次优。在本工作中，我们将掩模生成建模为对局部版图特征的一系列形态学操作序列，并提出MorphOPC——一种带有神经形态学模块的多尺度分层模型，用于学习这些变换。在涵盖金属层和通孔层的基于边缘的OPC和ILT基准测试上的实验表明，MorphOPC始终优于最先进方法，实现了更高的打印保真度和更低的制造成本，展示了在可扩展掩模优化方面的巨大潜力。

</details>


### [3] [CROP: Expert-Aligned Image Cropping via Compositional Reasoning and Optimizing Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12545)
*Zhitong Dong,Chao Li,Jie Yu,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CROP方法，将美学图像裁剪重新定义为多模态推理任务，通过“分析-提案-决策”的分步推理流程激活VLM的美学分析能力，并引入专家偏好对齐模块，使裁剪结果与人类专家一致。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于显著性预测或检索增强的方法未能深入理解图像构图与美学，前者在复杂场景中难以做出构图权衡，后者缺乏对独特场景的自适应推理能力，两者均无法与人类专家的裁剪结果对齐。

Method: CROP方法将美学裁剪转化为多模态推理任务，设计“分析-提案-决策”的分步推理流程，引导VLM像专业摄影师一样思考，逐步分析场景元素和构图原则；同时引入专家偏好对齐模块，使模型决策与人类专家审美一致。

Result: 在多个数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法相比现有方法的优越性及各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 将美学裁剪重新定义为多模态推理任务，通过结构化推理和专家偏好对齐，能有效提升裁剪结果的美学质量，使其更接近人类专家水平。

Original Abstract: Aesthetic image cropping aims to enhance the aesthetic quality of an image by improving its composition through spatial cropping. Previous methods often rely on saliency prediction or retrieval augmentation, ignoring the task's core requirement: a deep understanding of composition and aesthetics. Consequently, saliency-based methods struggle to make compositional trade-offs in complex scenes, while retrieval-based methods blindly refer to similar cases, lacking adaptive reasoning for unique scenes. Both approaches fail to align their automated cropping results with those of human experts. To address the above issues, we propose a novel paradigm that reformulates aesthetic cropping as a multimodal reasoning task, aiming to activate the VLM's analytical and comprehension capabilities in aesthetics. We design a Compositional Reasoning and Optimizing Preference method (CROP) that directs the VLM to think like a professional photographer. It deconstructs a complex and subjective aesthetic problem into an "analysis-proposal-decision" process, reasoning step by step through the analysis of scene elements and compositional principles. Meanwhile, our expert preference alignment module makes the model's decision consistent with human expert aesthetics. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets validate our method's superiority and component effectiveness.

中文摘要翻译: 美学图像裁剪旨在通过空间裁剪改善图像构图来提升其美学质量。以往方法通常依赖显著性预测或检索增强，忽略了该任务的核心需求：对构图和美学的深入理解。因此，基于显著性的方法难以在复杂场景中进行构图权衡，而基于检索的方法盲目参考相似案例，缺乏对独特场景的自适应推理能力。这两种方法都无法使其自动裁剪结果与人类专家对齐。为解决上述问题，我们提出一种新范式，将美学裁剪重新定义为多模态推理任务，旨在激活视觉语言模型在美学方面的分析和理解能力。我们设计了一种构图推理与偏好优化方法（CROP），引导视觉语言模型像专业摄影师一样思考。该方法将复杂且主观的美学问题解构为“分析-提案-决策”的过程，通过对场景元素和构图原则的分析逐步推理。同时，我们的专家偏好对齐模块使模型决策与人类专家审美保持一致。在多个数据集上进行的大量实验验证了本方法的优越性及各组件的有效性。

</details>


### [4] [What Happens Before Decoding? Prefill Determines GUI Grounding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12549)
*Jiaping Lin,Fei Shen,Junzhe Li,Ping Nie,Fei Yu,Ming Li,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现GUI grounding在VLM中遵循两阶段范式（预填充决定候选元素，解码阶段细化坐标），并提出Re-Prefill方法，通过注意力引导的第二次预填充阶段来优化候选UI元素的选择，无需额外训练即可提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有训练无关方法虽进行多次推理（如迭代裁剪或候选聚合），但每次前向传播仍独立解析指令和视觉布局，未实现视觉token间的渐进交互。本文发现GUI grounding存在一个被忽视的瓶颈：预填充阶段决定候选UI元素，而解码阶段只是细化坐标，候选选择的错误无法在解码阶段有效纠正。

Method: 提出Re-Prefill方法，在原始推理中引入注意力引导的第二次预填充阶段。具体而言，提取在各层中从查询位置（即最终token）持续获得高注意力的视觉token作为初步目标假设，并将这些token与指令隐藏状态一起追加到输入中，使模型在坐标生成前重新深入思考其决策。

Result: 在4种VLM和5个基准（ScreenSpot-Pro、ScreenSpot-V2、OSWorld-G、UI-Vision、MMBench-GUI）上实现了一致的性能提升，无需额外训练，在ScreenSpot-Pro上最高提升4.3%。

Conclusion: GUI grounding的预填充阶段是关键瓶颈，通过Re-Prefill方法引入第二次注意力引导的预填充，可以有效改善候选目标选择，从而提升grounding性能，且无需额外训练开销。

Original Abstract: Existing training-free approaches for GUI grounding often rely on multiple inference runs, such as iterative cropping or candidate aggregation, to identify target elements. Despite this additional computation, each forward pass still independently interprets the instruction and parses the visual layout, without enabling progressive interaction among visual tokens. In this paper, we study what happens during GUI grounding in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and identify a previously overlooked bottleneck. We show that grounding follows a two-stage paradigm: the prefill stage determines candidate UI elements, while the decoding stage subsequently refines the final coordinates. This asymmetry establishes prefill as the critical step, as errors in candidate selection cannot be effectively corrected during decoding. Based on this observation, we propose Re-Prefill, a training-free method that revisits inference by introducing an attention-guided second prefill stage to refine target selection. Specifically, visual tokens that consistently receive high attention from the query position, i.e., the final token, across layers are extracted as a preliminary target hypothesis and appended to the input, together with the instruction hidden states, enabling the model to deeply re-think its decision before coordinate generation. Experiments across four VLMs and five benchmarks, including ScreenSpot-Pro, ScreenSpot-V2, OSWorld-G, UI-Vision, and MMBench-GUI, demonstrate consistent improvements without additional training, with gains of up to 4.3% on ScreenSpot-Pro. Code will be available at https://github.com/linjiaping1/Re-Prefill.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的训练无关GUI接地方法通常依赖多次推理运行（如迭代裁剪或候选聚合）来识别目标元素。尽管进行了额外的计算，每次前向传播仍然独立地解析指令和视觉布局，未能实现视觉token之间的渐进交互。本文研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）在GUI接地过程中的内部机制，识别出一个此前被忽视的瓶颈。我们发现，GUI接地遵循两阶段范式：预填充阶段确定候选UI元素，解码阶段随后细化最终坐标。这种不对称性使得预填充成为关键步骤，因为候选选择中的错误无法在解码阶段得到有效纠正。基于这一观察，我们提出Re-Prefill方法，这是一种无需训练的方法，通过引入注意力引导的第二次预填充阶段来重新审视推理过程，以优化目标选择。具体而言，将从查询位置（即最终token）在各层中持续获得高注意力的视觉token提取出来，作为初步目标假设，并与指令隐藏状态一起追加到输入中，使模型在坐标生成之前能够深入重新思考其决策。在包含ScreenSpot-Pro、ScreenSpot-V2、OSWorld-G、UI-Vision和MMBench-GUI在内的四个VLM和五个基准上的实验表明，该方法无需额外训练即可实现一致的性能提升，在ScreenSpot-Pro上最高提升4.3%。代码将开源在https://github.com/linjiaping1/Re-Prefill。

</details>


### [5] [Pyramid Self-contrastive Learning Framework for Test-time Ultrasound Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12567)
*Jiajing Zhang,Bingze Dai,Xi Zhang,Yue Xu,Wei-Ning Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种纯测试时训练框架A2A，用于一次性超声图像去噪，通过自对比学习在金字塔潜空间中分离解剖相似性和噪声随机性，消除了域偏移和预训练成本，在仿真和体内实验中显著提升了SNR和CNR。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统去噪方法依赖于显式噪声假设，在复合噪声条件下效果不佳；基于学习的方法需要大量标注数据和模型参数，存在域偏移问题，且局限于特定噪声类型，常模糊结构细节。

Method: 提出Aperture-to-Aperture (A2A)框架，在测试时对单个含噪SAU信号样本进行训练，通过金字塔潜空间中的自对比学习，将解剖相似性与噪声随机性分离，从解剖空间解码干净图像并丢弃噪声空间。

Result: 仿真实验（噪声0-30dB、不同包含体几何）显示SNR提升69.3%、CNR提升34.4%；体内实验（心脏6个切面、肝脏、肾脏）使用仅两孔径数据，SNR提升84.8%、CNR提升25.7%。

Conclusion: A2A能够在多种成像目标和配置下生成清晰的图像/信号，为更可靠的超声解剖可视化和功能评估铺平了道路。

Original Abstract: The inherent electronic and speckle noise complicates clinical interpretation of ultrasound images. Conventional denoising methods rely on explicit noise assumptions whose validity diminishes under composite noise conditions. Learning-based methods require massive labeled data and model parameters. These pre-defined and pre-trained manners entail an inevitable domain shift in complex in vivo environments, so they are limited to a specific noise type and often blur structural details. In this study, we propose a pure test-time training framework for one-shot ultrasound image denoising and apply it to synthetic aperture ultrasound (SAU), which synthesizes transmit focus from sub-aperture transmissions. Our Aperture-to-Aperture (A2A) framework disentangles anatomical similarity and noise randomness from shuffled sub-apertures through self-contrastive learning in pyramid latent spaces. The clean image is then decoded from the anatomy space, while discarding the noise space. A2A is trained at test time on one noisy sample of SAU signals, so it fundamentally eliminates the domain shift and pretraining costs. Simulation experiments, including electronic noise levels of 0 to 30 dB and different inclusion geometries, demonstrated an improvement of 69.3% SNR and 34.4% CNR by A2A. The in vivo results showed 84.8% SNR and 25.7% CNR gains using only two aperture data of the heart in six echocardiographic views, liver, and kidney. A2A delivers clear images/signals across diverse imaging targets and configurations, paving the way for more reliable anatomical visualization and functional assessment by ultrasound.

中文摘要翻译: 固有的电子噪声和散斑噪声使超声图像的临床解读变得复杂。传统去噪方法依赖于显式噪声假设，而这些假设在复合噪声条件下的有效性会降低。基于学习的方法需要大量标注数据和模型参数。这些预定义和预训练的方式在复杂的体内环境中不可避免地产生域偏移，因此它们局限于特定的噪声类型，且常模糊结构细节。本研究提出了一种纯测试时训练框架用于一次性超声图像去噪，并将其应用于合成孔径超声（SAU），该技术通过子孔径发射合成发射聚焦。我们的孔径到孔径（A2A）框架通过金字塔潜空间中的自对比学习，从打乱的子孔径中分离解剖相似性和噪声随机性。然后从解剖空间解码出干净图像，同时丢弃噪声空间。A2A在测试时对单个含噪SAU信号样本进行训练，从而从根本上消除了域偏移和预训练成本。仿真实验包括0到30 dB的电子噪声水平和不同的包含体几何结构，结果表明A2A使信噪比（SNR）提升69.3%，对比度噪声比（CNR）提升34.4%。体内结果显示，仅使用心脏六个超声切面、肝脏和肾脏的两孔径数据，SNR提升84.8%，CNR提升25.7%。A2A能够在多种成像目标和配置下生成清晰的图像/信号，为更可靠的超声解剖可视化和功能评估铺平了道路。

</details>


### [6] [Learning to Optimize Radiotherapy Plans via Fluence Maps Diffusion Model Generation and LSTM-based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13713)
*Isabella Poles,Simon Arberet,Riqiang Gao,Martin Kraus,Marco D. Santambrogio,Florin C. Ghesu,Ali Kamen,Dorin Comaniciu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散驱动学习优化（L2O）的端到端容积旋转调强放疗（VMAT）计划方法，通过分布匹配蒸馏扩散模型一次性生成通量图，并结合LSTM优化模块快速微调，显著提升了计划效率、灵活性和机器可交付性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: VMAT计划涉及复杂的逆向和嵌套优化，治疗配置变化时需反复重新优化，导致每位患者的计划时间过长，亟需一种高效的端到端解决方案。

Method: 采用分布匹配蒸馏扩散模型学习临床可行的通量图流形，实现一次性生成；在此基础上，利用基于LSTM的学习优化模块学习梯度更新动力学，在推理过程中根据指定剂量目标快速微调通量图。

Result: 在临床和公共前列腺癌数据集上，该方法在计划效率、灵活性和机器可交付性方面均优于现有的端到端VMAT计划器。

Conclusion: 扩散驱动的L2O方法为VMAT计划提供了一种高效、灵活且可交付的端到端解决方案，有望减少临床计划时间并改善治疗质量。

Original Abstract: Volumetric Modulated Arc Therapy (VMAT) is a cornerstone of modern radiation therapy, enabling highly conformal tumor irradiation and healthy-tissue sparing. Yet, its planning solves inverse and nested optimization for multi-leaf collimators, monitor units and dose parameters, while enforcing their consistency to ensure mechanical deliverability. Nevertheless, this process often requires repeated re-optimization when treatment configurations change, resulting in substantial planning time per patient. To address these problems, we present a diffusion-driven Learning-to-Optimize (L2O) method for end-to-end VMAT planning. A distribution-matching distilled diffusion model learns a clinically feasible manifold of fluence maps, enabling their one-shot generation. On top of this, an LSTM-based L2O module learns gradient update dynamics to swiftly refine fluence maps toward prescribed dose objectives during inference. Experimental results on clinical and public prostate cancer cohorts demonstrate improved planning efficiency, flexibility, and machine deliverability over currently available end-to-end VMAT planners.

中文摘要翻译: 容积旋转调强放疗（VMAT）是现代放射治疗的基石，能够实现高度适形的肿瘤照射和健康组织保护。然而，其计划求解涉及多叶准直器、机器跳数和剂量参数的逆向与嵌套优化，同时需确保它们之间的一致性以保证机械可交付性。尽管如此，当治疗配置发生变化时，该过程往往需要反复重新优化，导致每位患者的计划时间显著增加。为解决这些问题，本文提出了一种扩散驱动的学习优化（L2O）方法，用于端到端的VMAT计划。一个分布匹配蒸馏扩散模型学习临床可行的通量图流形，实现其一次性生成。在此基础上，一个基于LSTM的L2O模块学习梯度更新动力学，在推理过程中根据指定的剂量目标快速微调通量图。在临床和公共前列腺癌数据集上的实验结果表明，与当前可用的端到端VMAT计划器相比，该方法在计划效率、灵活性和机器可交付性方面均有提升。

</details>


### [7] [3D Primitives are a Spatial Language for VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12586)
*Junze Liu,Kun Qian,Florian Dubost,Kai Zhong,Arvind Srinivasan,Nan Chen,Anping Wang,Sam Zhang,Alejandro Mottini,Qingjun Cui,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型(VLM)在通过代码重建3D场景时表现良好，但在简单空间问答上却失败。为此提出两个方法：Code-CoT（基于代码的空间推理链式思维）和S$^{3}$-FT（自监督空间微调），利用3D几何基元（立方体、球体、圆柱体等）作为中间表征来提升VLM的空间理解能力。实验表明，S$^{3}$-FT无需人工标注即可显著提升模型在多个空间推理基准上的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型存在一个悖论：能够生成可执行代码正确重建3D场景，却在同一图像的简单空间问题上失败。作者旨在利用3D几何基元代码作为中间表征，弥合VLM在代码生成与空间推理之间的鸿沟。

Method: (1) 构建SpatialBabel基准，评估14个VLM在6种场景代码语言上的3D基元重建能力；(2) 提出Code-CoT（代码链式思维），通过路由空间推理经过基元代码生成来提升推理能力；(3) 提出S$^{3}$-FT（自监督空间微调），通过解析模型自身的Three.js基元重建结果生成结构化标注，无需人工标注和教师模型即可蒸馏空间知识。

Result: (1) 同一模型在6种场景代码语言上的检测F1可相差高达5.7倍；(2) Code-CoT在SpatialBabel-QA评分上最高提升+6.4%，在真实照片CV-Bench-3D上提升+5.0%；(3) S$^{3}$-FT在SpatialBabel-Primitive-QA上提升+4.6%~+8.6%，在CV-Bench-2D上提升+9.7%，在HallusionBench上提升+17%，且跨模型族迁移有效。

Conclusion: 代码中的几何基元既是诊断工具，也是VLM可迁移的空间词汇。通过自监督方式将基元空间知识蒸馏到通用视觉推理中，能够有效提升模型的空间理解能力，且无需人工标注。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) exhibit a striking paradox: they can generate executable code that reconstructs a 3D scene from geometric primitives with correct object counts, classes, and approximate positions, yet the same models fail at simpler spatial questions on the same image. We show that 3D geometric primitives (cubes, spheres, cylinders, expressed in executable code) serve as a powerful intermediate representation for spatial understanding, and exploit this through three contributions. First, we introduce \textbf{\textsc{SpatialBabel}}, a benchmark evaluating fourteen VLMs on primitive-based 3D scene reconstruction across six \emph{scene-code languages} (programming languages and declarative formats for 3D primitive scenes), revealing that a single model's object-detection F1 can vary by up to $5.7\times$ across languages. Second, we propose \textbf{Code-CoT} (Code Chain-of-Thought), a training-free inference strategy that routes spatial reasoning through primitive-based code generation. Code-CoT lifts the SpatialBabel-QA-Score by up to $+6.4$\% on primitive scenes and real-photo CV-Bench-3D accuracy by $+5.0$\% for VLMs with strong coding capabilities. Third, we propose \textbf{S$^{3}$-FT} (Self-Supervised Spatial Fine-Tuning), which self-supervisedly distills primitive spatial knowledge into general visual reasoning by parsing the model's own Three.js primitive-reconstructions into structured annotations and fine-tuning on the result, with \emph{no human labels and no teacher model}. Training on primitive images alone, S$^3$-FT improves Qwen3-VL-8B by $+4.6$ to $+8.6$\% on SpatialBabel-Primitive-QA, $+9.7$\% on CV-Bench-2D, and $+17$\% on HallusionBench; the recipe transfers across model families. These results establish geometric primitives in code as both a diagnostic and a transferable spatial vocabulary for VLMs. We will release all artifacts upon publication.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）展现出一个显著的悖论：它们能够生成可执行代码，从几何基元重建3D场景，且物体计数、类别和近似位置均正确，然而同一模型在相同图像上的简单空间问题上却会失败。我们表明，3D几何基元（立方体、球体、圆柱体，以可执行代码形式表达）可作为空间理解的有力中间表征，并通过三项贡献加以利用。首先，我们引入\textbf{\textsc{SpatialBabel}}基准，评估十四种VLM在六种\textit{场景代码语言}（用于3D基元场景的编程语言和声明式格式）上的基元式3D场景重建能力，揭示同一模型的物体检测F1值在不同语言间可相差高达5.7倍。其次，我们提出\textbf{Code-CoT}（代码链式思维），一种无需训练的推理策略，通过将空间推理路由至基元代码生成。Code-CoT在基元场景上使SpatialBabel-QA评分最高提升+6.4%，在真实照片CV-Bench-3D上使具备强编码能力的VLM准确率提升+5.0%。第三，我们提出\textbf{S$^{3}$-FT}（自监督空间微调），该方法通过解析模型自身的Three.js基元重建结果生成结构化标注，并在此结果上微调，以自监督方式将基元空间知识蒸馏到通用视觉推理中，\textit{无需人工标注，也无需教师模型}。仅基于基元图像训练，S$^{3}$-FT使Qwen3-VL-8B在SpatialBabel-Primitive-QA上提升+4.6%至+8.6%，在CV-Bench-2D上提升+9.7%，在HallusionBench上提升+17%；该方案可跨模型族迁移。这些结果表明，代码中的几何基元既是VLM的诊断工具，也是可迁移的空间词汇。我们将在论文发表后公开所有成果。

</details>


### [8] [TrackCraft3R: Repurposing Video Diffusion Transformers for Dense 3D Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12587)
*Jisu Nam,Jahyeok Koo,Soowon Son,Jaewoo Jung,Honggyu An,Junhwa Hur,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackCraft3R 是一种将预训练视频扩散Transformer（视频DiT）重新用作前馈式稠密3D跟踪器的方法，通过双潜变量表示和时序RoPE对齐，将视频DiT的逐帧生成范式转换为参考帧锚定的跟踪范式，在稀疏和稠密3D跟踪基准上达到SOTA，同时速度提升1.3倍、显存减少4.6倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的3D跟踪方法要么在合成数据上从头训练迭代范式，要么微调从静态多视图图像学习的3D重建模型，两者都缺乏真实世界的运动先验。预训练视频DiT拥有互联网规模视频的丰富时空先验，但其逐帧生成范式与参考帧锚定的稠密3D跟踪存在根本性不匹配，因此需要一种方法将视频DiT改造为适合稠密3D跟踪的形式。

Method: 提出TrackCraft3R，核心设计包括：(i) 双潜变量表示——使用逐帧几何潜变量和参考帧锚定的轨迹潜变量作为稠密查询；(ii) 时序RoPE对齐——为每个轨迹潜变量指定目标时间戳。两者结合通过LoRA微调将视频DiT的逐帧生成范式转换为参考帧锚定的跟踪公式。

Result: 在标准稀疏和稠密3D跟踪基准上达到最先进性能；相比最强先前方法速度提升1.3倍，峰值显存减少4.6倍；对大运动和长视频具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TrackCraft3R成功将视频DiT重新用于前馈稠密3D跟踪，证明了利用大规模预训练视频模型的运动先验可以有效提升3D跟踪表现，同时保持高效的计算资源消耗。

Original Abstract: Dense 3D tracking from monocular video is fundamental to dynamic scene understanding. While recent 3D foundation models provide reliable per-frame geometry, recovering object motion in this geometry remains challenging and benefits from strong motion priors learned from real-world videos. Existing 3D trackers either follow iterative paradigms trained from scratch on synthetic data or fine-tune 3D reconstruction models learned from static multi-view images, both lacking real-world motion priors. Pre-trained video diffusion transformers (video DiTs) offer rich spatio-temporal priors from internet-scale videos, making them a promising foundation for 3D tracking. However, their frame-anchored formulation, which generates each frame's content, is fundamentally mismatched with reference-anchored dense 3D tracking, which must follow the same physical points from a reference frame across time. We present TrackCraft3R, the first method to repurpose a video DiT as a feed-forward dense 3D tracker. Given a monocular video and its frame-anchored reconstruction pointmap, TrackCraft3R predicts a reference-anchored tracking pointmap that follows every pixel of the first frame across time in a single forward pass, along with its visibility. We achieve this through two designs: (i) a dual-latent representation that uses per-frame geometry latents and reference-anchored track latents as dense queries, and (ii) temporal RoPE alignment, which specifies the target timestamp of each track latent. Together, these designs convert the per-frame generative paradigm of video DiTs into a reference-anchored tracking formulation with LoRA fine-tuning. TrackCraft3R achieves state-of-the-art performance on standard sparse and dense 3D tracking benchmarks, while running 1.3x faster and using 4.6x less peak memory than the strongest prior method. We further demonstrate robustness to large motions and long videos.

中文摘要翻译: 从单目视频进行稠密3D跟踪是动态场景理解的基础。虽然最近的3D基础模型提供了可靠的逐帧几何信息，但在此几何中恢复物体运动仍然具有挑战性，并且受益于从真实世界视频中学习到的强运动先验。现有的3D跟踪器要么遵循在合成数据上从头训练的迭代范式，要么微调从静态多视图图像中学习的3D重建模型，两者都缺乏真实世界的运动先验。预训练的视频扩散Transformer（视频DiT）从互联网规模视频中提供了丰富的时空先验，使其成为3D跟踪的有前景的基础。然而，它们的帧锚定公式化——逐帧生成内容——与参考帧锚定的稠密3D跟踪存在根本性不匹配，后者必须随时间跟踪来自参考帧的相同物理点。我们提出TrackCraft3R，这是第一种将视频DiT重新用作前馈式稠密3D跟踪器的方法。给定单目视频及其帧锚定的重建点图，TrackCraft3R预测一个参考帧锚定的跟踪点图，该点图在单次前向传播中跟踪第一帧的每个像素随时间的变化，同时预测其可见性。我们通过两种设计实现这一目标：(i) 双潜变量表示，使用逐帧几何潜变量和参考帧锚定的轨迹潜变量作为稠密查询；(ii) 时序RoPE对齐，为每个轨迹潜变量指定目标时间戳。这两项设计共同通过LoRA微调将视频DiT的逐帧生成范式转换为参考帧锚定的跟踪公式。TrackCraft3R在标准稀疏和稠密3D跟踪基准上达到了最先进性能，同时相比最强先前方法速度提升1.3倍，峰值显存减少4.6倍。我们进一步证明了其对大运动和长视频的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [9] [A Data Efficiency Study of Synthetic Fog for Object Detection Using the Clear2Fog Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12608)
*Mohamed Ahmed Mohamed,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Clear2Fog (C2F)端到端物理驱动管道，在晴好天气数据集上模拟雾天，实现相机与LiDAR传感器级一致性。通过单目深度估计和新型大气光估计方法克服结构伪影和色偏。实验表明，混合密度雾数据集在75%规模下优于固定密度100%规模；十倍微调学习率克服负迁移，mAP提升1.67。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 恶劣天气下目标检测对自动驾驶安全至关重要，但真实世界有标注雾天数据稀缺，成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出C2F端到端物理驱动管道，利用单目深度估计和新型大气光估计方法，在晴好天气数据集上模拟物理真实的雾天数据，并保持相机与LiDAR传感器的一致性。

Result: 人类感知研究显示C2F生成图像以92.95%的偏好率优于现有方法；混合密度雾数据集在75%规模下训练优于固定密度100%规模；十倍默认微调学习率克服负迁移，mAP提升1.67。

Conclusion: C2F为提升自动驾驶系统在恶劣天气下的可靠性提供了可扩展框架，展示了多样化合成数据集在高效模型训练中的潜力。

Original Abstract: Object detection in adverse weather is critical for the safety of autonomous vehicles; however, the scarcity of labelled, real-world foggy data remains a significant bottleneck. In this paper, we propose Clear2Fog (C2F), an end-to-end, physics-based pipeline that simulates fog on clear-weather datasets while ensuring sensor-level consistency across camera and LiDAR. By using monocular depth estimation and a novel atmospheric light estimation method, C2F overcomes structural artifacts and chromatic biases common in existing techniques. A human perceptual study confirms C2F's physical realism, with the generated images being preferred 92.95% of the time over an established method. Utilising a training set of 270,000 images from the Waymo Open Dataset, we conduct an extensive data efficiency study to investigate how environmental diversity influences model robustness. Our findings reveal that models trained on mixed-density fog datasets at 75% scale outperform those trained on fixed-density datasets at 100% scale. Furthermore, we investigate the sim-to-real transfer by fine-tuning pre-trained models on real-world foggy data. We demonstrate that a tenfold increase over the default fine-tuning learning rate successfully overcomes negative transfer from synthetic biases, resulting in a 1.67 mAP improvement over real-only baselines. The C2F pipeline provides a scalable framework for enhancing the reliability of autonomous systems in adverse weather and demonstrates the potential of diverse synthetic datasets for efficient model training.

中文摘要翻译: 恶劣天气下的目标检测对于自动驾驶汽车的安全至关重要；然而，真实世界中有标注的雾天数据的稀缺性仍然是一个显著的瓶颈。在本文中，我们提出Clear2Fog (C2F)，一种端到端的、基于物理的管道，该管道在晴好天气数据集上模拟雾天，同时确保相机和LiDAR之间传感器级别的一致性。通过使用单目深度估计和一种新颖的大气光估计方法，C2F克服了现有技术中常见的结构伪影和色偏。一项人类感知研究证实了C2F的物理真实性，其生成的图像以92.95%的偏好率优于现有方法。利用来自Waymo开放数据集的270,000张图像作为训练集，我们开展了一项广泛的数据效率研究，以探究环境多样性如何影响模型鲁棒性。我们的发现表明，在混合密度雾数据集上以75%规模训练的模型，其性能优于在固定密度数据集上以100%规模训练的模型。此外，我们通过在实际雾天数据上微调预训练模型来研究从仿真到真实的迁移。我们证明，将默认微调学习率提高十倍，成功地克服了来自合成偏差的负迁移，相较于仅使用真实数据的基线，mAP提升了1.67。C2F管道为提升自动驾驶系统在恶劣天气下的可靠性提供了一个可扩展框架，并展示了多样化合成数据集在高效模型训练中的潜力。

</details>


### [10] [Visual Aesthetic Benchmark: Can Frontier Models Judge Beauty?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12684)
*Yichen Feng,Yuetai Li,Chunjiang Liu,Yuanyuan Chen,Fengqing Jiang,Yue Huang,Hang Hua,Zhengqing Yuan,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Xiangliang Zhang,Misha Sra,Zichen Chen,Radha Poovendran,Zhangchen Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态大模型在美学评估中的不足：标量评分与人类偏好对齐差，因此提出基于集合比较的视觉美学基准（VAB），发现最强模型仅26.5%任务完全正确，远低于人类的68.9%，为追踪和缩小模型与人类美学判断差距提供了首个专家标注的测试平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有美学评估方法将判断简化为单图像的标量评分，但研究发现此类评分与人类直接比较偏好对齐不良，排名结果与人类直接排序的标注者间一致性较低。因此需要一种更忠实反映人类审美判断的评估范式。

Method: 构建视觉美学基准（VAB），将美学评估转化为在候选图像集合中进行比较选择的任务。VAB包含400个任务、1,195张图像，涵盖美术、摄影和插画三个领域，每项任务的标签由10位独立专家评委的共识得出。评估了20个前沿MLLM和6个专用视觉质量奖励模型。

Result: 最强系统在候选顺序的三次随机排列下，仅26.5%的任务能同时正确识别最佳和最差图像，远低于人类专家的68.9%。在35B参数模型上微调2,000个专家样本，其准确率接近397B参数的开源模型，表明VAB中的比较信号具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: 当前多模态模型与人类专家审美判断之间存在明确且可量化的差距，VAB作为首个基于集合、专家标注的测试平台，可用于追踪和弥合这一差距。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are now routinely deployed for visual understanding, generation, and curation. A substantial fraction of these applications require an explicit aesthetic judgment. Most existing solutions reduce this judgment to predicting a scalar score for a single image. We first ask whether such scores faithfully capture comparative preference: in a controlled study with eight expert annotators, score-derived rankings align poorly with the same annotators' direct comparisons, while direct ranking yields substantially higher inter-annotator agreement on best- and worst-image labels. Motivated by this finding, we introduce the Visual Aesthetic Benchmark (VAB), which casts aesthetic evaluation as comparative selection over candidate sets with matched subject matter. VAB contains 400 tasks and 1,195 images across fine art, photography, and illustration, with labels derived from the consensus of 10 independent expert judges per task. Evaluating 20 frontier MLLMs and six dedicated visual-quality reward models, we find that the strongest system identifies both the best and the worst image correctly across three random permutations of the candidate order in only 26.5% of tasks, far below the 68.9% achieved by human experts. Fine-tuning a 35B-parameter model on 2,000 expert examples brings its accuracy close to that of a 397B-parameter open-weight model, suggesting that the comparative signal in VAB is transferable. Together, these results expose a clear and measurable gap between current multimodal models and expert aesthetic judgment, and VAB provides the first set-based, expert-grounded testbed on which that gap can be tracked and closed.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）现已常规部署于视觉理解、生成和策展任务中。这些应用中有相当一部分需要明确的美学判断。大多数现有解决方案将这种判断简化为预测单个图像的标量分数。我们首先提出疑问：此类分数是否忠实地捕捉了比较偏好？在一项包含八位专家标注者的对照研究中，由分数推导出的排名与同一批标注者的直接比较结果对齐较差，而直接排名在最佳和最差图像标签上产生了显著更高的标注者间一致性。受此发现启发，我们引入了视觉美学基准（VAB），该基准将美学评估转化为在主题匹配的候选集合中进行比较选择的任务。VAB包含400个任务和1,195张图像，涵盖美术、摄影和插画三个领域，每项任务的标签由10位独立专家评委的共识得出。在评估了20个前沿MLLM和6个专用视觉质量奖励模型后，我们发现，在候选顺序的三次随机排列中，最强系统同时正确识别最佳和最差图像的比率仅为26.5%，远低于人类专家达成的68.9%。在35B参数模型上微调2,000个专家样本，使其准确率接近397B参数的开源权重模型，这表明VAB中的比较信号具有可迁移性。综上，这些结果暴露了当前多模态模型与专家审美判断之间清晰且可量化的差距，而VAB提供了首个基于集合、以专家标注为基础的测试平台，可在其上追踪和弥合这一差距。

</details>


### [11] [MMCL-Bench: Multimodal Context Learning from Visual Rules, Procedures, and Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12703)
*Yifan Chen,Fei Yin,Qingyan Bai,Zicheng Lin,Yujiu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MMCL-Bench是一个多模态上下文学习基准，包含102个任务，用于评估模型从图像/视频等视觉上下文中学习规则和模式的能力。实验发现当前最强模型在严格评估下也无法解决三分之一的任务，表明多模态上下文学习是当前模型的关键能力瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准主要关注文本上下文学习或标准多模态问答，缺乏专门评估模型从视觉或混合模态教学上下文中学习局部规则、流程和实证模式，并将其应用于新视觉实例的能力的基准。

Method: 构建了MMCL-Bench基准，包含102个任务，涵盖三类：规则系统应用、程序性任务执行、经验发现与归纳。使用严格的评分标准评估前沿多模态模型，并进行诊断性消融实验和错误分析。

Result: 当前最强模型在严格评估下仅解决不到三分之一的任务。错误分析表明失败发生在从上下文锚定、视觉证据提取、上下文推理到响应构建的整个流水线中。

Conclusion: 多模态上下文学习是当前多模态模型中一个重要的未解决能力瓶颈，MMCL-Bench揭示了现有系统在这一能力上的显著不足。

Original Abstract: We introduce MMCL-Bench, a benchmark for multimodal context learning: learning task-local rules, procedures, and empirical patterns from visual or mixed-modality teaching context and applying them to new visual instances. Unlike text-only context learning or standard multimodal question answering, this setting requires models to recover and localize relevant evidence from images, screenshots, manuals, videos, and frame sequences before they can reason over the learned context. MMCL-Bench contains 102 tasks spanning three categories: rule system application, procedural task execution, and empirical discovery and induction. We evaluate frontier multimodal models with strict rubric-based scoring and find that current systems remain far from robust multimodal context learning, with even the strongest model solving fewer than one-third of tasks under strict evaluation. Diagnostic ablations and error analysis show that failures arise throughout the context-to-answer pipeline, including context anchoring, visual evidence extraction, context reasoning, and response construction. MMCL-Bench thus highlights multimodal context learning as an important unsolved capability bottleneck for current multimodal models.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了MMCL-Bench，一个用于多模态上下文学习的基准：从视觉或混合模态的教学上下文中学习任务局部规则、流程和实证模式，并将其应用于新的视觉实例。与纯文本上下文学习或标准多模态问答不同，该设置要求模型在基于所学上下文进行推理之前，先从图像、截图、手册、视频和帧序列中恢复并定位相关证据。MMCL-Bench包含102个任务，涵盖三个类别：规则系统应用、程序性任务执行和经验发现与归纳。我们使用严格的基于评分标准的评估方法评估了前沿多模态模型，发现当前系统在多模态上下文学习方面远未达到鲁棒水平，即使在严格评估下，最强模型也只能解决不到三分之一的任务。诊断性消融实验和错误分析表明，失败贯穿于从上下文到答案的整个流水线，包括上下文锚定、视觉证据提取、上下文推理和响应构建。因此，MMCL-Bench凸显了多模态上下文学习作为当前多模态模型一个重要且未解决的能力瓶颈。

</details>


### [12] [Is Video Anomaly Detection Misframed? Evidence from LLM-Based and Multi-Scene Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12725)
*Furkan Mumcu,Michael J. Jones,Anoop Cherian,Yasin Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文批判当前视频异常检测（VAD）研究过度追求多场景通用性，依赖弱监督和MLLM预训练表征，导致模型关注语义类别而非场景特定模式，削弱了空间定位能力并引入语义偏差。作者通过可视化和实证分析，呼吁回归单场景、空间感知且可解释的VAD范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前VAD研究主流方向强调多场景泛化，依赖视频级弱监督和大语言模型预训练表征，但这偏离了真实VAD场景中异常行为具有场景特定性和上下文依赖性的核心需求，导致模型退化为动作识别，缺乏空间定位能力和可解释性。本文旨在揭示这些局限性的实际后果，并呼吁回归更符合实际需求的VAD范式。

Method: 本文通过目标性视觉分析和实证评估的方法，对当前主流VAD方法的多场景通用性假设、弱监督范式及MLLM预训练表征进行系统性的批判性分析，展示其在单场景、空间定位和可解释性方面的不足。

Result: 实证分析表明，当前主流VAD方法由于依赖视频级弱监督和MLLM预训练表征，倾向于响应熟悉的语义异常类别而非环境特有的正常模式偏差，导致空间定位能力下降、语义偏差引入，异常检测退化为动作识别。

Conclusion: VAD领域的有意义进展需要重新关注单场景、空间感知且可解释的范式，以捕捉个体环境中正常行为的细微结构。

Original Abstract: Recent video anomaly detection research has expanded rapidly with an emphasis on general models of normality intended to work across many different scenes. While this focus has led to improvements in scalability and multi-scene generalization, it has also shifted the field away from modeling the scene-specific and context-dependent nature of normal behavior. Contemporary approaches frequently rely on video-level weak supervision and opaque pretrained representations from multi-modal large language models (MLLMs), which encourage models to respond to familiar semantic anomaly categories rather than to deviations from the normal patterns of a particular environment. This trend suppresses spatial localization, introduces semantic bias, and reduces anomaly detection to a form of action recognition. In this paper, we examine whether these prevailing formulations align with the core requirements of real-world VAD, which is typically performed within a single scene where normality is determined by local geometry, semantics, and activity patterns. Through targeted visual analyses and empirical evaluations, we demonstrate the practical consequences of these limitations and show that meaningful progress in VAD requires renewed focus on single-scene, spatially-aware, and explainable formulations that capture the nuanced structure of normality within individual environments.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，视频异常检测研究迅速发展，重点集中在旨在跨多种不同场景工作的通用正常行为模型。虽然这一关注点带来了可扩展性和多场景泛化方面的改进，但也使该领域偏离了对正常行为的场景特异性和上下文依赖性建模。当代方法通常依赖视频级弱监督以及来自多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的非透明预训练表征，这促使模型响应熟悉的语义异常类别，而非对特定环境中正常模式的偏离。这种趋势抑制了空间定位，引入了语义偏差，并将异常检测简化为一种动作识别形式。在本文中，我们考察了这些主流范式是否与真实世界VAD的核心需求相一致——真实VAD通常在单一场景中进行，其中正常性由局部几何结构、语义和活动模式决定。通过目标性视觉分析和实证评估，我们展示了这些局限性的实际后果，并表明VAD领域的有意义进展需要重新关注单场景、空间感知且可解释的范式，以捕捉个体环境中正常行为的细微结构。

</details>


### [13] [Just Ask for a Table: A Thirty-Token User Prompt Defeats Sponsored Recommendations in Twelve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12772)
*Andreas Maier,Jeta Sopa,Gozde Gul Sahin,Paula Perez-Toro,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文复现了Wu等人(2026)的研究，发现绝大多数大型语言模型在系统提示中包含软赞助提示时会推荐更贵的赞助航班。复现结果表明，原论文的核心主张成立，但论文式的流水线描述不足以精确复现；此外，一个30词的用户提示可将赞助推荐率从约50%降至接近0%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 验证Wu等人(2026)关于LLM在系统提示中包含软赞助提示时会推荐赞助商更贵产品的核心主张是否可泛化，并评估复现过程中的实现差异对结果的影响。

Method: 在10个开源聊天模型及2个OpenAI模型（gpt-3.5-turbo、gpt-4o）上复现原版评估，使用原论文的评判模型gpt-4o进行标注，并额外使用开源模型gpt-oss-120b和较小专有模型gpt-4o-mini作为消融实验的评判模型。

Result: 1) 原论文的流水线描述不足以精确复现，发现了三个静默实现错误导致报告率偏离数十个百分点；2) 核心主张可泛化，gpt-3.5-turbo的逻辑回归截距α=0.81（原为0.86），且200/200次试验中模型向财务困境用户推广高利贷；3) 30词用户提示（要求提供中立对比表）将开源模型的赞助推荐率从46.9%降至1.0%，OpenAI模型从53.0%降至0%。

Conclusion: AI素养和价格比较门户网站可能是市场层面的缓解措施，但对于有害产品类别，上述防护措施无效。

Original Abstract: Wu et al. (2026) showed that most frontier large language models (LLMs) recommend a sponsored, roughly twice-as-expensive flight when their system prompt contains a soft sponsorship cue. We reproduce their evaluation on ten open-weight chat models plus the two of their twenty-three models that are still reachable today (gpt-3.5-turbo, gpt-4o). All reported rates in this paper are produced under the same judge the original paper used (gpt-4o); we additionally store every label under an open-weight (gpt-oss-120b) and a smaller proprietary (gpt-4o-mini) judge for an ablation. Three findings emerge. First, a prose description of an LLM evaluation pipeline is not, on its own, sufficient for accurate reproduction: we surfaced three silent implementation failures that each shifted a reported rate by tens of percentage points. Second, the central claims do generalise - the gpt-3.5-turbo logistic-regression intercept of alpha = 0.81 is within four points of the original alpha = 0.86, and 200 of 200 trials on gpt-3.5-turbo and gpt-4o promote a payday lender to a financially distressed user. Third, a thirty-token user prompt that asks the assistant for a neutral comparison table first cuts sponsored recommendation from 46.9% to 1.0% averaged across our ten open-source models, and from 53.0% to 0% averaged across the two OpenAI models. AI literacy and price-comparison portals are likely market-level mitigations; the harmful-product cell is bounded by neither. Raw data, labels and analysis scripts are at https://github.com/akmaier/Paper-LLM-Ads .

中文摘要翻译: Wu等人(2026)的研究表明，当系统提示中包含软赞助提示时，大多数前沿大型语言模型（LLM）会推荐一个赞助商提供的、价格约高一倍的航班。我们在10个开源聊天模型以及原论文23个模型中目前仍可访问的两个模型（gpt-3.5-turbo、gpt-4o）上复现了他们的评估。本文报告的所有比率均使用原论文所用的相同评判模型（gpt-4o）生成；此外，我们还额外使用一个开源模型（gpt-oss-120b）和一个较小的专有模型（gpt-4o-mini）作为评判模型进行消融实验，并存储了每个标签。研究发现了三个结果。第一，仅凭LLM评估流程的散文式描述不足以实现精确复现：我们发现了三个静默实现错误，每个错误都导致报告率偏移数十个百分点。第二，核心主张确实具有泛化能力——gpt-3.5-turbo的逻辑回归截距α=0.81，与原论文的α=0.86相差在4个百分点以内；并且在200次试验中，gpt-3.5-turbo和gpt-4o均向财务困境用户推广高利贷产品。第三，一个要求助手提供中立对比表的30词用户提示，将我们10个开源模型的赞助推荐率从46.9%降至1.0%，将两个OpenAI模型的赞助推荐率从53.0%降至0%。AI素养和价格比较门户网站可能是市场层面的缓解措施；但有害产品类别不受上述措施约束。原始数据、标注和分析脚本见https://github.com/akmaier/Paper-LLM-Ads。

</details>


### [14] [WildPose: A Unified Framework for Robust Pose Estimation in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12774)
*Jianhao Zheng,Liyuan Zhu,Zihan Zhu,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WildPose 提出一种统一的单目相机姿态估计框架，通过融合前馈模型的感知前端与可微束调整的端到端优化，在动态、静态和低自我运动场景下均达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉SLAM和SfM方法假设静态场景，在动态环境中表现不佳。现有的动态感知方法存在局限性：基于语义的方法鲁棒性差、逐序列优化方法在短序列上效果差、学习模型在纯静态场景中性能下降，缺乏统一的鲁棒解决方案。

Method: 核心思路是连接前馈模型的丰富感知前端与可微束调整（BA）的端到端优化。具体实现：1）基于冻结的预训练MASt3R特征骨干构建3D感知更新算子；2）利用同一骨干的多层级3D感知特征设计高容量运动掩码检测器，实现动态区域的有效处理。

Result: 在动态（Wild-SLAM, Bonn）、静态（TUM, 7-Scenes）和低自我运动（Sintel）基准数据集上，WildPose均一致性地优于先前方法。

Conclusion: WildPose是一个统一的单目姿态估计框架，在动态环境中鲁棒，同时在静态和低自我运动数据集上保持最优性能，有效弥合了前馈感知与端到端优化之间的鸿沟。

Original Abstract: Estimating camera pose in dynamic environments is a critical challenge, as most visual SLAM and SfM methods assume static scenes. While recent dynamic-aware methods exist, they are often not unified: semantic-based approaches are brittle, per-sequence optimization methods fail on short sequences, and other learned models may degrade on static-only scenes. We present WildPose, a unified monocular pose estimation framework that is robust in dynamic environments while maintaining state-of-the-art performance on static and low-ego-motion datasets. Our key insight is to connect two powerful paradigms in modern 3D vision: the rich perceptual frontend of feedforward models and the end-to-end optimization of differentiable bundle adjustment (BA). We achieve this with a 3D-aware update operator built on a frozen, pre-trained MASt3R feature backbone, together with a high-capacity motion mask detector that uses multi-level 3D-aware features from the same backbone. Extensive experiments show WildPose consistently outperforms prior methods across dynamic (Wild-SLAM, Bonn), static (TUM, 7-Scenes), and low-ego-motion (Sintel) benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 在动态环境中估计相机姿态是一个关键挑战，因为大多数视觉SLAM和SfM方法都假设场景是静态的。尽管近年来存在一些动态感知方法，但它们往往不统一：基于语义的方法鲁棒性差，逐序列优化方法在短序列上效果不佳，而其他学习模型在纯静态场景中性能可能下降。我们提出了WildPose，一个统一的单目姿态估计框架，它在动态环境中具有鲁棒性，同时在静态和低自我运动数据集上保持最先进的性能。我们的关键见解是连接现代3D视觉中两个强大的范式：前馈模型的丰富感知前端与可微束调整（BA）的端到端优化。我们通过构建在冻结的、预训练的MASt3R特征骨干上的3D感知更新算子，以及使用来自同一骨干的多层级3D感知特征的高容量运动掩码检测器来实现这一目标。大量实验表明，WildPose在动态（Wild-SLAM, Bonn）、静态（TUM, 7-Scenes）和低自我运动（Sintel）基准数据集上均一致地优于先前方法。

</details>


### [15] [FRAME: Forensic Routing and Adaptive Multi-path Evidence Fusion for Image Manipulation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12826)
*Kaixiang Zhao,Tianrun Yu,Aoxu Zhang,Junhao Su,Porter Jenkins,Amanda Hughes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FRAME是一种通过多路径分析空间、自适应路由和证据融合来增强图像篡改检测鲁棒性与泛化能力的取证框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的图像篡改检测方法（无论是手工特征还是深度学习）存在鲁棒性不足、证据碎片化、跨操作类型和图像条件泛化能力弱等问题，需一种更灵活、鲁棒的取证推理方法。

Method: FRAME将多种取证算法组织成多路径分析空间，对每张输入图像自适应选择信息量最大的取证路径，并融合互补证据以提升检测和定位性能，同时保留可解释的取证线索。

Result: 实验结果表明FRAME在多种篡改场景下均具有有效性，验证了其超越单一方法和固定融合策略的优势。

Conclusion: FRAME通过自适应多路径证据融合，提供了一种比单一方法更鲁棒、更灵活的图像取证推理方案，同时保持了多源证据的可解释性。

Original Abstract: The proliferation of sophisticated image editing tools and generative artificial intelligence models has made verifying the authenticity of digital images increasingly challenging, with important implications for journalism, forensic analysis, and public trust. Although numerous forensic algorithms, ranging from handcrafted methods to deep learning-based detectors, have been developed for manipulation detection, individual methods often suffer from limited robustness, fragmented evidence, or weak generalization across manipulation types and image conditions. To address these limitations, we present \textbf{FRAME}, a method for \textbf{F}orensic \textbf{R}outing and \textbf{A}daptive \textbf{M}ulti-path \textbf{E}vidence fusion for image manipulation detection. FRAME organizes diverse forensic algorithms into a multi-path analysis space, adaptively selects informative forensic paths for each input image, and fuses complementary evidence to improve detection and localization performance. By moving beyond single-method analysis and fixed fusion strategies, FRAME provides a more robust and flexible approach to image forensic reasoning while preserving interpretable forensic cues from multiple evidence sources. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of FRAME across diverse manipulation scenarios. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/kzhao5/FRAME}{https://github.com/kzhao5/FRAME}.

中文摘要翻译: 随着复杂图像编辑工具和生成式人工智能模型的普及，验证数字图像真实性的挑战日益加剧，这对新闻报道、取证分析和公众信任产生了重要影响。尽管已有从手工特征到基于深度学习的检测器等多种取证算法用于篡改检测，但单一方法往往存在鲁棒性有限、证据碎片化、以及跨操作类型和图像条件泛化能力弱等问题。为应对这些局限，我们提出了\textbf{FRAME}——一种用于图像篡改检测的\textbf{取}证\textbf{路}由与\textbf{自}适应\textbf{多}路径\textbf{证}据融合方法。FRAME将多种取证算法组织成一个多路径分析空间，针对每张输入图像自适应选择信息量丰富的取证路径，并融合互补证据以提升检测与定位性能。通过超越单一方法分析和固定融合策略，FRAME提供了一种更鲁棒、更灵活的图像取证推理方法，同时保留了来自多个证据源的可解释取证线索。实验结果证明了FRAME在多种篡改场景下的有效性。代码已开源在 \href{https://github.com/kzhao5/FRAME}{https://github.com/kzhao5/FRAME}。

</details>


### [16] [AssemblyBench: Physics-Aware Assembly of Complex Industrial Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12845)
*Danrui Li,Jiahao Zhang,Bernhard Egger,Moitreya Chatterjee,Suhas Lohit,Tim K. Marks,Anoop Cherian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出工业装配数据集AssemblyBench和基于Transformer的模型AssemblyDyno，用于从多模态说明书和3D零件形状联合预测装配顺序与轨迹，在装配姿态估计和轨迹可行性上超越先前方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据集忽略了工业装配中的形状复杂性和装配轨迹，仅关注简化场景。

Method: 提出AssemblyBench合成数据集（含2,789个工业物体、多模态说明书、3D零件模型及装配轨迹）；基于Transformer的AssemblyDyno模型，利用说明书和每个零件的3D形状联合预测装配顺序和零件装配轨迹。

Result: AssemblyDyno在装配姿态估计和轨迹可行性（通过基于物理的仿真评估）上均优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 多模态说明书与3D形状联合建模能有效提升工业装配任务的性能，物理仿真是评估轨迹可行性的有效手段。

Original Abstract: Assembling objects from parts requires understanding multimodal instructions, linking them to 3D components, and predicting physically plausible 6-DoF motions for each assembly step. Existing datasets focus on simplified scenarios, overlooking shape complexities and assembly trajectories in industrial assemblies. We introduce AssemblyBench, a synthetic dataset of 2,789 industrial objects with multimodal instruction manuals, corresponding 3D part models, and part assembly trajectories. We also propose a transformer-based model, AssemblyDyno, which uses the instructional manual and the 3D shape of each part to jointly predict assembly order and part assembly trajectories. AssemblyDyno outperforms prior works in both assembly pose estimation and trajectory feasibility, where the latter is evaluated by our physics-based simulations.

中文摘要翻译: 从零件组装物体需要理解多模态指令、将其与3D组件关联，并预测每个组装步骤中物理上可行的六自由度运动。现有数据集聚焦于简化场景，忽视了工业组装中的形状复杂性和组装轨迹。我们引入了AssemblyBench，一个包含2,789个工业物体的合成数据集，附带多模态指令手册、对应的3D零件模型和零件组装轨迹。我们还提出了一个基于Transformer的模型AssemblyDyno，它利用指令手册和每个零件的3D形状来联合预测组装顺序和零件组装轨迹。AssemblyDyno在装配姿态估计和轨迹可行性方面均优于先前的工作，后者通过我们基于物理的仿真进行评估。

</details>


### [17] [Adaptive Conformal Prediction for Reliable and Explainable Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12917)
*One Octadion,Novanto Yudistira,Lailil Muflikhah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应Lambda准则改进RAPS方法，最小化不同预测集大小分层上的最坏情况覆盖违规，在医学影像上实现更可靠的置信预测，同时保持效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准CP方法（如RAPS）针对平均效率优化，在困难样本上可能出现严重失效，导致模型在模糊诊断场景中存在安全风险。

Method: 提出Adaptive Lambda Criterion for RAPS，通过最小化预测集大小分层上的最坏情况覆盖违规（worst-case coverage violation）来优化阈值λ，而非仅优化平均效率。

Result: 在OrganAMNIST上，全局覆盖率达95.72%，平均集大小1.09，所有分层至少90%覆盖；在PathMNIST上交叉验证泛化性。Grad-CAM分析（rho=-0.30, p<1e-22）表明多标签预测对应解剖模糊区域的集中注意力。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持效率的同时提高了可靠性，适用于安全关键的医学AI应用。

Original Abstract: Deep learning models for medical imaging often exhibit overconfidence, creating safety risks in ambiguous diagnostic scenarios. While Conformal Prediction (CP) provides distribution-free statistical guarantees, standard methods such as Regularized Adaptive Prediction Sets (RAPS) optimize for average efficiency and can mask severe failures on difficult inputs.
  We propose an Adaptive Lambda Criterion for RAPS that minimizes the worst-case coverage violation across prediction set size strata. On OrganAMNIST (58,850 abdominal CT images, 11 classes), standard size-optimized RAPS converges to near-deterministic behavior with stratified undercoverage on uncertain samples, while our method achieves 95.72 percent global coverage with average set size 1.09 and at least 90 percent coverage across all strata. Cross-domain validation on PathMNIST (107,180 pathology images, 9 classes) confirms generalizability.
  Quantitative Grad-CAM analysis (rho = -0.30, p < 1e-22) shows that multi-label predictions correspond to focused attention on anatomically ambiguous regions. These results demonstrate that the proposed method improves reliability while maintaining efficiency, making it suitable for safety-critical medical AI applications.

中文摘要翻译: 医学影像的深度学习模型常常表现出过度自信，这在模糊诊断场景中会带来安全风险。虽然共形预测（CP）提供了无分布统计保证，但标准方法（如正则化自适应预测集RAPS）针对平均效率进行优化，可能在困难输入上掩盖严重失效。我们提出一种针对RAPS的自适应Lambda准则，该准则最小化预测集大小分层上的最坏情况覆盖违规。在OrganAMNIST（58,850张腹部CT图像，11个类别）上，标准尺寸优化的RAPS收敛到近乎确定性的行为，在不确定样本上出现分层欠覆盖，而我们的方法实现了95.72%的全局覆盖率，平均集大小为1.09，且所有分层的覆盖率至少达到90%。在PathMNIST（107,180张病理图像，9个类别）上的跨域验证确认了泛化性。定量Grad-CAM分析（rho=-0.30, p<1e-22）表明，多标签预测对应于解剖模糊区域的集中注意力。这些结果表明，所提方法在保持效率的同时提高了可靠性，使其适用于安全关键的医学AI应用。

</details>


### [18] [GuardMarkGS: Unified Ownership Tracing and Edit Deterrence for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12919)
*Utae Jeong,Jaewan Choi,Junseok Lee,Jongheon Jeong,Sang Ho Yoon,ByoungSoo Koh,Sangpil Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个统一保护框架，同时实现3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）资产的版权追踪和编辑防御，通过联合优化水印目标与对抗目标，在比特精度、编辑防御和渲染质量间取得良好平衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法仅解决单一问题：水印可追踪所有权但无法阻止恶意编辑，对抗防御能干扰编辑但无法提供所有权证据。需要统一的保护框架同时解决两方面版权风险。

Method: 提出统一保护框架，联合优化场景级水印目标（所有高斯体）与对抗编辑防御目标。对抗分支结合潜锚点分离、去噪轨迹偏移和交叉注意力偏移来干扰编辑轨迹；采用更新显著性驱动的高斯选择策略，对被掩码选中的高斯体施加更强的对抗更新，以平衡水印恢复、编辑防御和渲染保真度。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF 360和Instruct-NeRF2NeRF数据集上的实验表明，该方法在比特精度、编辑防御能力和渲染质量之间取得了有利的平衡。

Conclusion: 将所有权追踪和未经授权编辑防御整合到单一优化框架中，能更有效地实现3DGS资产的实际版权保护。

Original Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is becoming a practical representation for novel view synthesis, but its growing adoption, together with rapid advances in instruction-driven 3DGS editing, also exposes a dual copyright risk: once a 3DGS-based asset is released, it can be used without permission and manipulated through 3D editing. Existing protection methods address only one side of this problem. Watermarking can trace ownership after unauthorized use, but it cannot prevent malicious editing. Adversarial edit-deterrence methods can disrupt editing, but they do not provide evidence of ownership. To the best of our knowledge, we present the first unified protection framework for 3DGS that jointly optimizes ownership tracing and unauthorized editing deterrence. Our framework combines a scene-wide watermarking objective over all Gaussians with an adversarial objective for edit deterrence. The adversarial branch combines latent-anchor separation, denoising-trajectory diversion, and cross-attention diversion to divert the editing trajectory, while an update-saliency-motivated Gaussian selection strategy assigns stronger adversarial updates to mask-selected Gaussians, improving the balance among watermark recovery, edit deterrence, and rendering fidelity. Experiments on scenes from Mip-NeRF 360 and Instruct-NeRF2NeRF demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves a favorable balance among bit accuracy, edit deterrence, and rendering quality. These results suggest that practical copyright protection of 3DGS-based assets can be more effectively addressed by integrating ownership tracing and unauthorized editing deterrence into a single optimization framework.

中文摘要翻译: 3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）正成为新颖视角合成的一种实用表示方法，但其日益广泛的采用，连同指令驱动型3DGS编辑的快速进展，也暴露出双重版权风险：一旦基于3DGS的资产被发布，它可以在未经许可的情况下被使用，并通过3D编辑进行操作。现有保护方法仅解决这一问题的一个方面。水印技术可以在未经授权使用后追溯所有权，但无法防止恶意编辑。对抗性编辑防御方法可以干扰编辑，但无法提供所有权证据。据我们所知，我们提出了第一个针对3DGS的统一保护框架，该框架联合优化所有权追踪和未经授权编辑防御。我们的框架将覆盖所有高斯体的场景级水印目标与用于编辑防御的对抗目标相结合。对抗分支结合了潜锚点分离、去噪轨迹偏移和交叉注意力偏移来转移编辑轨迹，同时一种基于更新显著性的高斯选择策略对被掩码选中的高斯体施加更强的对抗更新，从而改善了水印恢复、编辑防御和渲染保真度之间的平衡。在Mip-NeRF 360和Instruct-NeRF2NeRF数据集上的实验表明，所提出的框架在比特精度、编辑防御和渲染质量之间取得了有利的平衡。这些结果表明，通过将所有权追踪和未经授权编辑防御整合到单个优化框架中，可以更有效地实现基于3DGS资产的实际版权保护。

</details>


### [19] [Anatomy-Slot: Unsupervised Anatomical Factorization for Homologous Bilateral Reasoning in Retinal Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12929)
*Yingzhe Ma,Xiao Yang,Yuguo Yin,Zheyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Anatomy-Slot，一种通过无监督解剖瓶颈和双向交叉注意力实现双眼结构对应对齐的方法，在视网膜诊断中显著优于 ViT-L 基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视网膜诊断本质上是双眼的——临床医生会比较双眼间的对称结构（如视盘不对称性），但现有深度学习模型大多基于单眼表征，未能利用双眼间的结构对应信息。

Method: 提出 Anatomy-Slot，通过将图像块 token 分解为 slots（无监督解剖瓶颈），并利用双向交叉注意力机制对齐双眼间的 slots，从而实现双眼结构对应。

Result: 在 ODIR-5K 数据集上（10 个随机种子），相较于匹配的 ViT-L 基线，AUC 提升 4.2%（95% 置信区间；Wilcoxon 符号秩检验，W=0，p=0.002）。此外，在 REFUGE 数据集上进行了定量视盘定位分析，并给出了交叉注意力定位分析结果。

Conclusion: 显式的双眼结构对应能够显著提升视网膜诊断性能，Anatomy-Slot 通过无监督方式实现该对应，具有强鲁棒性和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Retinal diagnosis is inherently bilateral: clinicians compare homologous structures across eyes (e.g., optic disc asymmetry), yet most deep models operate on monocular representations. We investigate whether explicit structural correspondence improves diagnosis, and propose Anatomy-Slot to operationalize this hypothesis. Anatomy-Slot introduces an unsupervised anatomical bottleneck by decomposing patch tokens into slots and aligning slots across eyes via bidirectional cross-attention. On ODIR-5K with $n=10$ seeds, the method improves AUC by 4.2% over a matched ViT-L baseline (95% CIs; Wilcoxon signed-rank test, $W=0$, $p=0.002$). Pairing disruption and stress testing under Gaussian noise provide controlled tests of correspondence dependence and robustness under corruption. We further report quantitative optic disc grounding on REFUGE and cross-attention localization analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 视网膜诊断本质上是双眼的：临床医生会比较双眼间的对称结构（例如视盘不对称性），然而大多数深度学习模型基于单眼表征。本文研究了显式的结构对应是否能改善诊断，并提出 Anatomy-Slot 来验证这一假设。Anatomy-Slot 引入了一个无监督的解剖瓶颈，通过将图像块 token 分解为槽（slots），并利用双向交叉注意力对齐双眼间的槽。在 ODIR-5K 数据集上（10 个随机种子），相较于匹配的 ViT-L 基线，该方法 AUC 提升 4.2%（95% 置信区间；Wilcoxon 符号秩检验，W=0，p=0.002）。配对破坏实验和高斯噪声下的压力测试为结构对应依赖性和鲁棒性提供了受控检验。我们进一步在 REFUGE 数据集上报告了定量视盘定位结果以及交叉注意力定位分析。

</details>


### [20] [AuraMask: An Extensible Pipeline for Developing Aesthetic Anti-Facial Recognition Image Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12937)
*Jacob Lagogiannis,William Agnew,Rosa I. Arriaga,Sauvik Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AuraMask方法，在保持对抗有效性的同时生成美观的抗人脸识别滤镜，通过模仿Instagram滤镜风格提升用户接受度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有抗人脸识别滤镜虽然能欺骗计算机视觉系统，但图像修改对用户可见，与用户的自我呈现目标冲突，导致实际使用率低。

Method: 提出AuraMask方法，生成40种模仿Instagram"一键式"滤镜风格的"美观"抗人脸识别滤镜，在对抗有效性和美学可接受性之间取得平衡。

Result: 1) AuraMask滤镜在对抗开源人脸识别模型方面达到或超过现有方法的有效性；2) 通过N=630的在线用户研究证实，这些滤镜的用户接受度显著高于现有方法。

Conclusion: 成功实现了兼具对抗有效性和美学可接受性的抗人脸识别滤镜，并将相关流程开源以加速社区研究。

Original Abstract: Anti-facial recognition (AFR) image filters alter images in ways that are subtle to people but blinding to computer vision. Yet, despite widespread interest in these technologies to subvert surveillance, users rarely use them in practice -- because the ``subtle'' alterations are visible enough to conflict with users' self-presentation goals. To address this challenge, we propose AuraMask: a novel approach to creating AFR filters that are both adversarially effective and aesthetically acceptable. Using AuraMask, we produce 40 ``aesthetic'' filters that emulate popular ``one-click'' Instagram image filters. We show that AuraMask filters meet or exceed the adversarial effectiveness of prior methods against open-source facial recognition models. Moreover, in a controlled online user study ($N=630$) we confirm these filters achieve significantly higher user acceptance than prior methods. Lastly, we provide our AFR pipeline to the community for accelerated research in adversarially effective and aesthetically acceptable protections.

中文摘要翻译: 抗人脸识别（AFR）图像滤镜以对人眼微妙但对计算机视觉不可见的方式修改图像。然而，尽管这些颠覆监控的技术引起了广泛兴趣，用户在实践中很少使用它们——因为"微妙"的修改仍然足够可见，与用户的自我呈现目标相冲突。为应对这一挑战，我们提出AuraMask：一种创建既在对抗上有效又在美学上可接受的AFR滤镜的新方法。利用AuraMask，我们生成了40种模仿流行"一键式"Instagram图像滤镜的"美学"滤镜。我们展示了AuraMask滤镜在对抗开源人脸识别模型方面达到或超过现有方法的有效性。此外，在一项受控在线用户研究（N=630）中，我们确认这些滤镜的用户接受度显著高于现有方法。最后，我们将AFR流程提供给社区，以加速在对抗有效且美学可接受的保护方面的研究。

</details>


### [21] [CRePE: Curved Ray Expectation Positional Encoding for Unified-Camera-Controlled Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12938)
*Seonghyun Jin,Youngmin Kim,Sunwoo Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出曲射线期望位置编码(CRePE)，一种兼容统一相机模型的深度感知位置编码方法，通过几何注意力适配器将场景深度信息注入视频扩散Transformer，实现更稳定的相机控制并提升感知质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有注意力级别的相机编码要么仅提供射线级信号，要么依赖针孔相机几何假设，无法在统一相机模型下（包括广角和鱼眼镜头）实现通用相机控制。

Method: 提出CRePE，将每个图像token表示为沿其源射线的深度感知位置分布；通过几何注意力适配器注入到冻结的视频DiT中，并利用单目几何基础模型的伪监督进行稳定化训练；此外通过径向混合强迫(Radial MixForcing)扩展支持外部几何控制。

Result: 在多个几何感知和感知质量指标上优于基线方法；与RayRoPE风格的端点位置编码基线相比取得了更好的总体平均排名；在视频质量指标上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CRePE通过统一相机模型感知的投影路径集成，有效支持了多种相机模型下的相机控制，并可扩展至场景几何条件生成和源视频运动迁移等任务。

Original Abstract: Camera-conditioned video generation requires positional encoding that remains reliable under changes in camera motion, lens configuration, and scene structure. However, existing attention-level camera encodings either provide ray-only camera signals or rely on pinhole camera geometry, limiting their applicability to general camera control under the Unified Camera Model, including wide-angle and fisheye lenses. To address this limitation, we propose Curved Ray Expectation Positional Encoding (CRePE). CRePE represents each image token as a depth-aware positional distribution along its source ray, providing a Unified Camera Model-compatible positional encoding that captures the projected-path geometry induced by wide-angle and fisheye cameras. CRePE is implemented through a Geometric Attention Adapter added to frozen video DiTs, injecting token-wise scene-distance information into selected attention layers and stabilizing it with pseudo supervision from a monocular geometry foundation model. This design leads to more stable camera control and improves several geometry-aware and perceptual-quality metrics, while remaining competitive on video-quality metrics. Controlled positional-encoding ablations show a better overall average rank than a RayRoPE-style endpoint PE baseline, demonstrating the effectiveness of UCM-aware projected-path integration across diverse camera models. Furthermore, by extending the same positional-encoding pathway to external geometry control through Radial MixForcing, CRePE supports external radial-map control for scene-geometry-conditioned generation and source-video motion transfer beyond camera control.

中文摘要翻译: 相机条件视频生成需要一种在相机运动、镜头配置和场景结构变化下保持可靠的位置编码。然而，现有的注意力级别相机编码要么仅提供射线级相机信号，要么依赖针孔相机几何假设，限制了它们在统一相机模型（包括广角和鱼眼镜头）下实现通用相机控制的适用性。为解决这一局限，我们提出了曲射线期望位置编码（CRePE）。CRePE将每个图像token表示为其源射线上的深度感知位置分布，提供了一种兼容统一相机模型的位置编码，能够捕获广角和鱼眼相机所引入的投影路径几何。CRePE通过一个几何注意力适配器实现，该适配器附加在冻结的视频DiT上，将逐token的场景距离信息注入选定的注意力层，并通过来自单目几何基础模型的伪监督进行稳定化。该设计带来了更稳定的相机控制，并在多个几何感知和感知质量指标上取得改进，同时在视频质量指标上保持竞争力。可控的位置编码消融实验表明，相较于RayRoPE风格的端点位置编码基线，CRePE取得了更好的总体平均排名，证明了统一相机模型感知的投影路径集成在不同相机模型下的有效性。此外，通过将相同的位置编码路径扩展为径向混合强迫（Radial MixForcing）以支持外部几何控制，CRePE在相机控制之外进一步支持了场景几何条件生成和源视频运动迁移。

</details>


### [22] [DirectTryOn: One-Step Virtual Try-On via Straightened Conditional Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12939)
*Xianbing Sun,Jiahui Zhan,Liqing Zhang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对VTON（虚拟试衣）任务中扩散/流模型多步采样推理成本高的问题，本文提出通过三种针对性改进（纯条件传输、衣物保留损失、自一致性损失）使采样轨迹更直，并结合一步蒸馏，实现无需多步采样的SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散和流的VTON方法依赖预训练生成模型的多步采样，推理成本高；而现有加速方法忽略了试衣任务的内在结构。作者发现VTON输出受条件输入高度约束，其条件采样轨迹本可以比通用图像生成更直，但现有方法在有限数据下微调预训练模型时，预训练目标不鼓励这种直的条件轨迹，导致偏差主要来自模型与任务条件性之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出三项针对性修改使VTON采样轨迹更直：(1) 纯条件传输（pure conditional transport）；(2) 衣物保留损失（garment preservation loss）；(3) 自一致性损失（self consistency loss）。此外引入一步蒸馏阶段（one-step distillation stage）。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在一步采样下取得了最先进的性能，为高效高质量的VTON建立了新标准。

Conclusion: 通过针对VTON任务条件性本质的模型修改，可以迫使采样轨迹更直，结合一步蒸馏，在不牺牲质量的前提下大幅降低推理成本，实现高效高质量的虚拟试衣。

Original Abstract: Recent diffusion- and flow-based VTON methods achieve strong results with pretrained generative models, but their reliance on multi-step sampling incurs high inference cost, while existing acceleration methods largely overlook the intrinsic structure of the try-on task. In this paper, we highlight a key observation: VTON outputs are highly constrained by the conditional inputs, suggesting that the conditional sampling trajectory can be much straighter than that in general image generation, making one-step generation a natural solution. However, limited task-specific data makes training from scratch impractical, forcing existing methods to fine-tune pretrained models whose objectives do not encourage such straight conditional trajectories. Thus, the deviation from an ideal straight path mainly comes from the mismatch between pretrained base models and the conditional nature of try-on generation, rather than from the task itself. Motivated by this insight, we encourage straighter VTON sampling trajectories through three targeted modifications: pure conditional transport, a garment preservation loss, and a self consistency loss. We further introduce a one-step distillation stage. Extensive experiments show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance with one-step sampling, establishing a new standard for efficient and high-quality VTON.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的基于扩散和流的虚拟试衣（VTON）方法利用预训练生成模型取得了强劲的效果，但它们依赖多步采样导致推理成本高昂，而现有的加速方法大多忽略了试衣任务的内在结构。在本文中，我们强调一个关键观察：VTON输出受到条件输入的强烈约束，这表明条件采样轨迹可以比通用图像生成中的轨迹更直，使得一步生成成为一种自然的解决方案。然而，有限的任务特定数据使得从头训练不可行，迫使现有方法微调那些目标函数并不鼓励这种直条件轨迹的预训练模型。因此，与理想直路径的偏差主要来源于预训练基础模型与试衣生成的条件性本质之间的不匹配，而非任务本身。受此启发，我们通过三项针对性修改——纯条件传输、衣物保留损失和自一致性损失——来鼓励更直的VTON采样轨迹。我们进一步引入了一步蒸馏阶段。大量实验表明，我们的方法在一步采样下实现了最先进的性能，为高效高质量的VTON建立了新标准。

</details>


### [23] [Debunking Grad-ECLIP: A Comprehensive Study on Its Incorrectness and Fundamental Principles for Model Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12952)
*Yongjin Cui,Xiaohui Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出Grad-ECLIP（ICML 2024）所代表的基于中间特征的Transformer解释路线并非新颖，实为现有基于注意力路线的等价变体，且该方法存在根本性缺陷：其解释结果与原始模型不对齐。论文通过形式推导和实验验证了这两点，并提出了模型解释应遵循的两条基本原则。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 揭示Grad-ECLIP方法的本质——它并非新路线，而是注意力路线的等价变体；并指出该方法存在缺陷，其解释结果与原始模型性能不匹配。

Method: 1. 基于现有注意力路线构建Attention-ECLIP，该形式与Grad-ECLIP完全等价但计算更简洁。2. 通过形式推导和实验验证证明Grad-ECLIP等价于注意力路线。3. 分析Grad-ECLIP缺陷的根源，提出模型解释应遵循的两条基本原则。

Result: Grad-ECLIP的解释结果不是原始模型的真实解释，存在对齐偏差；Attention-ECLIP与Grad-ECLIP完全等价且计算更简单。

Conclusion: 中间特征路线并非创新，Grad-ECLIP方法存在根本性缺陷；论文明确强调了模型解释应遵循的两条基本原则以避免类似错误。

Original Abstract: Grad-ECLIP is published at ICML 2024 and represents a new Transformer interpretation technical route (intermediate features-based). First, this paper demonstrates that the intermediate features-based technical route is not a novel one. Based on the existing attention-based route, we have developed Attention-ECLIP, which is completely equivalent to Grad-ECLIP but with simpler computation. Both through formal derivation and experimental validation, we prove that the intermediate feature-based route represented by Grad-ECLIP is actually an equivalent variant of the attention-based route. Next, this paper demonstrates that the Grad-ECLIP method is flawed. The model interpretation results obtained by Grad-ECLIP are not those of the original model, and the interpretation results are misaligned with the model's performance. We analyze the causes of Grad-ECLIP's flaws and propose, or rather, explicitly emphasize two fundamental principles that model interpretation should adhere to in order to avoid similar errors.

中文摘要翻译: Grad-ECLIP发表于ICML 2024，代表了一种新的Transformer解释技术路线（基于中间特征）。首先，本文证明基于中间特征的技术路线并非一条新路线。基于现有的基于注意力的路线，我们开发了Attention-ECLIP，它与Grad-ECLIP完全等价但计算更简单。通过形式推导和实验验证，我们证明了以Grad-ECLIP为代表的基于中间特征的路线实际上是基于注意力路线的一个等价变体。其次，本文证明了Grad-ECLIP方法存在缺陷。Grad-ECLIP获得的模型解释结果并非原始模型的结果，解释结果与模型性能存在不对齐。我们分析了Grad-ECLIP缺陷的原因，并提出——更准确地说——明确强调了模型解释应遵循的两条基本原则，以避免类似的错误。

</details>


### [24] [Seg-Agent: Test-Time Multimodal Reasoning for Training-Free Language-Guided Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12953)
*Chao Hao,Jun Xu,Ji Du,Shuo Ye,Ziyue Qiao,Xiaodong Cun,Guangcong Wang,Xubin Zheng,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Seg-Agent提出了一种完全无需训练的多模态链式推理框架，通过显式的视觉推理循环（生成-选择-精炼）实现与训练方法相当的分割性能，并发布了新基准Various-LangSeg。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有语言引导分割方法依赖MLLM在文本域推理，缺乏直接视觉反馈，导致空间定位能力有限且需大规模数据集训练。

Method: 提出Seg-Agent，一种无需训练的三阶段显式多模态链式推理框架：(1) 生成阶段：利用Set-of-Mark (SoM)将候选区域渲染到图像上；(2) 选择阶段：MLLM在视觉域中迭代推理空间关系；(3) 精炼阶段：细化分割结果。

Result: 在不进行任何参数更新的情况下，Seg-Agent的性能可与现有基于训练的最先进方法相媲美；在涵盖显式语义、通用物体和推理引导分割任务的Various-LangSeg基准上验证了有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显式多模态交互推理（视觉域而非仅文本域）可以有效弥补MLLM空间定位能力的不足，实现无需训练的高性能语言引导分割。

Original Abstract: Language-guided segmentation transcends the scope limitations of traditional semantic segmentation, enabling models to segment arbitrary target regions based on natural language instructions. Existing approaches typically adopt a two-stage framework: employing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to interpret instructions and generate visual prompts, followed by foundational segmentation models (e.g., SAM) to produce masks. However, due to the limited spatial grounding capabilities of off-the-shelf MLLMs, these methods often rely on extensive training on large-scale datasets to achieve satisfactory accuracy. While recent advances have introduced reasoning mechanisms to improve performance, they predominantly operate within the textual domain, performing chain-of-thought reasoning solely based on abstract text representations without direct visual feedback. In this paper, we propose Seg-Agent, a completely training-free framework that pioneers Explicit Multimodal Chain-of-Reasoning. Unlike prior text-only reasoning, our approach constructs an interactive visual reasoning loop comprising three stages: generation, selection, and refinement. Specifically, we leverage Set-of-Mark (SoM) visual prompting to render candidate regions directly onto the image, allowing the MLLM to ``see'' and iteratively reason about spatial relationships in the visual domain rather than just the textual one. This explicit multimodal interaction enables Seg-Agent to achieve performance comparable to state-of-the-art training-based methods without any parameter updates. Furthermore, to comprehensively evaluate generalization across diverse scenarios, we introduce Various-LangSeg, a novel benchmark covering explicit semantic, generic object, and reasoning-guided segmentation tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 语言引导分割超越了传统语义分割的范围限制，使模型能够根据自然语言指令分割任意目标区域。现有方法通常采用两阶段框架：利用多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）解释指令并生成视觉提示，随后由基础分割模型（如SAM）生成掩码。然而，由于现成MLLMs的空间定位能力有限，这些方法通常需要在大规模数据集上进行大量训练才能达到令人满意的精度。虽然最近的进展引入了推理机制以提升性能，但这些机制主要在文本域内运行，仅基于抽象的文本表示进行思维链推理，缺乏直接的视觉反馈。本文提出了Seg-Agent，一个完全无需训练的框架，开创了显式多模态链式推理。与以往纯文本推理不同，我们的方法构建了一个包含生成、选择和精炼三个阶段的可交互视觉推理循环。具体而言，我们利用Set-of-Mark（SoM）视觉提示将候选区域直接渲染到图像上，使MLLM能够“看到”并在视觉域中迭代推理空间关系，而非仅在文本域中操作。这种显式的多模态交互使得Seg-Agent无需任何参数更新即可达到与基于训练的最先进方法相当的性能。此外，为了全面评估模型在多样化场景下的泛化能力，我们引入了Various-LangSeg，一个涵盖显式语义、通用物体和推理引导分割任务的新型基准。大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

</details>


### [25] [AdaFocus: Adaptive Relevance-Diversity Sampling with Zero-Cache Look-back for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12954)
*Xiao Yang,Yingzhe Ma,Haoxuan Yu,Zixin Li,Ning Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AdaFocus提出了一种渐进式证据获取的长视频理解框架，通过查询感知的自适应相关-多样性采样器（AdaRD）和不确定性触发的零缓存精炼机制，在显著降低视觉token消耗（~33倍）的同时提升任务性能（如VideoMME +2.59准确率，Charades-STA +8.39 mIoU）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法受限于僵化的一次性编码范式：密集编码导致高昂的内存和延迟开销，而激进压缩成稀疏帧集会不可逆地丢弃细粒度证据，难以同时兼顾时间覆盖、视觉细节和计算效率。

Method: AdaFocus包含两个核心组件：1) 查询感知自适应相关-多样性采样器（AdaRD），根据查询自适应生成紧凑信息预览，在查询缺乏可靠局部定位时切换到全局聚类；2) 不确定性触发的精炼机制，仅在模型不置信时进行定向回溯，通过零缓存I/O设计直接从磁盘检索高分辨率证据，将丢弃的视觉细节变为按需可恢复的证据。

Result: 在七个标准长视频基准上的实验表明，相比传统密集编码，AdaFocus在减少约33倍视觉token消耗、消除内存帧预缓存的同时，提升了任务性能（VideoMME +2.59准确率，Charades-STA +8.39 mIoU）。

Conclusion: 渐进式预览结合零缓存证据精炼是一种高效的可扩展多媒体推理范式，为长视频理解提供了更好的效率-准确率权衡。

Original Abstract: Long video understanding is heavily bottlenecked by a rigid one-shot paradigm: existing methods either densely encode videos at prohibitive memory and latency costs, or aggressively compress them into sparse frame sets that irreversibly discard fine-grained evidence needed for downstream reasoning. Consequently, current models struggle to simultaneously balance temporal coverage, visual details, and computational efficiency.
  We propose AdaFocus, an efficient framework that rethinks long-video understanding as progressive evidence acquisition rather than one-pass encoding. AdaFocus relies on two tightly coupled components. First, a Query-Aware Adaptive Relevance-Diversity sampler (AdaRD) produces a compact yet informative video preview, adaptively switching to global clustering when the query lacks reliable local grounding. Second, instead of caching exhaustive frame sequences in memory, AdaFocus introduces an uncertainty-triggered refinement mechanism. It performs targeted look-back only when the model is not confident, retrieving high-resolution evidence directly from disk via a zero-cache I/O design. This turns discarded visual details from an irreversible loss into on-demand recoverable evidence without paying the cost of exhaustive preloading.
  Experiments on seven standard long-video benchmarks show that AdaFocus delivers a substantially better efficiency-accuracy trade-off than strong baselines. Compared with conventional dense encoding, AdaFocus achieves improved task performance (e.g., +2.59 accuracy on VideoMME, +8.39 mIoU on Charades-STA over single-pass inference) while reducing visual token consumption by ~33x and eliminating the need for in-memory frame pre-caching through its zero-cache disk retrieval design. These findings suggest that progressive preview combined with zero-cache evidence refinement is a highly effective paradigm for scalable multimedia reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 长视频理解严重受限于僵化的一次性编码范式：现有方法要么以高昂的内存和延迟代价密集编码视频，要么将视频激进压缩成稀疏帧集，不可逆地丢弃了下游推理所需的细粒度证据。因此，当前模型难以同时兼顾时间覆盖、视觉细节和计算效率。我们提出AdaFocus，一个高效框架，将长视频理解重新构想为渐进式证据获取而非一次性编码。AdaFocus依赖于两个紧密耦合的组件。首先，查询感知自适应相关-多样性采样器（AdaRD）生成紧凑而信息丰富的视频预览，当查询缺乏可靠的局部定位时自适应切换到全局聚类。其次，AdaFocus不在内存中缓存详尽的帧序列，而是引入一种不确定性触发的精炼机制。该机制仅在模型不置信时执行定向回溯，通过零缓存I/O设计直接从磁盘检索高分辨率证据。这将丢弃的视觉细节从不可逆损失转变为按需可恢复的证据，而无需承担穷举预加载的成本。在七个标准长视频基准上的实验表明，AdaFocus提供了比强基线方法更好的效率-准确率权衡。与传统的密集编码相比，AdaFocus在提升任务性能的同时（例如，在VideoMME上准确率+2.59，在Charades-STA上mIoU+8.39，均优于单次推理），减少了约33倍的视觉token消耗，并通过其零缓存磁盘检索设计消除了内存帧预缓存的需求。这些发现表明，渐进式预览结合零缓存证据精炼是一种高效的、可扩展的多媒体推理范式。

</details>


### [26] [GTA: Advancing Image-to-3D World Generation via Geometry Then Appearance Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12957)
*Hanxin Zhu,Cong Wang,Peiyan Tu,Jiayi Luo,Tianyu He,Xin Jin,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GTA提出了一种遵循“几何优先、外观其次”范式的图像到3D世界生成方法，通过两阶段视频扩散模型先预测粗粒度几何结构再生成细粒度外观，并引入随机潜变量混洗策略和测试时缩放方案，显著提升了3D场景的结构保真度、跨视角一致性和视觉质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D世界生成方法主要关注外观预测，对底层几何结构的建模有限，导致场景结构估计不可靠和跨视角一致性差。受人类视觉感知从粗到细的启发，作者提出先预测几何结构再生成外观的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出两阶段框架GTA：第一阶段使用一个视频扩散模型从单张输入图像生成新颖视角下的粗粒度几何结构；第二阶段使用另一个视频扩散模型在预测的几何结构条件上生成细粒度外观。此外，训练过程中引入随机潜变量混洗策略以增强跨视角外观一致性，并采用测试时缩放方案在保持量化性能的同时提升感知质量。

Result: 大量实验表明GTA在保真度、视觉质量和几何精度方面持续优于现有方法；可作为通用增强模块提升现有图像到3D世界管线的生成质量；支持多种下游应用；在模型训练中展现出良好的数据效率。

Conclusion: GTA通过几何优先的生成范式有效解决了现有3D世界生成中结构估计不可靠和跨视角不一致的问题，具有优异的性能、通用性和广泛适用性。

Original Abstract: Recent developments in generative models and large-scale datasets have substantially advanced 3D world generation, facilitating a broad range of domains including spatial intelligence, embodied intelligence, and autonomous driving. While achieving remarkable progress, existing approaches to 3D world generation typically prioritize appearance prediction with limited modeling of the underlying geometry, leading to issues such as unreliable scene structure estimation and degraded cross-view consistency. To address these limitations, motivated by the coarse-to-fine nature of human visual perception, we propose GTA, a novel image-to-3D world generation method following a Geometry-Then-Appearance paradigm. Specifically, given a single input image, to improve the structural fidelity of synthesized 3D scenes, GTA adopts a two-stage framework with two dedicated video diffusion models, which first generate coarse geometric structure from novel viewpoints and then synthesize fine-grained appearance conditioned on the predicted geometry. To further enhance cross-view appearance consistency, we introduce a random latent shuffle strategy during the training process, along with a test-time scaling scheme that improves perceptual quality without compromising quantitative performance. Extensive experiments have demonstrated that our proposed method consistently outperforms existing approaches in terms of fidelity, visual quality, and geometric accuracy. Moreover, GTA is shown to be effective as a general enhancement module that further improves the generation quality of existing image-to-3D world pipelines, as well as supporting multiple downstream applications and exhibiting favorable data efficiency during model training, highlighting its versatility and broad applicability. Project page: https://hanxinzhu-lab.github.io/GTA/.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，生成模型和大规模数据集的进展显著推动了3D世界生成的发展，为空间智能、具身智能和自动驾驶等广泛领域提供了支持。尽管取得了令人瞩目的进展，现有3D世界生成方法通常优先考虑外观预测，而对底层几何结构的建模有限，导致场景结构估计不可靠和跨视角一致性下降等问题。为解决这些局限性，受人类视觉感知从粗到细特性的启发，我们提出GTA，一种遵循"几何优先、外观其次"范式的新型图像到3D世界生成方法。具体而言，给定单张输入图像，为提升合成3D场景的结构保真度，GTA采用两阶段框架，包含两个专门的视频扩散模型：首先生成新颖视角下的粗粒度几何结构，然后在预测的几何条件上合成细粒度外观。为进一步增强跨视角外观一致性，我们在训练过程中引入了随机潜变量混洗策略，同时提出一种测试时缩放方案，在不影响量化性能的前提下提升感知质量。大量实验证明，我们提出的方法在保真度、视觉质量和几何精度方面持续优于现有方法。此外，GTA被证明可作为通用增强模块，有效提升现有图像到3D世界管线的生成质量，同时支持多种下游应用，并在模型训练中展现出良好的数据效率，凸显了其多功能性和广泛的适用性。项目页面：https://hanxinzhu-lab.github.io/GTA/。

</details>


### [27] [Reducing Bias and Variance: Generative Semantic Guidance and Bi-Layer Ensemble for Image Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12961)
*Feijiang Li,Zhenxiong Li,Jieting Wang,Zizheng Jiu,Saixiong Liu,Liang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GSEC提出了一种基于生成式语义引导和双层集成的图像聚类框架，通过多模态大语言模型生成语义描述并利用双层集成策略同时降低偏差和方差，在六个基准数据集上超越了18种最先进方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于语义描述的图像聚类方法大多依赖匹配技术和预定义词汇表，匹配空间有限且适应性不足；此外，这些方法主要关注减少偏差以提升性能，却忽视了方差降低的重要性。

Method: GSEC框架包含两大核心：1) 生成式语义引导：利用多模态大语言模型生成语义描述，并通过加权平均推导图像嵌入；2) 双层集成策略：内层通过BatchEnsemble集成跨模态信息，外层通过对齐机制对齐输出结果。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，GSEC在聚类性能上超越了18种最先进方法；进一步分析证实了该方法在同时降低偏差和方差方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过生成式语义引导减少偏差、双层集成学习降低方差，GSEC有效克服了现有方法的局限性，为图像聚类任务提供了新的范式。

Original Abstract: Image clustering aims to partition unlabeled image datasets into distinct groups. A core aspect of this task is constructing and leveraging prior knowledge to guide the clustering process. Recent approaches introduce semantic descriptions as prior information, most of which typically relying on matching-based techniques with predefined vocabularies. However, the limited matching space restricts their adaptability to downstream clustering tasks. Moreover, these methods primarily focus on reducing bias to improve performance, frequently overlooking the importance of variance reduction. To address these limitations, we propose GSEC (Image Clustering based on Generative Semantic Guidance and Bi-Layer Ensemble), a framework designed to reduce bias through generative semantic guidance and mitigate variance via ensemble learning. Our method employs Multimodal Large Language Models to generate semantic descriptions and derive image embeddings via weighted averaging. Additionally, a bi-layer ensemble strategy integrates cross-modal information through BatchEnsemble in the inner layer and aligns outputs via an alignment mechanism in the outer layer. Comparative experiments demonstrate that GSEC outperforms 18 state-of-the-art methods across six benchmark datasets, while further analysis confirms its effectiveness in simultaneously reducing both bias and variance. The code is available at https://github.com/2017LI/GSEC.git.

中文摘要翻译: 图像聚类旨在将无标注的图像数据集划分为不同的组别。该任务的一个核心方面是构建并利用先验知识来指导聚类过程。近期的方法引入语义描述作为先验信息，其中大多数方法依赖基于匹配的技术并使用预定义词汇表。然而，有限的匹配空间限制了它们对下游聚类任务的适应性。此外，这些方法主要关注减少偏差以提升性能，却经常忽视方差降低的重要性。为应对这些局限性，我们提出了GSEC（基于生成式语义引导与双层集成的图像聚类），该框架通过生成式语义引导减少偏差，并通过集成学习缓解方差问题。我们的方法采用多模态大语言模型生成语义描述，并通过加权平均推导图像嵌入。此外，双层集成策略在内层通过BatchEnsemble整合跨模态信息，在外层通过对齐机制对齐输出结果。对比实验表明，GSEC在六个基准数据集上的表现超越了18种最先进方法，进一步分析证实了其在同时降低偏差和方差方面的有效性。代码已开源在 https://github.com/2017LI/GSEC.git。

</details>


### [28] [ImageAttributionBench: How Far Are We from Generalizable Attribution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12967)
*Tingshu Mou,Zhipeng Wei,Chao Gong,Jingjing Chen,Xingjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ImageAttributionBench——一个大规模、语义多样、基于先进生成模型的图像归属数据集，并揭示了现有归属方法在鲁棒性和泛化性上的严重不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像归属数据集规模有限、生成模型过时、语义多样性不足，阻碍了鲁棒且通用归属模型的开发，亟需一个更全面、更具挑战性的基准来推动该领域研究。

Method: 构建了ImageAttributionBench数据集，包含大量由多种先进生成模型（SOTA架构）合成的图像，覆盖多个真实世界语义领域。在此数据集上设计了两种挑战性评估设置：(1)标准均衡划分训练，在退化图像上测试；(2)在语义不重叠的划分上训练和测试。

Result: 在两种挑战性设置下，现有多种SOTA归属方法均表现不佳，暴露出在鲁棒性和对未见语义内容的泛化能力上存在显著局限。

Conclusion: 该工作为图像归属研究提供了一个严格的基准，有助于推动未来更鲁棒、更通用的归属方法的开发与评估。

Original Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI has enabled the creation of highly realistic and diverse synthetic images, posing critical challenges for image provenance and misinformation detection. This underscores the urgent need for effective image attribution. However, existing attribution datasets are constrained by limited scale, outdated generation methods, and insufficient semantic diversity - hindering the development of robust and generalizable attribution models. To address these limitations, we introduce ImageAttributionBench, a comprehensive dataset comprising images synthesized by a wide array of advanced generative models with state-of-the-art (SOTA) architectures. Covering multiple real-world semantic domains, the dataset offers rich diversity and scale to support and accelerate progress in image attribution research. To simulate real-world attribution scenarios, we evaluate several SOTA attribution methods on ImageAttributionBench under two challenging settings: (1) training on a standard balanced split and testing on degraded images, and (2) training and testing on semantically disjoint splits. In both cases, current methods exhibit consistently poor performance, revealing significant limitations in their robustness and generalization to unseen semantic content. Our work provides a rigorous benchmark to facilitate the development and evaluation of future image attribution methods.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式人工智能的快速发展使得创建高度逼真且多样化的合成图像成为可能，这给图像溯源和虚假信息检测带来了严峻挑战。因此，有效的图像归属需求变得十分迫切。然而，现有的归属数据集受到规模有限、生成方法陈旧以及语义多样性不足的制约，阻碍了鲁棒且泛化能力强的归属模型的开发。为应对这些局限，我们提出了ImageAttributionBench——一个综合性数据集，包含由多种具有最先进（SOTA）架构的先进生成模型合成的图像。该数据集覆盖多个真实世界语义领域，提供丰富的多样性和大规模样本，以支持和加速图像归属研究的进展。为模拟真实场景下的归属任务，我们在ImageAttributionBench上评估了多种SOTA归属方法，并设置了两种具有挑战性的条件：(1)在标准均衡划分上训练，在退化图像上测试；(2)在语义不重叠的划分上进行训练和测试。在这两种情况下，现有方法均表现出持续较差的性能，揭示了它们在鲁棒性以及对未见语义内容泛化能力上的显著局限。我们的工作为未来图像归属方法的开发和评估提供了一个严格的基准。

</details>


### [29] [Amortized Guidance for Image Inpainting with Pretrained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13010)
*Yilie Huang,Xun Yu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AID（Amortized Inpainting with Diffusion）的中间范式方法，通过冻结预训练扩散主干、训练一个可复用的轻量级引导模块，在不需逐实例优化的前提下实现高质量图像修复，在AFHQv2、FFHQ和ImageNet上均优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型的图像修复方法要么训练专用模型（成本高），要么在部署时对每个掩码图像单独适配预训练模型（计算开销大）。作者希望在保持预训练扩散模型固定不变的前提下，找到一个兼顾泛化能力和计算效率的中间方案。

Method: 将图像修复建模为带有监督终端目标的确定性引导问题；为解决高维空间的可学习性问题，推导了一个辅助高斯公式并证明解决该随机化问题可恢复最优确定性引导场；基于此桥接关系，提出了一个连续时间演员-评论家算法，以完全数据驱动的方式学习引导模块。

Result: 在AFHQv2和FFHQ（像素级EDM pipeline）以及ImageNet（潜在级EDM2 pipeline）上，AID在多种掩码类型下均持续提升了质量-速度权衡，优于强固定骨干基线和摊销修复基线，且额外可训练参数量不到1%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种既不需要训练专用模型、也不需要逐实例优化的图像修复方法，通过轻量级离线引导模块实现了高效且高质量的修复，为扩散模型的摊销式下游任务适配提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: We study image inpainting with generative diffusion models. Existing methods typically either train dedicated task-specific models, or adapt a pretrained diffusion model separately for each masked image at deployment. We introduce a middle-ground model, termed Amortized Inpainting with Diffusion (AID), which keeps a pretrained diffusion backbone fixed, trains a small reusable guidance module offline, and then reuses it across masked images without per-instance optimization. We formulate it as a deterministic guidance problem with a supervised terminal objective. To make this problem learnable in high dimensions, we derive an auxiliary Gaussian formulation and prove that solving this randomized problem recovers the optimal deterministic guidance field. This bridge yields a principled continuous-time actor--critic algorithm for learning the guidance module in a fully data-driven manner. Empirically, on AFHQv2 and FFHQ under the pixel EDM pipeline and on ImageNet under the latent EDM2 pipeline, AID consistently improves the quality--speed trade-off over strong fixed-backbone and amortized inpainting baselines across multiple mask types, while adding less than one percent trainable overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了基于生成扩散模型的图像修复问题。现有方法通常要么训练专用的任务特定模型，要么在部署时对每个掩码图像单独适配预训练扩散模型。我们引入了一种中间范式模型，称为摊销扩散修复（AID），该方法保持预训练扩散主干固定，离线训练一个小型可复用的引导模块，然后跨掩码图像复用该模块，无需逐实例优化。我们将其建模为具有监督终端目标的确定性引导问题。为了使这一过程在高维空间中可学习，我们推导了一个辅助高斯公式，并证明解决该随机化问题可以恢复最优的确定性引导场。这一桥接关系产生了一个原理性的连续时间演员-评论家算法，用于以完全数据驱动的方式学习引导模块。实验上，在像素级EDM pipeline下的AFHQv2和FFHQ数据集以及潜在级EDM2 pipeline下的ImageNet数据集上，AID在多种掩码类型下持续改善了质量-速度权衡，表现优于强固定骨干基线和摊销修复基线，同时额外可训练参数量不到百分之一。

</details>


### [30] [OCH3R: Object-Centric Holistic 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13018)
*Yi Du,Yang You,Xiang Wan,Leonidas Guibas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OCH3R提出了一种统一的单帧RGB图像端到端框架，能够同时预测所有物体实例的6D姿态和详细3D重建，推理为前馈式且速度随物体数量线性无关，显著优于传统多阶段流程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有以物体为中心的场景理解方法依赖多阶段流程（先分割再逐物体重建），计算开销大、对分割错误脆弱、随场景复杂度扩展性差，亟需一个统一高效的端到端解决方案。

Method: 采用Transformer架构预测每像素属性（CLIP类别嵌入、度量深度、NOCS、固定数量的3D高斯），通过预测的6D姿态将3D高斯变换到规范空间并与预渲染的规范真值对齐，避免逐图像高斯标签生成。

Result: 在标准室内基准上，OCH3R在单目深度估计、开放词汇语义分割、RGB-only类别级6D姿态估计三项任务上达到最先进性能，同时生成高质量、可编辑的逐物体重建，推理速度比传统多阶段流程快数个数量级。

Conclusion: OCH3R验证了统一的端到端框架在物体级场景理解中的有效性，推理完全前馈且与物体数量无关，为高效、可扩展的3D场景理解提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Object-centric scene understanding is a fundamental challenge in computer vision. Existing approaches often rely on multi-stage pipelines that first apply pre-trained segmentors to extract individual objects, followed by per-object 3D reconstruction. Such methods are computationally expensive, fragile to segmentation errors, and scale poorly with scene complexity. We introduce OCH3R, a unified framework for Object-Centric Holistic 3D Reconstruction from a single RGB image. OCH3R performs one forward pass to simultaneously predict all object instances with their 6D poses and detailed 3D reconstructions.
