中文摘要翻译: 心电图（ECG）是心脏评估的临床标准，但其需要专用硬件，无法扩展至日常生活中的持续监测。光电容积描记术（PPG）在可穿戴设备中无处不在，但缺乏ECG特异的诊断形态，且易受运动伪影和传感器噪声干扰。PPG-to-ECG生成旨在通过从外周脉搏信号恢复电形态和时序信息来弥合这一差距。然而，现有方法主要依赖统计对齐和数据驱动生成，未能显式构建围绕生理感知的电-血流动力学因素的隐空间结构，也缺乏来自前向生理动力学的约束。为解决这些挑战，我们提出了PG-LRF——一种生理引导的隐空间整流流框架。PG-LRF引入了一个电-血流动力学模拟器，通过共享的心动周期相位动态联合建模ECG和PPG。在该模拟器的引导下，生理感知自编码器学习了一个结构化的电-血流动力学隐空间。然后，我们将该模拟器引导融入PPG条件的隐空间整流流中，在生成传输过程中强制施加ECG侧的形态一致性约束以及ECG-to-PPG前向血流动力学一致性约束。在大规模MC-MED数据集上的实验表明，PG-LRF显著提升了PPG-to-ECG生成质量及下游心血管疾病分类性能，证明了其能够生成在ECG-to-PPG血流动力学通路下既保持信号保真度又具备生理合理性的ECG信号。

</details>


### [90] [Multiport Antenna Q-factor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12564)
*Vojtech Neuman,Miloslav Capek,Lukas Jelinek*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单端口Q因子推广的多端口天线带宽估计方法，通过将储能矩阵转换为端口等效形式并利用端口参数，推导出可单频评估带宽的公式，并用偶极子阵列和电大贴片天线阵列验证了理论。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单端口天线带宽估计方法不适用于多端口天线系统，亟需一种能够考虑馈电和匹配依赖关系的多端口带宽评估方法。

Method: 基于单端口Q因子的推广，将储能矩阵转换为端口等效形式，利用端口参数和总有源反射系数推导带宽估计公式，实现单频率点的带宽评估。

Result: 通过两种不同的偶极子阵列和电大贴片天线阵列的实例，验证了所提出的多端口带宽估计理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效估计多端口天线带宽，且考虑了馈电与匹配对带宽的影响，具有单频评估的便利性。

Original Abstract: This article proposes an estimate of multiport antenna bandwidth based on a generalization of a single-port Q-factor. The explicit derivation is based on converting the stored energy matrix to its port equivalent and on the port parameters themselves. The work discusses the bandwidth dependencies on feeding and matching. Derived formulas are shown to utilize the total active reflection coefficient and allow for a single-frequency bandwidth evaluation. Examples comprising two different dipole arrays and electrically large patch antenna arrays validate the theory.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于单端口Q因子推广的多端口天线带宽估计方法。其显式推导基于将储能矩阵转换为其端口等效形式以及端口参数本身。本文讨论了带宽对馈电和匹配的依赖性。推导出的公式利用总有效反射系数，并允许进行单频率带宽评估。包含两种不同偶极子阵列和电大贴片天线阵列的实例验证了该理论。

</details>


### [91] [Geometry-Aware Multi-Armed Bandits for Antenna Beam Selection on Spheres, Tori, $\SO(3)$, and Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13005)
*Yuriy Dorn,Changsheng Chen,Ning Xie*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于毫米波相控阵和RIS辅助链路的波束对齐方法，包括一个在离散环面 ($\mathbb Z_B)^M$ 上计算的克罗内克分解内在乘积Matérn核（$O(M)$复杂度），以及一个自适应滑动窗口控制器AdaptiveGP-v2，通过逐样本边际似然自动选择窗口大小，消除了部署时的每速度校准需求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在毫米波相控阵和RIS辅助链路中，波束对齐面临短TTI预算和多普勒引起的非平稳性双重挑战。臂空间是黎曼流形（如$\sphere^2$、$\torus^n$、$\SO(3)$或离散环面），其配置空间可达$K\sim10^{90}$，传统高斯过程（GP）方法因计算不可行而无法应用。因此需要可扩展的高斯过程方法和自适应非平稳性处理策略。

Method: (C1) 提出一种克罗内克分解内在乘积Matérn核，在离散环面$(\mathbb Z_B)^M$上仅需$O(M)$次查表即可完成核计算，使GP-UCB在$K\sim10^{90}$的配置空间中变得可行。(C2) 提出AdaptiveGP-v2在线滑动窗口控制器，通过逐样本边际似然自动选择窗口大小$W$，并引入预测方差和漂移z分数重置触发器以及重置后的$\beta$提升机制。

Result: 在四速度（$v\in\{0.02,0.08,0.12,0.20\}$ km/h）、20种子配对实验中，AdaptiveGP-v2在$T=3000$时与手动调优的固定窗口oracle统计上不可区分（Holm-Bonferroni校正后配对差异过零）。在四个静态3GPP风格毫米波基准测试中，内在核GP-UCB相比码本UCB1/Thompson累计遗憾降低25-45%，相比环面臂空间上的欧几里得环境GP-UCB降低10-33%。100 MHz宽带OFDM消融实验确认该优势在频率选择性衰落下仍然保持（初始接入约32 Mbps/UE vs. UCB1）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的内在核GP-UCB和自适应滑动窗口控制器能够有效处理毫米波波束对齐中的高维离散臂空间和非平稳环境，在性能上与最优固定窗口方案相当，且无需部署时的每速度校准，显著降低了实际部署的调参成本。

Original Abstract: Beam alignment in mmWave phased arrays and RIS-assisted links is a stochastic bandit under both short TTI budgets and Doppler-induced non-stationarity. The arm space is a Riemannian manifold: $\sphere^2$ for steering, $\torus^n$ for phase combining, $\SO(3)$ for panel orientation, or the discrete torus $(\mathbb Z_B)^M$ with up to $K\!\sim\!10^{90}$ configurations for $B$-level RIS ($B\!=\!2^b$, $b$ bits/element); the intrinsic Matérn kernel of Borovitskiy et al.\ provides the base GP. We contribute two algorithmic pieces. \textbf{(C1)} A Kronecker-factorised intrinsic-product Matérn kernel on $(\mathbb Z_B)^M$ evaluating in $O(M)$ table lookups, making GP-UCB tractable at $K\sim 10^{90}$ where the extrinsic alternative is infeasible. \textbf{(C2)} AdaptiveGP-v2, an online sliding-window controller that selects $W$ by per-sample marginal likelihood, with predictive-variance and drift $z$-score reset triggers and a post-reset $β$-boost. On a four-speed ($v\!\in\!\{0.02,0.08,0.12,0.20\}$~km/h), $20$-seed paired campaign at $T\!=\!3000$, AdaptiveGP-v2 is statistically indistinguishable from the hand-tuned fixed-window oracle at every speed (Holm--Bonferroni-corrected paired differences cross zero); the operational benefit is the absence of a deployment-time per-speed calibration step, not a mean-regret improvement. On four static 3GPP-style mmWave benchmarks, intrinsic-kernel GP-UCB reduces cumulative regret by $25$--$45\%$ vs.\ codebook UCB1/Thompson and by $10$--$33\%$ vs.\ Euclidean-ambient GP-UCB on the toroidal arm spaces; a wideband OFDM ablation on a $100$~MHz channel confirms the advantage persists under frequency-selective fading ($\sim\!32$~Mbps/UE at initial access vs.\ UCB1). A third-party-simulator sanity check on Sionna CDL is reported in Section~V.

中文摘要翻译: 毫米波相控阵和RIS辅助链路中的波束对齐是一个面临短TTI预算和多普勒诱导非平稳性的随机赌博机问题。臂空间是黎曼流形：$\sphere^2$（波束控制）、$\torus^n$（相位组合）、$\SO(3)$（面板朝向），或离散环面$(\mathbb Z_B)^M$，其配置数可达$K\!\sim\!10^{90}$（$B$级RIS，$B\!=\!2^b$，$b$比特/单元）；Borovitskiy等人的内在Matérn核提供了基础高斯过程。我们贡献了两个算法模块。\textbf{(C1)} 在$(\mathbb Z_B)^M$上提出一种克罗内克分解的内在乘积Matérn核，仅需$O(M)$次查表即可完成计算，使得GP-UCB在$K\sim10^{90}$的规模下变得可行，而外蕴方法在此规模下不可行。\textbf{(C2)} AdaptiveGP-v2，一种在线滑动窗口控制器，通过逐样本边际似然自动选择窗口$W$，配备预测方差和漂移$z$分数重置触发器以及重置后的$\beta$提升机制。在四速度（$v\!\in\!\{0.02,0.08,0.12,0.20\}$ km/h）、20种子配对实验中，在$T\!=\!3000$时，AdaptiveGP-v2在每个速度下与手动调优的固定窗口oracle统计上不可区分（Holm-Bonferroni校正的配对差异过零）；其操作优势在于无需部署时的每速度校准步骤，而非均值遗憾的改进。在四个静态3GPP风格毫米波基准测试中，内在核GP-UCB相比码本UCB1/Thompson累计遗憾降低25-45%，相比环面臂空间上的欧几里得环境GP-UCB降低10-33%；在100 MHz信道上的宽带OFDM消融实验确认该优势在频率选择性衰落下仍然保持（初始接入约32 Mbps/UE vs. UCB1）。第三节报告了在Sionna CDL上的第三方模拟器一致性验证。

</details>


### [92] [Compact Latent Manifold Translation: A Parameter-Efficient Foundation Model for Cross-Modal and Cross-Frequency Physiological Signal Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13248)
*Bo Cui,Xiaowen Song,Yaowen Zhang,Shunzhe Zhang,B. J. F. van Beijnum,Monique Tabak,Ying Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出紧凑潜在流形翻译（CLMT），一种仅0.09B参数的高效统一框架，通过两级离散翻译范式（通用分词器+上下文提示潜在翻译器）弥合生理时间序列（如ECG和PPG）的模态与频率差异，在跨模态合成和极端超分辨率任务中显著超越大规模基线模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生理信号基础模型受限于连续潜在空间，存在模态纠缠严重、缺乏高保真跨频率生成能力、计算成本高无法部署于边缘设备等问题。

Method: 提出CLMT框架：1）通用分词器（Universal Tokenizer）利用分层残差向量量化（RVQ）将异构信号解耦为孤立、结构良好的离散潜在流形，防止跨模态干扰；2）上下文提示潜在翻译器（Context-Prompted Latent Translator）通过整合静态生理先验，将离散令牌跨模态映射，将复杂信号合成问题转化为纯潜在序列翻译任务。

Result: 0.09B模型显著优于大规模基线：在跨模态PPG到ECG合成中解决时间相位漂移，临床R峰检测F1分数从0.37提升至0.83；在极端跨频率超分辨率（25Hz到100Hz）任务中，成功恢复高频诊断标志，皮尔逊相关系数达0.9956。

Conclusion: 通过学习生物信号的通用离散语言并以极低计算开销实现跨模态/跨频率翻译，为可部署于边缘设备的多模态医学基础模型开辟了新路径。

Original Abstract: The analysis of physiological time series, such as electrocardiograms (ECG) and photoplethysmograms (PPG), is persistently hindered by modality and frequency gaps stemming from heterogeneous recording devices. Existing foundation models typically rely on continuous latent spaces, which frequently suffer from severe modality entanglement, lack high-fidelity cross-frequency generative capacity, and impose high computational costs that prohibit edge-device deployment. In this paper, we propose Compact Latent Manifold Translation (CLMT), a highly parameter-efficient (0.09B) unified framework that bridges these gaps through a novel two-stage discrete translation paradigm. First, we introduce a Universal Tokenizer utilizing Hierarchical Residual Vector Quantization (RVQ) to decouple heterogeneous signals into isolated, well-structured discrete latent manifolds, effectively preventing inter-modality interference. Second, a Context-Prompted Latent Translator maps these discrete tokens across modalities by integrating static physiological priors, reframing complex signal synthesis as a pure latent sequence translation task. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our 0.09B model significantly outperforms massive baselines. In cross-modal PPG-to-ECG synthesis, it resolves temporal phase drift and dramatically improves the clinical R-peak detection F1-score from 0.37 (baseline) to 0.83. Furthermore, in extreme cross-frequency super-resolution (25Hz to 100Hz), it successfully recovers high-frequency diagnostic landmarks, achieving an unprecedented Pearson correlation of 0.9956. By learning a universal discrete language for biological signals with a fraction of the computational footprint, our approach sets a new trajectory for edge-deployable, multi-modal medical foundation models.

中文摘要翻译: 心电图（ECG）和光电容积描记图（PPG）等生理时间序列的分析，长期受到异质记录设备所导致的模态和频率差异的阻碍。现有的基础模型通常依赖连续潜在空间，这类空间常常遭受严重的模态纠缠问题、缺乏高保真度的跨频率生成能力，并且计算成本高昂，无法在边缘设备上部署。本文提出紧凑潜在流形翻译（CLMT），一种高度参数高效（0.09B）的统一框架，通过一种新颖的两阶段离散翻译范式弥合上述差距。首先，我们引入一个利用分层残差向量量化（RVQ）的通用分词器，将异构信号解耦为孤立、结构良好的离散潜在流形，有效防止跨模态干扰。其次，一个上下文提示潜在翻译器通过整合静态生理先验将这些离散令牌跨模态映射，将复杂的信号合成重新定义为纯粹的潜在序列翻译任务。大量评估表明，我们的0.09B模型显著优于大规模基线模型。在跨模态PPG到ECG合成中，它解决了时间相位漂移问题，并将临床R峰检测F1分数从0.37（基线）大幅提升至0.83。此外，在极端跨频率超分辨率（25Hz至100Hz）任务中，它成功恢复了高频诊断标志，达到了前所未有的0.9956皮尔逊相关系数。通过以极小的计算开销学习生物信号的通用离散语言，我们的方法为可部署于边缘的多模态医学基础模型开辟了新道路。

</details>


### [93] [Implementing Fluid Antennas in the Beamspace: Performance Evaluation and Codebook Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13286)
*Pablo Ramírez-Espinosa,F. Javier López-Martínez,David Morales-Jiménez*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文评估了基于超表面的流体天线系统（FAS）在多用户通信中的性能，提出了一种考虑实际因素的通用码本设计方法，并证明通过合理设计，基于超表面的FAS利用干扰零空间投影实现干扰消除，显著优于概念性FAS设计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于超表面的FAS作为流体天线概念的廉价、可扩展且实用的替代方案已被提出，但其在多用户通信场景下的性能尚未得到全面评估，因此需要对其性能进行深入研究。

Method: 将FAS的现有信号模型扩展到电子可重构设计，将天线响应显式纳入等效信道及相关结构中；提出一种考虑反射和辐射效率等实际因素的通用码本设计流程，用于设计不同的天线配置（即FAS端口）。

Result: 数值结果表明，在系统受干扰主导时（即FAS的自然工作区间），基于超表面的FAS在性能上有显著提升，且这种提升不受空间传播特性的影响。

Conclusion: 通过合理设计，基于超表面的FAS可利用干扰零空间投影实现干扰消除，而非传统概念性FAS依赖空间灵活性实现相长/相消干涉，从而在多用户通信中显著优于概念性FAS设计。

Original Abstract: Metasurface-based fluid antenna systems (FASs) have been recently proposed as an inexpensive, scalable and practical alternative implementation for the fluid-antenna concept. This work thoroughly evaluates the performance of metasurface-based FASs in the context of multi-user communications. We extend the state-of-the-art signal model of FASs to electronically-reconfigurable designs, explicitly including the antenna response in the equivalent channel and resulting correlation structure. A general codebook design procedure, accounting for practical aspects like reflections and radiation efficiency, is presented and used to design the different antenna configurations (regarded as FAS ports). Importantly, we show that, with proper design, metasurface-based FASs can significantly outperform conceptual ones. While state-of-the-art theoretical embodiments of FAS rely on spatial flexibility for constructive/destructive interference, metasurface-based FASs exploit interference cancellation through projection onto the interference null space. Numerical results show a remarkable improvement when the system is dominated by interference (i.e., the natural FASs operational regime), regardless of spatial propagation characteristics.

中文摘要翻译: 基于超表面的流体天线系统（FAS）最近被提出作为流体天线概念的一种廉价、可扩展且实用的替代实现方案。本文全面评估了基于超表面的FAS在多用户通信中的性能。我们将FAS的现有信号模型扩展到电子可重构设计，将天线响应显式纳入等效信道及相关结构中。提出了一种考虑反射和辐射效率等实际因素的通用码本设计流程，用于设计不同的天线配置（即FAS端口）。重要的是，我们表明，通过合理设计，基于超表面的FAS可以显著优于概念性FAS。当前最先进的理论FAS实现依赖于空间灵活性来实现相长/相消干涉，而基于超表面的FAS则通过投影到干扰零空间来利用干扰消除。数值结果表明，当系统受干扰主导时（即FAS的自然工作区间），无论空间传播特性如何，性能均有显著提升。

</details>


### [94] [Decoupled Azimuth Elevation AoA Estimation Exploiting Kronecker Separable Steering Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13394)
*Faizan A. Khattak,Ian K. Proudler,Stephan Weiss,Fazal-E Asim*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子空间解耦框架的二维到达角估计方法，利用阵列流型向量的Kronecker积结构，通过低复杂度解耦方案分别恢复方位角和俯仰角子空间，在中等和大型阵列上比现有方法精度更高、所需快拍数更少。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有二维到达角估计方法（如2D-MUSIC、降维MUSIC、2D-ESPRIT）计算复杂度高或在小快拍数下性能不佳，需要一种计算经济且谱效率更高的子空间解耦方法。

Method: 首先从空间协方差矩阵中提取联合信号子空间，然后应用低复杂度解耦方案恢复方位角和俯仰角导向矩阵的列空间，最后利用估计的解耦子空间独立沿每个维度应用一维算法（如MUSIC、root MUSIC、ESPRIT），并通过二维谱函数配对。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明，对于中大型阵列，该方法比2D-MUSIC、降维MUSIC和2D-ESPRIT等现有方法精度更高，且所需快拍数更少，谱效率更优。

Conclusion: 利用URA、NURA、UPgA和NUPgA阵列流型向量的Kronecker积结构，所提出的子空间解耦框架能够实现低复杂度、高精度的二维AoA估计，特别适用于中大型阵列和快拍受限场景。

Original Abstract: Uniform rectangular arrays (URA), structured non-uniform rectangular arrays (NURA), and parallelogram shaped (UPgA and NUPgA) arrays admit steering vectors that can be expressed as the Kronecker product of azimuth and elevation steering vectors. Accordingly, the full steering matrix can be represented as the Khatri Rao product of the corresponding azimuth and elevation steering matrices. This paper exploits this structure to develop an economical subspace decoupling framework for two dimensional angle of arrival (AoA) estimation. The proposed method first extracts the joint signal subspace from the spatial covariance matrix. Then it applies a low complexity decoupling scheme to recover the column spaces of the azimuth and elevation steering matrices. With the estimated decoupled subspaces, conventional one dimensional algorithms such as MUSIC, root MUSIC, and ESPRIT can be applied independently along each dimension, followed by pairing through a two dimensional spectral function. Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed approach achieves higher accuracy than state of the art methods, i.e., two dimensional MUSIC, reduced-dimension MUSIC, and two-dimensional ESPRIT, for medium- and large scale arrays while requiring fewer snapshots, consequently with improved spectral efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 均匀矩形阵列（URA）、结构化非均匀矩形阵列（NURA）以及平行四边形阵列（UPgA和NUPgA）的导向向量可以表示为方位角和俯仰角导向向量的Kronecker积。相应地，完整的导向矩阵可以表示为对应方位角和俯仰角导向矩阵的Khatri-Rao积。本文利用这一结构，为二维到达角（AoA）估计开发了一种经济的子空间解耦框架。所提方法首先从空间协方差矩阵中提取联合信号子空间，然后应用低复杂度解耦方案恢复方位角和俯仰角导向矩阵的列空间。利用估计出的解耦子空间，可以沿每个维度独立应用传统一维算法（如MUSIC、root MUSIC和ESPRIT），随后通过二维谱函数进行配对。蒙特卡洛仿真表明，对于中大型阵列，所提方法相比现有先进方法（即二维MUSIC、降维MUSIC和二维ESPRIT）实现了更高的估计精度，同时所需快拍数更少，从而提高了谱效率。

</details>


### [95] [A Multi-Modal Intelligent U2V Channel Model for 6G Sensing-Communication Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13502)
*Shuo Wang,Zengrui Han,Lu Bai,Xiang Cheng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对6G智能通感一体化场景，提出了一种基于3D散射体预测的U2V信道模型。通过构建高保真混合通感一体化仿真数据集，设计3D-SPADE算法利用LiDAR点云预测散射体空间分布，并对散射体进行动态/静态聚类，实现对信道非平稳性和一致性的精确建模。实验表明该算法在散射体占用检测上达到93.26%召回率和95.74%精确率，信道统计特性与射线追踪高度一致。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有U2V信道模型在高度动态的城市场景中难以精确建模物理环境与电磁空间的映射关系，缺乏对散射体时空演变的有效预测和动态/静态区分，导致信道非平稳性建模精度不足。

Method: 构建高保真混合通感一体化U2V仿真数据集；提出3D-SPADE算法，利用LiDAR点云预测散射体三维空间分布；对散射体进行聚类并分类为动态和静态类型；通过3D-SPADE跟踪动态与静态簇的时变演化。

Result: 在宽车道场景下，3D-SPADE在不同VTD和UAV高度下均能稳定实现高散射体占用检测性能，最优配置下召回率达93.26%，精确率达95.74%。信道统计特性与射线追踪结果高度一致，优于标准化模型和不一致模型。

Conclusion: 探索物理环境与电磁空间的映射关系对U2V信道建模至关重要，所提出的3D-SPADE算法和信道模型有效提升了建模精度和信道非平稳性表征能力。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes a novel UAV-to-Vehicle (U2V) channel model for sixth-generation (6G) intelligent sensing-communication integration, based on three-dimensional (3D) scatterer prediction. To explore the mapping relationship between physical environment and electromagnetic space, a new high-fidelity mixed sensing-communication integration U2V simulation dataset under wide-lane scenarios with different vehicular traffic densities (VTDs) and UAV heights is constructed. Based on the constructed dataset, a novel 3D Scatterer Prediction and Distribution Estimation (3D-SPADE) algorithm is proposed, which leverages LiDAR point clouds to accurately predict the spatial distribution of scatterers. Furthermore, the clustering of scatterers and the subsequent classification into dynamic and static types are meticulously designed for highly dynamic U2V scenarios, while reducing computational complexity and improving modeling accuracy. As LiDAR point clouds vary over time, dynamic and static clusters evolve via 3D-SPADE, enabling precise modeling of channel non-stationarity and consistency. Simulation results demonstrate that, in the wide-lane scenario with varying VTDs and UAV heights, the proposed 3D-SPADE consistently achieves high scatterer occupancy detection performance within the voxel grid. In particular, under favorable configurations, recall reaches 93.26%, and precision reaches 95.74%, highlighting the reliability of 3D-SPADE. Key channel statistical characteristics are simulated and analyzed. These characteristics from the simulation experiments are highly consistent with ray-tracing results and exhibit better agreement than with the standardized model and inconsistent model, validating the necessity of exploring the mapping relationship and the effectiveness of the proposed model.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对第六代（6G）智能通感一体化，提出了一种基于三维（3D）散射体预测的新型无人机到车辆（U2V）信道模型。为探索物理环境与电磁空间之间的映射关系，构建了宽车道场景下不同车辆交通密度（VTD）和无人机高度下的高保真混合通感一体化U2V仿真数据集。基于所构建的数据集，提出了一种新颖的3D散射体预测与分布估计（3D-SPADE）算法，该算法利用LiDAR点云精确预测散射体的空间分布。在此基础上，针对高度动态的U2V场景，精心设计了散射体的聚类以及随后的动态/静态类型分类，同时降低了计算复杂度并提高了建模精度。由于LiDAR点云随时间变化，动态和静态聚类通过3D-SPADE进行演化，从而实现对信道非平稳性和一致性的精确建模。仿真结果表明，在变化VTD和无人机高度的宽车道场景下，所提出的3D-SPADE算法在体素网格内始终能实现高散射体占用检测性能。特别是在有利配置下，召回率达到93.26%，精确率达到95.74%，彰显了3D-SPADE的可靠性。仿真实验还对关键信道统计特性进行了模拟与分析。这些特性与射线追踪结果高度一致，且优于标准化模型和不一致模型，验证了探索映射关系的必要性以及所提模型的有效性。

</details>


### [96] [Manifold-Aware Information Gain and Lower Bounds for Gaussian-Process Bandits on Riemannian Quotient Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13524)
*Yuriy Dorn,Changsheng Chen,Ning Xie*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文为光滑紧致黎曼流形上具有Matérn-ν核的高斯过程bandit问题推导了依赖于流形几何（体积、曲率、商空间结构）的遗憾下界，并给出了五个方向的扩展分析，包括Assouad型下界、商空间上的外禀核GP-UCB算法上界、常数显式化、曲率依赖以及贝叶斯遗憾迁移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高斯过程bandit的遗憾界通常忽略或隐含了臂空间（arm space）的几何结构，而实际中臂空间往往具有内在的黎曼流形结构（如SO(3)等）。本文旨在揭示流形几何（体积、曲率、对称性）如何显式地影响遗憾常数，填补遗憾界中几何依赖性这一理论空白。

Method: (1) 利用Fano不等式及信息论方法，结合流形上Matérn核的RKHS性质，推导依赖于流形体积的遗憾下界；(2) 采用Assouad引理提供另一种下界证明，获得不同的T指数和对数因子；(3) 对商空间M̃/G场景，提出外禀核GP-UCB算法并证明|G|^{1/2}上界，辅以数值验证；(4) 通过Bishop-Gromov比较定理提取曲率依赖项；(5) 利用Yang-Barron/Castillo等人贝叶斯-Fano迁移技巧将下界转移到贝叶斯遗憾框架。

Result: (i) 主下界：E[R_T(f)] ≥ c_*(d,ν) B^{d/(2ν+d)} σ_n^{2ν/(2ν+d)} vol_g(M)^{ν/(2ν+d)} T^{(ν+d)/(2ν+d)} (log T)^{ν/(2ν+d)}，指数匹配Vakili等人上界，体积因子为首个显式几何常数；(ii) Assouad型下界：T指数更小但polylog因子为1/(log log T)^{(2ν+d)/(4(ν+d))}；(iii) 商空间上|G|^{1/2}上界及猜想调制形式；(iv) 曲率修正1+O(K ε_T^2)；(v) 成功迁移至贝叶斯遗憾。

Conclusion: 流形几何结构（体积、曲率、对称性）在高斯过程bandit的遗憾界中起着本质性作用，本文首次将其显式量化。该工作为几何感知的贝叶斯优化算法设计提供了理论基础，并为后续研究开辟了方向，包括精确曲率依赖、商空间调制常数及Assouad下界的紧性等。

Original Abstract: We prove a regret lower bound for Gaussian-process bandits on a smooth compact Riemannian manifold $\M$ of dimension $d$ with intrinsic Matérn-$ν$ kernel ($ν>d/2$) that exposes how the geometry of the arm space enters the constant. For any algorithm and time horizon $T$ exceeding an explicit threshold, the worst-case expected regret over the RKHS-ball $\|f\|_{\Hil_{k_ν}}\!\le\!B$ satisfies \begin{multline*} \E[R_T(f)]\;\ge\;c_*(d,ν)\,B^{d/(2ν+d)}\,σ_n^{2ν/(2ν+d)} \\ \cdot\,\vol_g(\M)^{ν/(2ν+d)}\,T^{(ν+d)/(2ν+d)}(\log T)^{ν/(2ν+d)}. \end{multline*} The exponent matches the Vakili--Khezeli--Picheny upper bound \cite{vakili2021information}; the $\vol_g(\M)^{ν/(2ν+d)}$ factor is, to our knowledge, the first explicit volume-dependent geometric constant in a manifold GP-bandit lower bound. We extend the analysis in five directions: (i)~a companion Assouad-style proof gives a different lower bound with a strictly smaller $T$-exponent $(2ν+3d)/(4(ν+d))$ but with a polylog factor of the form $1/(\log\log T)^{(2ν+d)/(4(ν+d))}$, sharpening the $(\log T)^{ν/(2ν+d)}$ Fano polylog of Theorem~\ref{thm:main}; (ii)~we prove a $|G|^{1/2}$ upper bound on the regret of an extrinsic-kernel GP-UCB algorithm on a quotient space $\M=\Mt/G$, plus a bracketing theorem (Theorem~\ref{thm:gauge-bracket}); the precise constant is conjectured to take the modulated form $(1+(|G|-1)h(\rinj/κ))^{1/2}$ (Conjecture~\ref{conj:gauge-modulated}), validated numerically on $\SO(3)$; (iii)~we write the leading constant $c_*(d,ν)$ out fully; (iv)~we extract a curvature dependence $1+O(K\eps_T^2)$ via Bishop--Gromov; (v)~we transfer the bound to the Bayesian regret framework via the Yang--Barron / Castillo et al.\ Bayesian-Fano transfer.

中文摘要翻译: 我们证明了光滑紧致黎曼流形$\M$（维度$d$）上具有内蕴Matérn-$\nu$核（$\nu>d/2$）的高斯过程bandit的遗憾下界，该下界揭示了臂空间的几何如何进入常数。对于任何算法和时间跨度$T$超过显式阈值，RKHS球$\|f\|_{\Hil_{k_\nu}}\!\le\!B$上的最坏情形期望遗憾满足
\begin{multline*}
\E[R_T(f)]\;\ge\;c_*(d,\nu)\,B^{d/(2\nu+d)}\,\sigma_n^{2\nu/(2\nu+d)} 
\cdot\,\vol_g(\M)^{\nu/(2\nu+d)}\,T^{(\nu+d)/(2\nu+d)}(\log T)^{\nu/(2\nu+d)}.
\end{multline*}
指数与Vakili--Khezeli--Picheny上界\cite{vakili2021information}相匹配；$\vol_g(\M)^{\nu/(2\nu+d)}$因子，据我们所知，是流形GP-bandit下界中首个显式的体积依赖几何常数。我们将分析扩展到五个方向：(i)~一种伴随的Assouad型证明给出了不同的下界，其$T$指数严格更小，为$(2\nu+3d)/(4(\nu+d))$，但polylog因子形式为$1/(\log\log T)^{(2\nu+d)/(4(\nu+d))}$，改进了定理~\ref{thm:main}中Fano的$(\log T)^{\nu/(2\nu+d)}$ polylog；(ii)~我们在商空间$\M=\Mt/G$上证明了一个外禀核GP-UCB算法的$|G|^{1/2}$遗憾上界，外加一个包络定理（定理~\ref{thm:gauge-bracket}）；推测精确常数取调制形式$(1+(|G|-1)h(\rinj/\kappa))^{1/2}$（猜想~\ref{conj:gauge-modulated}），并在$\SO(3)$上进行了数值验证；(iii)~我们将领先常数$c_*(d,\nu)$完全写出；(iv)~我们通过Bishop--Gromov引理提取出曲率依赖项$1+O(K\eps_T^2)$；(v)~我们通过Yang--Barron / Castillo等人的贝叶斯-Fano迁移技巧将下界迁移到贝叶斯遗憾框架。

</details>


### [97] [Joint Segment Activation and Antenna Placement for Uplink SWAN Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13580)
*Songnan Gu,Zhenqiao Cheng,Hao Jiang,Chongjun Ouyang,Yuanwei Liu,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了多用户上行链路分段波导赋能钉形天线系统（SWANs）的可达和速率，推导了可达和速率上界，理论上证明了最优分段激活水平的存在性，并提出了混合分段选择与聚合（HSS/A）方案及其低复杂度贪心算法，数值结果验证了理论分析并表明所提方案优于传统全分段聚合。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为了揭示分段波导赋能钉形天线系统（SWANs）的系统设计洞见，并解决分段激活与钉形天线（PA）放置的联合优化问题。

Method: 首先推导可达和速率的上界，基于此理论证明最优分段激活水平的存在性；然后提出混合分段选择与聚合（HSS/A）方案以联合优化分段激活和PA放置；并开发了相应的低复杂度贪心算法。

Result: 数值结果验证了理论分析的正确性，并表明所提出的HSS/A方案在可达和速率性能上优于传统的全分段聚合方案。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上建立了最优分段激活水平的存在性，所提出的HSS/A方案配合低复杂度贪心算法能够有效提升系统可达和速率性能。

Original Abstract: This article analyzes the achievable sum-rate of multiuser uplink segmented waveguide-enabled pinching-antenna systems (SWANs). To unveil system-design insights, an upper bound on the achievable sum-rate is derived, based on which the existence of an optimal segment activation level is theoretically established. Motivated by this result, hybrid segment selection and aggregation (HSS/A) schemes are proposed to jointly optimize segment activation and pinching-antenna (PA) placement. Correspondingly, low-complexity greedy algorithms are developed for the considered optimization problem. Numerical results validate the theoretical analysis and demonstrate that the proposed HSS/A schemes outperform conventional full-segment aggregation.

中文摘要翻译: 本文分析了多用户上行链路分段波导赋能钉形天线系统（SWANs）的可达和速率。为揭示系统设计洞见，推导了可达和速率的上界，并在此基础上从理论上证明了最优分段激活水平的存在性。受此结果启发，提出了混合分段选择与聚合（HSS/A）方案，以联合优化分段激活和钉形天线（PA）的放置。相应地，针对所考虑的优化问题开发了低复杂度的贪心算法。数值结果验证了理论分析，并表明所提出的HSS/A方案优于传统的全分段聚合方案。

</details>


### [98] [Air-Sea Surface Modeling and Operating Link Range Evaluation for AUV-to-UAV Optical Wireless Communication Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13661)
*Ikenna Chinazaekpere Ijeh,Mohammad Ali Khalighi,Wasiu O. Popoola*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了海-空界面粗糙度对水下无人机（AUV）到空中无人机（UAV）光无线通信（OWC）链路性能的影响，采用Cox-Munk和ECKV两种实验验证模型，推导了ECKV模型的解析表示，并通过解析和蒙特卡洛方法评估了链路遍历容量，为系统设计提供了实用见解。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 海-空界面的随机特性会引入光学畸变，损害AUV-to-UAV OWC链路的可靠性，因此需要定量研究海面粗糙度对通信性能的影响，以指导实际系统设计。

Method: 采用Cox-Munk和ECKV两种实验验证的海面粗糙度模型，推导ECKV模型的解析表示；结合解析分析与蒙特卡洛仿真，评估链路遍历容量。

Result: 得到了ECKV模型的易处理解析表示并基于实测海况数据验证；量化了工作距离、指向误差、接收视场角和太阳噪声水平对遍历容量的影响。

Conclusion: 海-空界面粗糙度对AUV-to-UAV OWC系统的链路遍历容量有显著影响，所提解析模型和评估方法可为实际系统设计提供有价值的参考。

Original Abstract: Air-sea surface interactions play a critical role in underwater-to-air optical wireless communication (OWC) links, particularly in vertical autonomous underwater vehicle (AUV) to unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) scenarios, where the stochastic nature of the sea surface introduces optical distortions that impair link reliability. This work investigates the impact of air-sea surface roughness on AUV-to-UAV OWC systems using two experimentally validated models: the classical Cox-Munk and the Elfouhaily-Chapron-Katsaros-Vandemark (ECKV). A tractable analytical representation of the ECKV model is derived and validated against measured sea-state data. Using both analytical and Monte Carlo approaches, the link ergodic capacity is evaluated with particular emphasis on operating range, pointing errors, receiver field-of-view, and solar noise level, providing practical system design insights.

中文摘要翻译: 海-空界面相互作用对水下到空中光无线通信（OWC）链路起着关键作用，特别是在垂直自主水下航行器（AUV）到无人机（UAV）通信场景中，海面的随机特性会引入光学畸变，从而损害链路的可靠性。本研究利用两种经过实验验证的模型——经典的Cox-Munk模型和Elfouhaily-Chapron-Katsaros-Vandemark（ECKV）模型——探究了海-空界面粗糙度对AUV-to-UAV OWC系统的影响。推导了ECKV模型的易处理解析表示，并基于实测海况数据进行了验证。采用解析方法和蒙特卡洛方法，重点评估了链路遍历容量在工作距离、指向误差、接收视场角和太阳噪声水平等参数下的表现，为实际系统设计提供了实用见解。

</details>

### [99] [Information as Maximum-Caliber Deviation: A bridge between Integrated Information Theory and the Free Energy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12536)
*Alexander Kearney*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过定义信息为有限时间窗口内实际动力学与约束最大熵（MaxCal）路径系综的偏差ψ，建立了自由能原理（FEP）与整合信息论（IIT 3.0）之间的严格数学桥梁，揭示了IIT的核心因果/效应库可直接从MaxCal变分原理导出，并证明了该框架与主动推理的数学对偶性。在马尔可夫链中心极限定理和伊辛模型大偏差理论下，信息ψ等价于预测编码中的预测误差，为解释神经培养实验中Φ的“山形轨迹”提供了物理基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: FEP与IIT虽在概念层面已有统一尝试并得到实证支持，但缺乏严格的数学映射，限制了其精确性和可检验性。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为两大框架提供严谨的数学桥梁。

Method: 将信息定义为有限时间窗口内实际动力学与约束最大熵（MaxCal）路径系综的偏差ψ；从MaxCal变分原理重新推导IIT 3.0的因果/效应库；建立与主动推理的数学对偶性（在Langevin动力学下CMEP与主动推理对偶）；应用马尔可夫链中心极限定理和大偏差理论分析伊辛模型。

Result: 信息ψ在中心极限定理和大偏差理论框架下等价于预测编码中的预测误差；该框架与违反涨落-耗散定理（FDT）的意识理论兼容；为神经培养中Φ的“山形轨迹”现象提供了理论解释。

Conclusion: 本文提供了FEP、IIT和热力学认知框架（如FDT违反理论）之间融合的物理和数学基础，为将IIT扩展到新的动力学机制提供了原理性路径。

Original Abstract: The Free Energy Principle (FEP) is a leading framework for mathematically modeling self-organization and learning, while Integrated Information Theory (IIT) is a computational ontology of consciousness oriented around irreducible cause and effect. While conceptual unifications have been proposed and appear to be supported by empirical findings, the absence of a rigorous mathematical mapping places upper bounds on their precision and testability. This work proposes that information can be defined as the deviation $ψ$ of realized dynamics from a constrained maximum-caliber (MaxCal) path ensemble over a finite time horizon. Under this definition, each of the cause/effect repertoires central to IIT 3.0 emerge directly from MaxCal variational principles, allowing IIT's phenomenological calculus to be re-derived from constrained entropy-maximization (CMEP). This framework supplies a theoretical bridge to active inference, which is mathematically dual to CMEP under Langevin dynamics, and offers a principled route for extending IIT to new dynamical regimes. When the approach is applied under the Central Limit Theorem (CLT) for Markov chains and via large deviations theory (LDT) to Ising models, information $ψ$ is shown to be equivalent to prediction error under accompanying predictive coding models. This may hold relevance to the ``hill-shaped trajectory'' of $Φ$ observed in neuronal cultures adapting to sensory inputs. Together, these results provide a physically and mathematically grounded rationale for studying the convergence of FEP, IIT, and thermodynamic frameworks of cognition such as recent work grounding consciousness in violations of the Fluctuation-Dissipation Theorem (FDT).

中文摘要翻译: 自由能原理（FEP）是建模自组织与学习的领先数学框架，而整合信息论（IIT）则是一种围绕不可约因果效应的意识计算本体论。尽管已有概念层面的统一尝试且得到实证发现支持，但缺乏严格的数学映射限制了它们的精确性和可检验性。本文提出，信息可定义为有限时间窗口内实际动力学与约束最大熵（MaxCal）路径系综的偏差ψ。在此定义下，IIT 3.0核心的因果/效应库直接从MaxCal变分原理中涌现，使IIT的现象学演算可从约束熵最大化（CMEP）重新推导。该框架为主动推理提供了理论桥梁——在Langevin动力学下主动推理与CMEP数学对偶——并为将IIT扩展到新的动力学机制提供了原理性路径。将本方法应用于马尔可夫链中心极限定理（CLT）以及通过大偏差理论（LDT）分析伊辛模型时，信息ψ被证明等价于伴随预测编码模型中的预测误差。这或许对神经培养适应感觉输入时观察到的Φ“山形轨迹”具有解释意义。综合而言，这些结果为研究FEP、IIT与热力学认知框架（如近期基于违反涨落-耗散定理（FDT）的意识理论）的收敛提供了物理和数学上的坚实基础。

</details>


### [100] [Why the Unfinished Keeps Returning: Canxianization and the Dynamics of Conscious Priority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12543)
*Hengjin Cai,Tianqi Cai*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出“残念化”（Canxianization）概念，描述一种扰动因无法被自我–世界边界闭合而反复占据意识优先级的认知机制，并将其与情绪唤醒、记忆强度、蔡格尼克效应、好奇心、预测误差和侵入性思维等现象区分开来。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有理论未能充分解释为何某些内容在触发条件消失后仍能反复返回意识，而非仅仅保持高记忆强度或高情绪唤醒。作者意图提出一个独立的理论框架来捕捉这一独特现象。

Method: 提出“残念化”理论框架，定义其关键条件（归因于自我–世界边界、价值标记、因果/行动闭合受阻、元认知耦合到自我模型）；区分潜在残念强度与观察到的意识复现；引入复现优先指数（RPI）和残念更新指数（CUI）；提出“冷残念化”作为关键判别维度；设计重置抗性测试与利害转移测试用于人工系统。

Result: 从理论上将残念化与情绪唤醒、记忆强度、蔡格尼克效应、好奇心、预测误差及侵入性思维等现象区分开来；识别出“冷残念化”（由结构不完整性驱动而非情感唤醒）这一关键判别维度；提出可通过重置抗性测试和利害转移测试在人工系统中进行验证。

Conclusion: 残念化不是记忆的持久性，而是失败的自我–世界修复。未完成的内容并非仅仅是停留在那里；当它关系到自我且抗拒闭合时，它就会在意识中复现。

Original Abstract: Some conscious contents disappear after access; others return repeatedly, long after their triggering conditions have ceased. We propose Canxianization as the process by which a perturbation becomes closure-resistant self-relevant unfinishedness and thereby acquires recurrent conscious priority. The theory distinguishes this phenomenon from emotional arousal, memory strength, the Zeigarnik effect, curiosity, prediction error, and intrusive thought. A perturbation becomes canxianized when it is attributed to the self-world boundary, value-marked, blocked from causal or action closure, and metacognitively coupled to the self-model. We distinguish latent canxian strength from observed conscious recurrence, and introduce a Recurrent Priority Index and a Canxian Update Index to separate productive from pathological recurrence. Cold Canxianization, recurrence driven by structural incompleteness rather than affective arousal, is identified as a critical discriminant. Reset Resistance and Stake Transfer tests are proposed for artificial systems. Canxianization is not memory persistence; it is failed self-world repair. The unfinished does not merely remain. When it concerns the self and resists closure, it returns.

中文摘要翻译: 有些意识内容在访问后便消失；另一些则在触发条件早已不复存在后反复返回。我们提出“残念化”（Canxianization）作为这样一个过程：通过它，一种扰动变成了闭合抵抗性的、与自我相关的未完成性，并由此获得反复出现的意识优先权。该理论将此现象与情绪唤醒、记忆强度、蔡格尼克效应、好奇心、预测误差以及侵入性思维加以区分。当一个扰动被归因于自我–世界边界、被赋予价值标记、在因果或行动闭合上受阻、并在元认知层面耦合到自我模型时，它便被残念化了。我们区分了潜在残念强度与观察到的意识复现，并引入了复现优先指数（Recurrent Priority Index）和残念更新指数（Canxian Update Index），以将生产性复现与病理性复现分开。冷残念化（Cold Canxianization），即由结构不完整性而非情感唤醒驱动的复现，被识别为一个关键判别维度。针对人工系统，我们提出了重置抗性测试（Reset Resistance Test）和利害转移测试（Stake Transfer Test）。残念化并非记忆的持续；它是失败的自我–世界修复。未完成的内容并非仅仅滞留。当其关乎自我且抗拒闭合时，它便会返回。

</details>


### [101] [Implicit Behavioral Decoding from Next-Step Spike Forecasts at Population Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12999)
*John R. Minnick,Jesus Gonzalez-Ferrer,Kamran Hussain,Jinghui Geng,Ash Robbins,Mohammed A. Mostajo-Radji,David Haussler,Jason Eshraghian,Mircea Teodorescu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个单一Mamba预测器，仅利用Neuropixels级别下一时刻锋电位计数进行训练，即可在前向传播中同时实现神经群体活动预测与行为状态解码。在Steinmetz视觉辨别基准上，其预测速率经轻量线性层解码后，在39个session、约27,000个神经元、1,994个保留试次上，小鼠选择解码准确率达75.7%，刺激侧解码达66.1%，均显著优于相同时间窗口下直接对原始锋电位计数进行线性解码的结果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 闭环脑机接口需要同时预测神经群体活动和读取动物行为状态，现有方法通常需要多个模型或复杂流程，本文希望用一个统一框架高效完成这两项任务。

Method: 使用单一Mamba架构作为预测器，仅训练于下一时刻Neuropixels尺度的锋电位计数；解码时在每个session上附加一个轻量级线性头，读取模型预测的速率而非原始锋电位计数，从而实现行为解码。

Result: 在Steinmetz视觉辨别基准上（39个session、约27,000个神经元、1,994个保留试次），Mamba预测速率解码小鼠选择达75.7±0.2%（约为随机水平的2.3倍），刺激侧达66.1±0.6%（约为随机水平的2倍）。相比500ms上下文窗口下对原始锋电位计数的线性解码器，Mamba在试次投票上分别高出4-6pp（反应）和4-6pp（刺激侧）。会话开始的校准模块约100-150个试次即可使解码效果接近渐近线（1-2pp以内），完整流程在50ms时间预算内可在工作站级GPU上运行。

Conclusion: Mamba预测器能够通过单一前向传播同时实现神经群体活动预测和行为解码，且解码性能优于在相同时间窗口下直接使用原始神经数据的方法，具备在闭环BCI实际部署中的计算效率和应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Closed-loop brain-computer interfaces often require both a forecast of upcoming neural population activity and a readout of the animal's behavioral state. A single Mamba forecaster, trained only on next-step spike counts at Neuropixels scale, can deliver both in one forward pass. A lightweight per-session linear head reading the model's predicted rates decodes behavior better than the same linear classifier reading the raw spike counts, under matched temporal context. We test on the Steinmetz visual-discrimination benchmark, which spans 39 sessions, roughly 27,000 neurons, and 1,994 held-out trials. Across three training seeds, Mamba's predicted rates decode mouse choice at 75.7$\pm$0.2% trial vote, roughly 2.3 times chance level, and stimulus side at 66.1$\pm$0.6%, about twice chance. Compared to a matched 500 ms-context linear decoder on the raw spike counts, Mamba wins at trial vote by 4-6 pp on response and 4-6 pp on stimulus side. A session-start calibration block of about 100-150 trials brings the readout within 1-2 pp of asymptote, and the full pipeline fits inside the 50 ms bin budget on workstation-class GPUs typical of tethered chronic Neuropixels recordings.

中文摘要翻译: 闭环脑机接口通常既需要预测即将到来的神经群体活动，也需要读取动物的行为状态。一个单一的Mamba预测器，仅通过下一时刻Neuropixels尺度的锋电位计数进行训练，即可在一次前向传播中同时完成这两项任务。在每个session上附加一个轻量级线性头，读取模型预测的速率，其在相同时间上下文下解码行为的表现优于使用相同线性分类器直接读取原始锋电位计数的结果。我们在Steinmetz视觉辨别基准上进行测试，该基准涵盖39个session、约27,000个神经元和1,994个保留试次。在三种训练随机种子下，Mamba预测速率解码小鼠选择的试次投票准确率为75.7±0.2%，约为随机水平的2.3倍；解码刺激侧的准确率为66.1±0.6%，约为随机水平的2倍。与在原始锋电位计数上使用匹配的500ms上下文线性解码器相比，Mamba在反应试次投票上高出4-6个百分点，在刺激侧也高出4-6个百分点。会话开始时约100-150个试次的校准模块即可将解码结果收敛至渐近线的1-2个百分点以内，且完整流程在典型的tethered慢性Neuropixels记录所用工作站级GPU上，可在50ms时间预算内完成。

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Learning When to Act: Communication-Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Run-Time Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12561)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Cody Fleming,Tristan Schuler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于李雅普诺夫安全盾牌的安全强化学习框架，该框架让单一策略同时学习控制输入和通信高效的时序决策，在倒立摆、推车-杆和四旋翼飞行器上相比基线方法实现了1.45-3.51倍的平均采样间隔提升，并通过运行时保障层提供比约束MDP更强的严格安全保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统安全强化学习关注智能体应该做什么（控制输入），而忽略了何时需要执行动作（时序决策）。本文旨在证明单一策略可以同时学习控制输入和通信高效的时序决策，在点态李雅普诺夫安全盾牌的保护下实现比固定LQR控制器在相同平均采样率下更稳定的表现。

Method: 提出了一种基于点态李雅普诺夫安全盾牌的框架，结合CARE-based LQR备份、李雅普诺夫证书和经典Lyapunov-STC。采用运行时保障（RTA）层，通过一步前向李雅普诺夫预测和预计算的LQR备份覆盖策略，提供比期望约束MDP更严格的保证。使用CARE导出的李雅普诺夫奖励函数跨环境迁移，通过单一权重w_c控制稳定性-通信权衡。

Result: 在倒立摆、推车-杆和平面四旋翼上，学习策略的平均采样间隔（MSI）分别是李雅普诺夫触发基线的1.91倍、1.45倍和3.51倍；固定LQR控制器在相同平均采样率下不稳定；移除RTA盾牌使MSI降低1.27-1.84倍并恶化状态范数；偏好条件扩展以2/11的训练计算量恢复完整权衡前沿；SAC实验证明算法无关性；在12状态3D四旋翼案例中扩展到高维系统，对±30%质量变化和扰动表现优雅降级。

Conclusion: 自适应时序决策（而非更低平均采样率）是安全稀疏控制的关键；点态李雅普诺夫安全盾牌是实现严格安全保障的有效手段；CARE导出的李雅普诺夫奖励函数可跨环境迁移；RTA盾牌不可或缺；该框架可扩展到经典STC难以处理的高维系统，并对模型不确定性和扰动具有鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) typically asks $\textit{what}$ an agent should do. We ask $\textit{when}$ it needs to act, and show that a single policy can jointly learn control inputs and communication-efficient timing decisions under a pointwise Lyapunov safety shield. We focus on stabilization around a known equilibrium, where CARE-based LQR backups, Lyapunov certificates, and classical Lyapunov-STC are well defined, enabling clean comparison against analytical baselines. A run-time assurance (RTA) layer overrides the policy via a one-step-ahead Lyapunov prediction and a precomputed LQR backup, providing a strictly stronger guarantee than constrained MDP methods that enforce safety only in expectation. On an inverted pendulum, cart--pole, and planar quadrotor, the learned policy achieves $1.91\times$, $1.45\times$, and $3.51\times$ higher mean inter-sample interval (MSI) than a Lyapunov-triggered baseline; a fixed LQR controller at the same average rate is unstable on all three plants, showing that adaptive timing, not a lower average rate, makes sparsity safe. A CARE-derived Lyapunov reward transfers across environments without redesign, with a single weight $w_c$ controlling the stability--communication tradeoff; ablations confirm the RTA shield is essential, with its removal reducing MSI by $1.27$--$1.84\times$ and degrading state norms. A preference-conditioned extension recovers the full tradeoff frontier from one model at $\tfrac{2}{11}$ of training compute, and SAC experiments show the results are algorithm-agnostic across discrete and continuous domains. A 12-state 3D quadrotor case study extends the framework to higher-dimensional systems where classical STC is intractable, and robustness to $\pm30\%$ mass variation and disturbances shows graceful degradation, with the RTA absorbing what the learned policy cannot.

中文摘要翻译: 安全强化学习通常关注智能体应该做什么。我们则关注智能体何时需要行动，并证明单一策略可以在点态李雅普诺夫安全盾牌下同时学习控制输入和通信高效的时序决策。我们专注于已知平衡点附近的镇定问题，其中基于CARE的LQR备份、李雅普诺夫证书和经典Lyapunov-STC都有明确定义，从而能够与解析基线进行清晰的比较。运行时保障（RTA）层通过一步前向李雅普诺夫预测和预计算的LQR备份覆盖策略，提供了比仅在期望意义上保证安全的约束MDP方法更严格的安全性保证。在倒立摆、推车-杆和平面四旋翼上，学习策略的平均采样间隔（MSI）分别比李雅普诺夫触发基线高出1.91倍、1.45倍和3.51倍；而相同平均采样率下的固定LQR控制器在这三个系统上均不稳定，表明自适应时序（而非更低平均采样率）才是安全稀疏控制的关键。CARE导出的李雅普诺夫奖励可跨环境迁移而无需重新设计，单一权重w_c即可控制稳定性-通信权衡；消融实验确认RTA盾牌不可或缺，移除后MSI降低1.27-1.84倍，状态范数恶化。偏好条件扩展以一个模型仅2/11的训练计算量恢复了完整权衡前沿，SAC实验表明该结果在离散和连续域上均具有算法无关性。12状态3D四旋翼案例研究将框架扩展到经典STC难以处理的高维系统，对±30%质量变化和扰动的鲁棒性表现为优雅降级，RTA吸收了学习策略无法处理的情况。

</details>


### [103] [OceanCBM: A Concept Bottleneck Model for Mechanistic Interpretability in Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12639)
*Sanah Suri,Kieran Ringel,Maike Sonnewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OceanCBM是一个用于海洋动力学时空预测与物理解释的概念瓶颈模型。它通过混合监督学习将信息路由到由地球物理流体动力学预定义的概念层和一个“自由”概念层，在保持预测性能的同时实现可解释、物理可解释的表征，并明确刻画了可解释性-性能之间的权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 极端海洋现象（如海洋热浪）难以预测且难以诊断，传统预测方法无法揭示物理驱动因素，而现有机器学习方法虽预测能力强但缺乏可解释性，且对物理真实性的保证有限。因此，需要一种既能保持预测技能又能提供物理可解释表征的模型。

Method: 提出了OceanCBM，一种用于时空预测和物理机制探查的概念瓶颈模型。该模型采用混合监督学习来预测混合层热含量（海洋热浪的关键前兆），通过中间概念层路由信息，该层包含由地球物理流体动力学预定义的指定概念和一个“自由”概念。这种设计施加了软物理结构而不过度约束模型，自由概念既能正则化概念预测又能捕获残差物理过程。

Result: 在集成初始化实验中，混合监督学习方法产生了一致的物理解释表征，而仅使用预测监督或仅使用概念监督的基线方法在预测性能相似的情况下学习了高度可变的潜在结构。OceanCBM在不牺牲预测技能的前提下实现了可解释、物理可解释的表征，并明确刻画了可解释性-性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 混合监督的概念瓶颈模型能够有效融合物理先验知识，在保持预测性能的同时提供物理可解释的中间表征，为海洋动力学等复杂地球物理系统的机理诊断提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: Extreme ocean phenomena are challenging not only to predict but to diagnose, as accurate forecasts alone do not reveal the underlying physical drivers. While recent machine learning approaches achieve strong predictive skill, they remain largely opaque and provide limited guarantees of fidelity to ground-truth physics. We introduce OceanCBM, the first concept bottleneck model (CBM) for spatiotemporal prediction and mechanistic interrogation of ocean dynamics. OceanCBM uses mixed supervision to predict mixed layer heat content, a key precursor of marine heatwaves, while routing information through an intermediate layer of prescribed concepts derived from geophysical fluid dynamics and a 'free' concept. This design imposes soft physical structure without over-constraining the model, and the free concept both regularizes concept predictions and captures residual physical processes. Across ensemble initializations, we show that mixed supervision yields consistent mechanistic representations, whereas prediction-only and prescription-only baselines learn highly variable latent structures despite similar predictive performance. OceanCBM achieves interpretable, physically grounded representations without sacrificing skill, explicitly characterizing the interpretability-performance trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 极端海洋现象不仅难以预测，也难以诊断，因为准确的预测本身并不能揭示其背后的物理驱动因素。尽管最近的机器学习方法取得了强大的预测能力，但它们仍然在很大程度上不透明，且对真实物理保真度的保证有限。我们提出了OceanCBM，这是首个用于海洋动力学时空预测与物理解释探查的概念瓶颈模型。OceanCBM采用混合监督来预测混合层热含量——海洋热浪的关键前兆，同时将信息路由通过一个由地球物理流体动力学预定义的指定概念和一个“自由”概念组成的中间层。这种设计在不过度约束模型的前提下施加了软物理结构，自由概念既能正则化概念预测，又能捕获残差物理过程。在集成初始化实验中，我们表明混合监督产生了一致的物理解释表征，而仅依赖预测监督或仅依赖概念监督的基线方法在预测性能相似的情况下学习了高度可变的潜在结构。OceanCBM在不牺牲预测技能的前提下实现了可解释、物理可解释的表征，明确刻画了可解释性-性能之间的权衡。

</details>


### [104] [Learning to Decide with AI Assistance under Human-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12646)
*Nina Corvelo Benz,Eleni Straitouri,Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI辅助决策中，AI置信度与人类置信度之间的对齐程度如何影响学习最优决策的复杂度。通过将问题建模为在线上下文学习问题，理论证明了完美对齐能降低遗憾界，实验验证了理论结果的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究发现AI置信度与人类置信度之间的对齐程度与AI辅助决策效用正相关，但尚未阐明这种对齐如何影响通过重复交互学习最优决策的复杂度。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将二元预测与二元决策问题等价转化为具有完全反馈的双臂在线上下文学习问题；推导期望遗憾的下界；在完美对齐假设下推导上界；利用Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz不等式的推广进一步改进上界；通过真实人类实验数据验证理论鲁棒性。

Result: 1) 建立了期望遗憾的Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))下界；2) 完美对齐下可实现O(√(|H|·T log T))的期望遗憾；3) 当√|H|=O(log T)且B可数时，遗憾界可改进至O(√(T log T))；4) 真实数据实验表明理论对非完美对齐场景具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: AI与人类置信度的对齐能够显著降低学习最优辅助决策的复杂度（即遗憾界），这为设计更高效的人机协作系统提供了理论依据。

Original Abstract: It is widely agreed that when AI models assist decision-makers in high-stakes domains by predicting an outcome of interest, they should communicate the confidence of their predictions. However, empirical evidence suggests that decision-makers often struggle to determine when to trust a prediction based solely on this communicated confidence. In this context, recent theoretical and empirical work suggests a positive correlation between the utility of AI-assisted decision-making and the degree of alignment between the AI confidence and the decision-makers' confidence in their own predictions. Crucially, these findings do not yet elucidate the extent to which this alignment influences the complexity of learning to make optimal decisions through repeated interactions. In this paper, we address this question in the canonical case of binary predictions and binary decisions. We first show that this problem is equivalent to a two-armed online contextual learning problem with full feedback, and establish a lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{|H| \cdot |B| \cdot T} )$ on the expected regret any learner can attain, where $H$ and $B$ denote the sets of human and AI confidence values. We then demonstrate that, under perfect alignment between AI and human confidence, a learner can attain an expected regret of $O(\sqrt{|H| \cdot T\log T})$ and, when $\sqrt{|H|} = O(\log T)$ and $B$ is countable, a non-trivial generalization of the Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz inequality improves the regret bound to $O(\sqrt{T\log T})$. Taken together, these results reveal that alignment can reduce the complexity of learning to make decisions with AI assistance. Experiments on real data from two different human-subject studies where participants solve simple decision-making tasks assisted by AI models show that our theoretical results are robust to violations of perfect alignment.

中文摘要翻译: 人们普遍认为，当AI模型通过预测目标结果来辅助高风险领域的决策者时，应传达其预测的置信度。然而，经验证据表明，决策者往往难以仅凭这种传达的置信度来判断何时应信任预测。在此背景下，最近的理论和实证工作表明，AI辅助决策的效用与AI置信度和决策者自身预测置信度之间的对齐程度呈正相关。关键的是，这些发现尚未阐明这种对齐在多大程度上影响通过重复交互学习最优决策的复杂度。在本文中，我们在二元预测和二元决策的经典情形下回答了这个问题。我们首先证明该问题等价于一个具有完全反馈的双臂在线上下文学习问题，并建立了任何学习器所能达到的期望遗憾的下界为Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))，其中H和B分别表示人类和AI置信度值的集合。接着我们证明，在AI与人类置信度完全对齐的情况下，学习器能够达到O(√(|H|·T log T))的期望遗憾；而当√|H|=O(log T)且B可数时，Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz不等式的非平凡推广将遗憾界改进至O(√(T log T))。综合这些结果表明，对齐能够降低学习在AI辅助下做出决策的复杂度。我们在两项不同的人类受试者研究（参与者借助AI模型完成简单决策任务）的真实数据上进行的实验表明，我们的理论结果对非完美对齐的偏离具有鲁棒性。

</details>


### [105] [Runtime Monitoring of Perception-Based Autonomous Systems via Embedding Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12651)
*Parv Kapoor,Abigail Hammer,Ashish Kapoor,Karen Leung,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），直接在学习的嵌入空间中进行运行时监控，避免传统感知到逻辑命题映射的脆弱性和语义偏差，通过嵌入距离定义谓词，实现高层感知概念的高效监控。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统运行时监控依赖将连续传感器观测映射到离散逻辑命题，这种抽象在感知驱动的自主系统中需要额外的学习模块，计算开销大、脆弱且语义对齐差，难以表达高层感知概念。

Method: 提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），通过观测嵌入与参考嵌入之间的距离定义谓词，并结合时序算子表达时序延展和序列感知行为；引入ETL监控器评估有界嵌入轨迹上的规约，并设计共形校准程序提供可靠且面向安全的谓词评估。

Result: 在多个操作环境中，ETL与真实语义具有强经验一致性，能够准确监控时序组合行为。

Conclusion: ETL有效解决了感知驱动自主系统监控中传统逻辑抽象失效的问题，在高维嵌入空间中实现了可靠且语义对齐的运行时监控。

Original Abstract: Runtime monitoring of autonomous systems traditionally relies on mapping continuous sensor observations to discrete logical propositions defined over low-dimensional state variables. This abstraction breaks down in perception-driven settings, where such mappings require additional learned modules that are often computationally expensive, brittle, and semantically misaligned. In this work, we propose Embedding Temporal Logic (ETL), a temporal logic that performs monitoring directly in learned embedding spaces. ETL defines predicates through distances between observed embeddings and target embeddings derived from reference observations. This formulation allows specifications to capture high-level perceptual concepts, such as similarity to visual goals or avoidance of semantic regions, that are difficult or impossible to express using traditional predicates. By composing these predicates with temporal operators, ETL naturally expresses temporally extended and sequential perceptual behaviors. We introduce ETL monitors for evaluating specifications over bounded embedding traces, along with a conformal calibration procedure that provides reliable and safety-oriented predicate evaluation. We evaluate our approach across multiple manipulation environments to show that ETL achieves strong empirical agreement with ground-truth semantics, including accurate monitoring of temporally composed behaviors.

中文摘要翻译: 传统自主系统的运行时监控依赖于将连续传感器观测映射到基于低维状态变量定义的离散逻辑命题上。这种抽象在感知驱动的场景中会失效，因为此类映射需要额外的学习模块，而这些模块通常计算开销大、脆弱且语义对齐差。在这项工作中，我们提出嵌入时序逻辑（Embedding Temporal Logic, ETL），一种直接在学习的嵌入空间中进行监控的时序逻辑。ETL通过观测嵌入与源自参考观测的目标嵌入之间的距离来定义谓词。该形式化方法允许规约捕获高层感知概念（例如与视觉目标的相似性或避让语义区域），这些概念使用传统谓词难以甚至无法表达。通过将这些谓词与时序算子组合，ETL能够自然地表达时序延展和序列感知行为。我们引入了ETL监控器来评估有界嵌入轨迹上的规约，并设计了一种共形校准程序，用以提供可靠且面向安全的谓词评估。我们在多个操作环境中评估了我们的方法，结果表明ETL与真实语义具有强经验一致性，包括对时序组合行为的准确监控。

</details>


### [106] [Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation via Peer Successes and Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12652)
*Weichen Yu,Xiaomin Li,Yizhou Zhao,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Haixin Wang,Yinyi Luo,Chen Henry Wu,Gaurav Mittal,Matt Fredrikson,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多轮次在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）框架，利用学生模型同一提示下的多个采样轨迹（包括成功与失败）构建更丰富的教师信号，相比独立处理每个轨迹的标准方法显著提升推理任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型后训练中使用的稀疏验证器奖励仅指示轨迹是否成功，缺乏对推理过程成功或失败位置的细粒度指导。现有在线策略蒸馏方法独立处理每个学生生成轨迹，忽略了同一提示下多个尝试之间的相互信息。

Method: 提出MOPD框架，基于学生模型在同一提示下的局部轮次组（rollout group），将成功与失败的同伴轨迹同时作为教师条件信号。研究了两种同伴上下文构建方式：正向同伴模仿（仅用成功轨迹）和对比成功-失败条件（同时使用成功与失败轨迹）。

Result: 在竞争性编程、数学推理、科学问答和工具使用基准上，MOPD一致优于标准在线策略基线。教师信号分析表明，混合成功-失败上下文能使教师分数更好地与验证器奖励对齐，说明性能提升源于更忠实、实例自适应的监督信号。

Conclusion: 有效的在线策略蒸馏应利用学生多轮次试错行为，而非将各个轨迹视为孤立样本独立处理。

Original Abstract: Large language models are often post-trained with sparse verifier rewards, which indicate whether a sampled trajectory succeeds but provide limited guidance about where reasoning succeeds or fails. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers denser token-level supervision by training on student-generated trajectories, yet existing methods typically distill each rollout independently and ignore the other attempts sampled for the same prompt. We introduce Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), a peer-conditioned distillation framework that uses the student's local rollout group to construct more informative teacher signals. MOPD conditions the teacher on both successful and failed peer rollouts: successes provide positive evidence for valid reasoning patterns, while failures provide structured negative evidence about plausible mistakes to avoid. We study two peer-context constructions: positive peer imitation and contrastive success-failure conditioning. Experiments on competitive programming, mathematical reasoning, scientific question answering, and tool-use benchmarks show that MOPD consistently improves over standard on-policy baselines. Further teacher-signal analysis shows that mixed success-failure contexts better align teacher scores with verifier rewards, indicating that the gains arise from more faithful, instance-adaptive supervision. These results indicate that effective on-policy distillation should exploit the student's multi-rollout trial-and-error behavior rather than treating rollouts as isolated samples.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型通常通过稀疏验证器奖励进行后训练，这种奖励仅能指示采样轨迹是否成功，而无法提供关于推理成功或失败位置的具体指导。在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过训练学生模型自身生成的轨迹来提供更密集的逐词级监督，然而现有方法通常独立处理每个采样轨迹，忽略了同一提示下的其他尝试。本文提出多轮次在线策略蒸馏（MOPD），一种同伴条件蒸馏框架，利用学生模型的局部轮次组构建更具信息量的教师信号。MOPD将教师模型同时建立在成功与失败的同伴轮次之上：成功案例为有效推理模式提供正向证据，而失败案例则为需避免的常见错误提供结构化负向证据。我们研究了两种同伴上下文构建方式：正向同伴模仿与对比性成功-失败条件。在竞争性编程、数学推理、科学问答和工具使用基准上的实验表明，MOPD一致优于标准在线策略基线。进一步的教师信号分析显示，混合成功-失败上下文能使教师分数更好地与验证器奖励对齐，表明性能提升源于更忠实、实例自适应的监督。这些结果表明，有效的在线策略蒸馏应利用学生模型的多轮次试错行为，而非将各个轮次视为孤立样本处理。

</details>


### [107] [Plan Before You Trade: Inference-Time Optimization for RL Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12653)
*Eun Go,Rohan Deb,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FPILOT框架，一种受模型预测控制启发的推理时优化方法，使预训练的强化学习投资组合管理智能体能在推理时利用价格预测来动态调整策略，无需重新训练即可提升收益和风险调整指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的强化学习投资组合管理智能体通常作为静态策略训练和部署，无法在推理时利用价格预测信息，限制了策略在动态市场中的适应性和性能。

Method: 受模型预测控制启发，利用未来价格不依赖于单个智能体投资组合分配这一关键结构性洞察，使用预测模型生成多步价格轨迹，构建基于分配的想象回报目标，在推理时每步执行前优化策略。

Result: 在TradeMaster DJ30基准上对五种策略学习算法进行评估，FPILOT在总收益和风险调整指标（夏普比率、索提诺比率、卡尔玛比率）上均产生一致改进，随机策略比确定性策略受益更多。使用合成预测验证了收益随预测质量提升而改善。

Conclusion: FPILOT是一种即插即用的推理时优化框架，能与任何预训练智能体兼容，无需重新训练即可将策略适配到预测器输出，且性能随金融预测技术进步而持续提升。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents for portfolio management are typically trained and deployed as static policies, with no mechanism for using price forecasts at inference time. We propose $\text{FPILOT}$ (**Fin**ancial **P**lugin **I**nference-time **L**earning for **O**ptimal **T**rading), a plugin inference-time optimization framework inspired by Model Predictive Control (MPC). Our key structural insight is that future prices mostly do not depend on one agent's portfolio allocation, so a suitable predictive model can produce a multi-step price trajectory without iterative action-conditioned rollouts as in typical reinforcement learning. At each decision step, we use the forecaster's predicted price trajectory to construct an allocation-based imagined return objective, and optimize the policy at inference-time before executing one step of the trade. Our framework is compatible with any pre-trained agent and adapts the policy to the forecaster's predictions without any retraining. Evaluated across five policy learning algorithms on the TradeMaster DJ30 benchmark, $\text{FPILOT}$ produces consistent improvements in total return and return-based risk-adjusted metrics (Sharpe, Sortino, Calmar), with stochastic policies benefiting more than deterministic ones. Further, using synthetic forecasts at calibrated quality levels, we show that gains consistently improve with forecaster quality, suggesting that our performance will improve based on advances in financial forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 用于投资组合管理的强化学习智能体通常被训练和部署为静态策略，缺乏在推理时使用价格预测的机制。我们提出FPILOT（金融插件推理时学习以实现最优交易），一种受模型预测控制（MPC）启发的插件式推理时优化框架。我们的关键结构性洞察在于，未来价格大多不依赖于单个智能体的投资组合分配，因此合适的预测模型可以生成多步价格轨迹，而无需像典型强化学习那样进行迭代的、基于动作条件的分步推演。在每个决策步骤，我们利用预测器产生的预测价格轨迹来构建基于分配的想象回报目标，并在执行一步交易之前于推理时对策略进行优化。我们的框架与任何预训练智能体兼容，无需重新训练即可将策略适配到预测器的输出。在TradeMaster DJ30基准上对五种策略学习算法进行评估，FPILOT在总收益和基于收益的风险调整指标（夏普比率、索提诺比率、卡尔玛比率）上均产生一致的改进，其中随机策略比确定性策略受益更多。此外，使用校准质量水平的合成预测，我们表明收益随预测器质量的提升而持续改善，这意味着我们的表现将随着金融预测领域的进步而提升。

</details>


### [108] [scShapeBench: Discovering geometry from high dimensional scRNAseq data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12662)
*Andrew J Steindl,João Felipe Rocha,Brian Tshilengi Di Bassinga,Zachary Warren,Matthew Scicluna,César Miguel Valdez Córdova,Shabarni Gupta,Leire Torices,Daniel Neumann,Timothy J. Mann,Ihuan Gunawan,Dhananjay Bhaskar,John G Lock,Christine L Chaffer,Guy Wolf,Smita Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出scShapeBench基准数据集和scReebTower基线方法，用于单细胞数据形状的自动检测，以辅助下游分析流程选择。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单细胞数据分析流程通常预设数据形状（如聚类、轨迹或原型结构），选择合适的流程依赖专家人工判断。随着AI科学家自动化趋势发展，自动检测数据形状成为选择下游分析流程的关键需求。

Method: 构建scShapeBench基准数据集，包含合成数据（基于真实骨架图采样）和专家标注的真实单细胞数据（分为四类：聚类、单轨迹、多分支、原型）。提出scReebTower基线方法，利用扩散几何提取Reeb图，连接可视化与流程选择，并提供拓扑感知的评估指标。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上，将scReebTower与PAGA和Mapper进行比较，结果表明scReebTower优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 论文贡献包括：基准数据集、评估指标和自动化形状检测的基线方法，为单细胞数据形状检测提供标准化评估框架。

Original Abstract: High-dimensional point cloud data arise across many scientific domains, especially single-cell biology. The shapes or topologies of these datasets determine the types of information that can be extracted. For example, clustered data supports cell-type identification, trajectory structures support transition analysis, and archetypal structures capture continua of cellular behaviors. Existing analysis pipelines often assume a specific shape. The standard Seurat pipeline combines UMAP visualization with Louvain clustering and therefore assumes clustered data, while tools such as Monocle and SPADE assume tree-like structures, and flow-based models such as MIOFlow and Conditional Flow Matching target trajectories. Choosing which pipeline to apply is therefore often left to bioinformaticians who visually inspect datasets before selecting an analysis strategy. With the rise of agentic AI scientists, automating shape detection is increasingly important for selecting downstream analysis pipelines. To address this problem, we introduce scShapeBench, a benchmark dataset for shape detection containing both synthetic and expert-annotated single-cell datasets. Synthetic datasets are sampled from ground-truth skeleton graphs with controlled variance. Real single-cell datasets are curated from diverse sources and annotated by experts into four categories: clusters, single trajectory, multi-branching, and archetypal. We additionally introduce scReebTower, a baseline method that uses diffusion geometry to extract Reeb graphs and connect visualization with pipeline selection. We provide topology-aware evaluation metrics and compare scReebTower against PAGA and Mapper on synthetic and real data. Our results indicate that scReebTower outperforms existing baselines. Overall, our contributions span benchmarks, evaluation metrics, and a baseline for automated shape detection in single-cell data.

中文摘要翻译: 高维点云数据出现在众多科学领域，尤其是单细胞生物学中。这些数据集的形状或拓扑结构决定了可以提取的信息类型。例如，聚类数据支持细胞类型识别，轨迹结构支持转变分析，而原型结构捕获细胞行为的连续谱。现有的分析流程通常假设特定形状。标准的Seurat流程将UMAP可视化与Louvain聚类结合，因此假设数据为聚类结构；而Monocle和SPADE等工具假设树状结构；MIOFlow和Conditional Flow Matching等基于流的模型则面向轨迹。因此，选择应用哪个流程通常留给生物信息学家，他们通过目视检查数据集后再选择分析策略。随着智能AI科学家的兴起，自动化形状检测对于选择下游分析流程变得越来越重要。为解决这一问题，我们引入了scShapeBench，一个用于形状检测的基准数据集，包含合成数据和专家标注的单细胞数据集。合成数据从带有受控方差的真实骨架图中采样。真实单细胞数据集来自多种来源，并由专家标注为四类：聚类、单轨迹、多分支和原型。我们还引入了scReebTower，一种基线方法，使用扩散几何提取Reeb图，将可视化与流程选择连接起来。我们提供了拓扑感知的评估指标，并在合成和真实数据上将scReebTower与PAGA和Mapper进行比较。结果表明，scReebTower优于现有基线方法。总体而言，我们的贡献涵盖了单细胞数据自动化形状检测的基准、评估指标和基线方法。

</details>


### [109] [ODRPO: Ordinal Decompositions of Discrete Rewards for Robust Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12667)
*Nirmal Patel,Fei Wang,Inderjit Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ODRPO框架，通过将离散奖励分解为序数二元指标来隔离自动评分器噪声，从而在大语言模型的RLAIF对齐中实现更稳健的策略优化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLAIF方法依赖基于LLM的自动评分器提供离散奖励，但这类评分器存在固有不稳定性（提示敏感性和采样随机性），噪声奖励样本会污染GRPO和MaxRL等优势估计器的归一化统计量，降低全局学习信号。简单增加采样数或多数投票虽可降噪但计算成本过高。

Method: 提出序数分解鲁棒策略优化（ODRPO），将离散奖励分解为一组序数二元指标（代表逐步递增的成功阈值），对每个阈值独立计算和累积优势，防止异常评估污染全局更新，同时隐式构建方差感知的学习课程。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B模型上，ODRPO相比基线在FACTS-grounding-v2上提升14.8%，在Alpaca-Evals上提升7.5%，且训练开销可忽略不计。理论分析也确认了其优化稳定性。

Conclusion: ODRPO为现代RLAIF中噪声离散评估环境下的模型对齐提供了可扩展且鲁棒的框架，能在不增加计算开销的前提下显著提升对齐性能。

Original Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) utilizes Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback (RLAIF) for non-verifiable domains such as long-form question answering and open-ended instruction following. These domains often rely on LLM based auto-raters to provide granular, multi-tier discrete rewards (e.g., 1-10 rubrics) that are inherently stochastic due to prompt sensitivity and sampling randomness. We empirically verify the stochasticity of auto-raters that can propagate and corrupt standard advantage estimators like GRPO and MaxRL, as a noisy reward samples can skew normalization statistics and degrade the global learning signal. Empirically, sampling more rewards and taking majority voting may reduce the noise and improve performance, but this approach is computationally expensive. To address this bottleneck, we introduce $\textbf{O}$rdinal $\textbf{D}$ecomposition for $\textbf{R}$obust $\textbf{P}$olicy $\textbf{O}$ptimization ($\textbf{ODRPO}$), a framework that structurally isolates evaluation noise by decomposing discrete rewards into a sequence of ordinal binary indicators. By independently computing and accumulating advantages across these progressively challenging success thresholds, ODRPO prevents outlier evaluations from corrupting the global update while establishing an implicit, variance-aware learning curriculum. Empirically, ODRPO achieves robust performance on Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-4B models, outperforming baselines with relative improvements of upto 14.8% on FACTS-grounding-v2 and 7.5% on Alpaca-Evals. Critically, these gains are achieved with negligible training-time overhead, as ODRPO requires no additional compute per step compared to standard estimators. Supported by theoretical analysis confirming its optimization stability, ODRPO provides a scalable and robust framework for aligning models within the noisy, discrete evaluation landscape of modern RLAIF.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）的对齐利用来自AI反馈的强化学习（RLAIF）来处理非可验证领域，如长文本问答和开放式指令遵循。这些领域通常依赖基于LLM的自动评分器提供细粒度的多层离散奖励（例如1-10分制），由于提示敏感性和采样随机性，这些奖励本身具有固有不稳定性。我们通过实验验证了自动评分器的随机性会污染并破坏标准优势估计器（如GRPO和MaxRL），因为噪声奖励样本会扭曲归一化统计量并降低全局学习信号。实验表明，采样更多奖励并采用多数投票可以减少噪声并提升性能，但这种方法计算成本高昂。为解决这一瓶颈，我们提出了序数分解鲁棒策略优化（ODRPO），该框架通过将离散奖励分解为一组序数二元指标，从结构上隔离评估噪声。通过在这些逐步递增的成功阈值上独立计算和累积优势，ODRPO防止异常评估污染全局更新，同时建立了一个隐式的方差感知学习课程。实验结果表明，ODRPO在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B模型上实现了稳健的性能，在FACTS-grounding-v2和Alpaca-Evals上分别相对基线提升了14.8%和7.5%。关键是，这些增益仅带来可忽略不计的训练开销，因为ODRPO相比标准估计器每个步骤不需要额外计算。理论分析证实了其优化稳定性，ODRPO为在现代RLAIF的噪声离散评估环境中对齐模型提供了一个可扩展且鲁棒的框架。

</details>


### [110] [A Unified Perspective for Learning Graph Representations Across Multi-Level Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12685)
*Mohamed Mahmoud Amar,Nairouz Mrabah,Mohamed Bouguessa,Abdoulaye Baniré Diallo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的多尺度图对比学习框架，并引入无参数的自适应加权机制，在多个下游任务上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图自监督学习方法大多局限于单一图抽象层级（如节点级或图级），且对所有样本施加统一惩罚强度，导致优化灵活性不足和收敛状态模糊。

Method: 提出统一对比框架，融合节点级、邻近级、簇级和图级信息，通过正对相似度与负对相异度的线性组合进行集成；同时引入无参数细粒度自加权机制，自适应地对偏离目标值的得分赋予更高权重。

Result: 在真实数据集上的分类、聚类和链路预测任务中，该方法在单层级和多层级场景下均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效整合多层级图信息，无参数自加权机制提升了优化灵活性和收敛质量，同时消除了多任务GSSL中的超参数调优开销。

Original Abstract: Graph Self-Supervised Learning (GSSL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for generating high-quality representations for graph-structured data. While multi-scale graph contrastive learning has received increasing attention, many existing methods still predominantly focus on a single graph abstraction level. To address this limitation, we propose a unified contrastive framework that can target node-level, proximity-level, cluster-level, and graph-level information and integrate them through a linear combination of similarity scores on positive pairs and dissimilarity scores (i.e., similarity scores on negative pairs). Furthermore, current approaches typically assign uniform penalty strengths to all examples, which reduces optimization flexibility and leads to ambiguous convergence status. To overcome this, we introduce a novel parameter-free fine-grained self-weighting mechanism that adaptively assigns weights to individual similarity and dissimilarity scores. The proposed mechanism emphasizes the scores that deviate significantly from their target values. Our approach not only enhances optimization flexibility but also eliminates the computational overhead of hyperparameter tuning in conventional multi-task GSSL methods. Comprehensive experiments on real-world datasets show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art approaches across downstream tasks, including classification, clustering, and link prediction, in both single-level and multi-level scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 图自监督学习（GSSL）已成为为图结构数据生成高质量表示的有力范式。尽管多尺度图对比学习日益受到关注，但许多现有方法仍主要聚焦于单一图抽象层级。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一个统一的对比框架，能够同时针对节点级、邻近级、簇级和图级信息，并通过正对相似度与负对相异度（即负对上的相似度）的线性组合来集成这些信息。此外，现有方法通常对所有样本施加统一的惩罚强度，这降低了优化灵活性并导致收敛状态模糊。为克服这一问题，我们引入了一种新颖的无参数细粒度自加权机制，该机制自适应地为每个相似度和相异度得分分配权重。所提出的机制会强调那些显著偏离目标值的得分。我们的方法不仅增强了优化灵活性，还消除了传统多任务GSSL方法中超参数调优的计算开销。在真实数据集上的综合实验表明，我们的方法在分类、聚类和链路预测等下游任务中，无论是在单层级还是多层级场景下，均一致优于现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [111] [IGT-OMD: Implicit Gradient Transport for Decision-Focused Learning under Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12693)
*Benjamin Amoh,Geoffrey G. Parker,Wesley Marrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对延迟双层优化的决策聚焦学习方法IGT-OMD，证明了延迟双层优化中“陈旧性放大”这一独特失效模式，并通过隐式梯度传输结合在线镜像下降将传输误差从二次依赖降至线性依赖，实现了首个次线性遗憾界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在线决策聚焦学习中存在反馈延迟问题，标准单层延迟优化理论无法解释双层优化中梯度陈旧性与内层求解器敏感性耦合导致的更高遗憾，亟需针对性分析与算法。

Method: 提出IGT-OMD算法，在Online Mirror Descent框架中应用隐式梯度传输（Implicit Gradient Transport），利用存储的内层解重新评估当前参数下的陈旧梯度，并采用队列长度自适应步长。

Result: 理论证明双层优化下陈旧性带来二次增长的传输误差而IGT-OMD降至线性依赖；实验上IGT-OMD在LQR、Warcraft最短路径和Sinkhorn最优传输任务中相较单层基线降低17%-55%决策损失，传输收益从单位延迟的0.0%单调增长至50轮延迟的9.5%。

Conclusion: IGT-OMD有效解决了延迟双层优化中的陈旧性放大问题，实现了首个次线性遗憾界，在多个决策聚焦学习任务中显著优于单层基线。

Original Abstract: Decision-focused learning trains predictive models end-to-end against downstream decision loss, but online settings suffer delayed feedback: outcomes may not arrive for many environment interactions. We identify \emph{staleness amplification}, a failure mode unique to bilevel optimization under delay, in which gradient staleness couples with inner-solver sensitivity to inflate regret beyond single-level delay theory. We prove that any black-box delayed optimizer incurs an irreducible regret cost from inner-solver approximation error, and that gradient staleness contributes a quadratically growing transport error without bilevel-aware correction. Our algorithm, \textbf{IGT-OMD}, applies Implicit Gradient Transport to hypergradients within Online Mirror Descent, re-evaluating stale gradients at the current parameters using stored inner solutions. This method reduces transport error from a quadratic to a linear dependence on delay and achieves the first sublinear regret bound for delayed bilevel optimization with queue-length-adaptive step sizes. Controlled experiments provide a \emph{mechanistic fingerprint}: transport benefit is exactly $0.0\%$ ($p=1.00$) at unit delay and grows monotonically to $9.5\%$ at fifty rounds ($p<0.001$), isolating the correction's effect. On Linear Quadratic Regulator, Warcraft shortest-path, and Sinkhorn optimal transport, IGT-OMD reduces decision loss by $17$--$55\%$ relative to single-level baselines, with phase transitions matching the theory.

中文摘要翻译: 决策聚焦学习以端到端方式训练预测模型以优化下游决策损失，但在线场景存在反馈延迟：结果可能需经过多次环境交互后才到达。我们识别了\textbf{陈旧性放大}——一种延迟下双层优化特有的失效模式，其中梯度陈旧性与内层求解器敏感性耦合，使遗憾超出单层延迟理论。我们证明任何黑箱延迟优化器都会因内层求解器近似误差产生不可约的遗憾代价，且若无双层感知校正，梯度陈旧性会贡献二次增长的传输误差。我们的算法\textbf{IGT-OMD}将隐式梯度传输应用于在线镜像下降中的超梯度，利用存储的内层解在当前参数下重新评估陈旧梯度。该方法将传输误差从延迟的二次依赖降至线性依赖，并采用队列长度自适应步长，实现了延迟双层优化的首个次线性遗憾界。受控实验提供了\textbf{机制指纹}：传输收益在单位延迟时为精确的$0.0\%$（$p=1.00$），随延迟单调增长至五十轮时的$9.5\%$（$p<0.001$），隔离了校正的效果。在线性二次型调节器、Warcraft最短路径和Sinkhorn最优传输任务中，IGT-OMD相较单层基线降低了$17$–$55\%$的决策损失，相变点与理论一致。

</details>


### [112] [KAST-BAR: Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Modeling for Universal Neural Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13133)
*Haoning Wang,Wenchao Yang,Shuai Shen,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KAST-BAR模型，通过知识锚定的语义动态拓扑与双流层次注意力编码器，解决EEG基础模型在复杂时空拓扑建模和生理-语义模态差距上的不足，在21个数据集预训练后于6个下游任务上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型对复杂时空拓扑的建模能力不足，且低层次生理信号与高层次文本语义之间存在固有的模态差距，限制了通用神经解码的性能。

Method: 设计双流层次注意力(DSHA)编码器捕捉非欧几里得脑拓扑（局部时序+全局空间）；提出知识锚定语义剖析器(KASP)生成物理 grounded 的实例级文本轮廓；利用语义文本感知精炼器(STAR)通过潜在专家查询动态重构EEG表示。

Result: 在21个多样化数据集上进行大规模预训练，在6个下游任务上持续取得最优性能。

Conclusion: KAST-BAR有效将专家级医学知识整合到EEG信号表示中，通过动态对齐多层级脑拓扑的生理表示与专家级语义空间，显著提升了通用神经解码能力。

Original Abstract: While EEG foundation models have shown significant potential in universal neural decoding across tasks, their advancement remains constrained by the inadequacy modeling of complex spatiotemporal topology, as well as the inherent modality gap between low-level physiological signals and high-level textual semantics. To address these challenges, we propose a Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Model (KAST-BAR), which dynamically aligns physiological representations derived from multi-level brain topology with an expert-level semantic space. Specifically, we design a Dual-Stream Hierarchical Attention (DSHA) encoder that accurately captures the brain's intrinsic non-Euclidean topology by modeling local temporal dynamics with global spatial contexts. On this basis, a Knowledge-Anchored Semantic Profiler (KASP) is proposed to synthesize physically-grounded and instance-level textual profiles, which subsequently drive a Semantic Text-Aware Refiner (STAR) to dynamically reconstruct EEG representations using Latent Expert Queries. By conducting large-scale pre-training on 21 diverse datasets to build a foundation model, KAST-BAR effectively integrates expert-level medical knowledge into EEG signal representations, consistently achieving superior performance across six downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/KAST-BAR/KAST-BAR

中文摘要翻译: 尽管EEG基础模型在跨任务通用神经解码方面展现出巨大潜力，但其发展仍受限于对复杂时空拓扑建模能力的不足，以及低层次生理信号与高层次文本语义之间固有的模态差距。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种知识锚定的语义动态拓扑脑自回归模型（KAST-BAR），该模型将源自多层级脑拓扑的生理表示动态地对齐到专家级语义空间。具体而言，我们设计了一个双流层次注意力（DSHA）编码器，通过建模局部时间动态与全局空间上下文，精确捕捉大脑固有的非欧几里得拓扑结构。在此基础上，我们提出知识锚定语义剖析器（KASP），用于合成具有物理依据且实例级别的文本轮廓，进而驱动语义文本感知精炼器（STAR）通过潜在专家查询动态重构EEG表示。通过在21个多样化数据集上进行大规模预训练以构建基础模型，KAST-BAR有效地将专家级医学知识整合到EEG信号表示中，在六个下游任务上持续取得最优性能。我们的代码可在https://github.com/KAST-BAR/KAST-BAR获取。

</details>


### [113] [UFO: A Domain-Unification-Free Operator Framework for Generalized Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12700)
*Hanli Qiao,George Em Karniadakis,Muhammad Muniruzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UFO是一种跨域神经算子框架，通过在多个表征域之间进行自适应联合条件交互，实现了离散化解耦的算子学习，在多种分布偏移下展现出准确、鲁棒且物理一致的预测能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经算子方法通常在单一表征域（如物理空间、谱空间或隐空间）中实现算子，限制了模型对多模态信息的利用和对分布外数据的泛化能力。为此，本文提出跨域框架，以突破单一域表示的限制。

Method: 提出UFO（Domain-Unification-Free Operator）框架，通过在多个不同域上的表示之间进行自适应、联合条件化的交互，实现跨域神经算子学习。该框架支持离散化解耦，即输入函数可在与训练时不同的分辨率或位置上采样，输出可在任意分辨率上查询。

Result: 在四个互补基准（涵盖不连续输入、不规则采样与谱失配、非线性动力学、随机高频场）上，UFO在分布偏移条件下均展现出准确、鲁棒且物理一致的预测性能。

Conclusion: 跨域、相位调制的实现方式为离散化解耦的神经算子学习提供了一种强大的新框架。

Original Abstract: Neural operators have become an effective framework for learning mappings between function spaces, yet most existing architectures realize operators within a single representational domain, such as physical, spectral, or latent space. In this work, we introduce UFO (Domain-Unification-Free Operator), a cross-domain neural operator framework that realizes operators through adaptive, jointly conditioned interactions among representations defined on distinct domains. UFO enables discretization decoupling: the input function can be observed at resolutions or locations different from those used during training, while the solution can be queried at arbitrary output resolutions. Across four complementary benchmarks covering discontinuous inputs, irregular sampling with spectral mismatch, nonlinear dynamics, and stochastic high-frequency fields, UFO delivers accurate, robust, and physically coherent predictions under distribution shifts. These results establish cross-domain, phase-modulated realization as a powerful framework for discretization-decoupled neural operator learning.

中文摘要翻译: 神经算子已成为学习函数空间之间映射的有效框架，然而大多数现有架构仅在单一表征域（如物理空间、谱空间或隐空间）中实现算子。在这项工作中，我们引入了UFO（域统一自由算子），这是一种跨域神经算子框架，通过在不同域上的表示之间进行自适应、联合条件化的交互来实现算子。UFO实现了离散化解耦：输入函数可以在与训练时不同的分辨率或位置上被观测，而解可以在任意输出分辨率上被查询。在涵盖不连续输入、不规则采样与谱失配、非线性动力学以及随机高频场这四个互补基准上，UFO在分布偏移条件下提供了准确、鲁棒且物理一致的预测。这些结果表明，跨域相位调制实现方式为离散化解耦的神经算子学习建立了一个强大的框架。

</details>


### [114] [Do Fair Models Reason Fairly? Counterfactual Explanation Consistency for Procedural Fairness in Credit Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12701)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Counterfactual Explanation Consistency (CEC)框架，用于检测和缓解公平机器学习模型中的隐藏程序性偏差——即不同群体个体虽获得相同预测结果，但模型推理过程不同。该方法通过对齐个体与其反事实样本的特征归因来消除这种偏差。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有公平性机器学习算法主要关注预测结果的均等化，但满足结果公平度量并不保证模型对不同群体使用相同的推理逻辑。作者发现结果公平模型可能对个体应用根本不同的推理方式，这种"隐藏的程序性偏差"被标准公平性度量和算法所忽视。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Explanation Consistency (CEC)框架：1）最近邻反事实生成方法；2）用于积分梯度比较的修改基线；3）个体级程序公平性度量指标；4）相应的训练损失函数。通过对齐个体与其反事实对应体之间的特征归因来检测和缓解偏差。

Result: 在合成数据、German Credit、Adult Income和HMDA抵押贷款数据上的实验表明，结果公平基线模型存在显著的隐藏偏差，而CEC能够在较小的效用代价下大幅降低这种偏差。

Conclusion: 识别了"Regime B"（相同结果，不同推理）这一关键盲区，并证明了CEC框架能有效检测和缓解结果公平模型中被忽视的程序性偏差。

Original Abstract: Machine learning algorithms in socially sensitive domains (e.g., credit decisions) often focus on equalizing predictive outcomes. However, satisfying these metrics does not guarantee that models use the same reasoning for different groups. We show that existing outcome-fair models can still apply fundamentally different reasoning to individuals, a ``hidden procedural bias'' missed by standard fairness metrics and algorithms. We propose Counterfactual Explanation Consistency (CEC), a framework that detects and mitigates this bias by aligning feature attributions between individuals and their counterfactual counterparts. Key contributions include a nearest-neighbor counterfactual generation method, a modified baseline for integrated gradient comparisons, an individual-level procedural fairness metric, and a corresponding training loss. We introduce a taxonomy identifying ``Regime B'' (same outcome, different reasoning) as a critical blind spot. Experiments on synthetic data, German Credit, Adult Income, and HMDA mortgage data demonstrate that outcome-fair baselines exhibit substantial hidden bias, while CEC substantially reduces it with modest utility cost.

中文摘要翻译: 在信贷决策等社会敏感领域中，机器学习算法通常侧重于均等化预测结果。然而，满足这些度量并不能保证模型对不同群体使用相同的推理逻辑。我们发现，现有的结果公平模型仍可能对个体应用根本不同的推理方式，这是一种被标准公平性度量和算法所忽视的"隐藏程序性偏差"。我们提出了反事实解释一致性（CEC）框架，通过对齐个体与其反事实对应体之间的特征归因来检测和缓解这种偏差。主要贡献包括：一种最近邻反事实生成方法、一种用于积分梯度比较的修改基线、一个个体级程序公平性度量指标，以及相应的训练损失函数。我们引入了一个分类体系，将"机制B"（相同结果，不同推理）识别为关键盲区。在合成数据、German Credit、Adult Income和HMDA抵押贷款数据上的实验表明，结果公平基线模型表现出显著的隐藏偏差，而CEC能够在较小的效用代价下大幅降低这种偏差。

</details>


### [115] [Early Data Exposure Improves Robustness to Subsequent Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12705)
*Lawrence Feng,Gaurav R. Ghosal,Jacob Mitchell Springer,Ziqian Zhong,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，模型在事后训练（post-training）中获取的能力在后续微调中的保留程度，主要取决于上游训练方式（尤其是早期暴露），而非事后训练后的即时表现。早期暴露（将事后训练数据混入预训练）能显著提升能力保留的鲁棒性，其效果与回放和 dropout 互补。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究多关注如何在下游干预（如回放）来缓解微调时的遗忘，但忽略了上游训练方式本身对能力保留鲁棒性的影响。本文旨在系统研究上游训练选择（尤其是数据分配时机）如何决定能力在后续微调中的存留。

Method: 采用受控的三阶段语言模型流水线：预训练→事后训练（获取目标能力）→下游微调（新目标）。在135M和1B两个规模、两个事后训练领域、两个下游微调任务上进行实验，比较不同训练配方（尤其是早期暴露 vs. 仅事后训练）对能力保留的影响。

Result: （1）事后训练的即时表现不能可靠预测后续微调后的能力保留；（2）早期暴露（将事后训练数据混入预训练）持续改善上游性能与下游性能的前沿；（3）在计算量匹配实验中，最优分配点既不在纯预训练也不在纯事后训练；（4）回放和dropout（通常在微调时用于缓解遗忘）在事后训练阶段应用时与早期暴露互补。

Conclusion: 对后续微调的鲁棒性应作为上游训练的一级目标来对待，通过预防性方式（如早期暴露）而非微调时的反应性措施来实现。上游训练选择（而非事后训练后的即时表现）是决定能力长期保留的关键因素。

Original Abstract: How can we train models whose post-trained capabilities survive subsequent fine-tuning? Rather than focusing on downstream interventions to mitigate forgetting of upstream capabilities, we study how upstream training choices - that is, the manner in which a capability is acquired - shape how robustly that capability is retained. We investigate this question in a controlled three-stage language-model pipeline: pretraining, post-training to acquire a target capability, and downstream fine-tuning on a new objective. Across 135M and 1B models, two post-training domains, and two downstream fine-tuning tasks, we find that immediate post-training performance does not reliably predict retention after subsequent fine-tuning: training recipes that look equivalent immediately after post-training can retain the target capability very differently after subsequent fine-tuning. In particular, early exposure - mixing post-training data into pretraining - consistently improves the frontier between retained upstream performance and downstream performance. In compute-matched experiments, where the target data must be allocated between pretraining and post-training, we find that the optimum lies at neither extreme. Together with our other empirical and theoretical findings, this supports the view that post-training drives immediate specialization while early exposure improves robustness to later forgetting. Replay and dropout, typically used to mitigate forgetting as it occurs during fine-tuning, provide complementary gains to early exposure when applied during post-training. Our findings suggest that robustness to subsequent fine-tuning should be treated as a first-class objective of upstream training, addressed preventatively through choices like early exposure rather than reactively during fine-tuning itself.

中文摘要翻译: 如何训练模型使其事后训练获得的能力能够在后续微调中保留？本文不关注下游缓解遗忘的干预措施，而是研究上游训练选择——即能力获取的方式——如何塑造该能力被保留的鲁棒性。我们在一个受控的三阶段语言模型流水线中研究这一问题：预训练、事后训练（获取目标能力）、以及在新目标上的下游微调。在135M和1B模型、两个事后训练领域以及两个下游微调任务上，我们发现事后训练的即时表现并不能可靠预测后续微调后的保留情况：在事后训练后立即看起来等价的训练配方，在后续微调后可以以非常不同的方式保留目标能力。特别地，早期暴露——将事后训练数据混入预训练——持续改善保留的上游性能与下游性能之间的前沿。在计算量匹配的实验中（目标数据必须在预训练和事后训练之间分配），我们发现最优分配点既不在于任何极端。结合我们其他的经验和理论发现，这支持了如下观点：事后训练驱动即时专门化，而早期暴露则改善对后续遗忘的鲁棒性。回放和dropout（通常用于在微调发生时缓解遗忘）在事后训练过程中应用时，与早期暴露提供互补的增益。我们的发现表明，对后续微调的鲁棒性应作为上游训练的一级目标来对待，通过诸如早期暴露之类的预防性方式而非在微调本身中的反应性措施来加以应对。

</details>


### [116] [Layer-wise Representation Dynamics: An Empirical Investigation Across Embedders and Base LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12714)
*Jingzhou Jiang,Yi Yang,Kar Yan Tam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层表示动力学（LRD）框架，通过三种逐层测量方法分析31个模型在30个MTEB任务上的表示变化，并展示了其在无标签模型选择和推理时层剪枝中的应用价值。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代语言模型不同层的隐藏状态变化显著，但现有逐层分析通常只关注单一变化维度，缺乏统一的、多维度的分析框架来全面理解层间表示动态。

Method: 提出LRD框架，包含三种逐层测量：Frenet（Grassmann流形上的速度和曲率，衡量全局子空间运动）、邻域保留分数（NRS，衡量局部近邻保留程度）、图过滤互信息（GFMI，衡量与最终层的对齐程度）。应用于31个模型（编码器、解码器嵌入模型及基础LLM）在30个MTEB任务上。

Result: (1) LRD揭示了仅从最后一层表示无法察觉的架构和任务级差异；(2) 三种模型级得分均与下游MTEB性能正相关，其中端到端子空间位移d_{0,L}最强；(3) 在15%和20%剪枝预算下，GFMI是唯一优于随机剪枝的规则，且在所有预算下中位变化最优；Frenet仅在最低预算下有效，NRS无法从模型选择迁移到剪枝任务。

Conclusion: 逐层结构信息既能用于模型解释，也能指导实际部署决策（如模型选择与推理时层剪枝）。

Original Abstract: Hidden states change substantially across the layers of modern language models, but most layer-wise analyses focus on one aspect of that change. We propose Layer-wise Representation Dynamics (LRD), a framework with three layer-wise measurement families: Frenet (Grassmann speed and curvature) for global subspace motion, Neighborhood Retention Score (NRS) for local nearest-neighbor retention, and Graph Filtration Mutual Information (GFMI) for alignment with the final layer. Applying LRD to 31 models (encoder-based and decoder-based embedders, plus base LLMs) on 30 MTEB tasks reveals architectural and task-level differences that are not apparent from final-layer representations alone. We then use LRD for two applications: label-free model selection and inference-time layer pruning. For selection, all three model-level scores correlate positively with downstream MTEB performance, with end-to-end subspace displacement (d_{0,L}) the strongest, and the same direction holds on a smaller base-LLM MMLU panel. For pruning, GFMI is the only measurement-guided rule that beats Random at the 15% and 20% budgets and has the best median change at every budget. Frenet is effective only at the lightest budget, while NRS does not transfer from model selection to pruning. These results show that layer-wise structure provides signal for both interpretation and deployment decisions.

中文摘要翻译: 现代语言模型各层的隐藏状态存在显著变化，但大多数逐层分析仅关注单一维度的变化。本文提出层表示动力学（Layer-wise Representation Dynamics, LRD）框架，包含三种逐层测量族：Frenet（Grassmann速度和曲率，用于全局子空间运动）、邻域保留分数（Neighborhood Retention Score, NRS，用于局部近邻保留）和图过滤互信息（Graph Filtration Mutual Information, GFMI，用于与最终层的对齐）。将LRD应用于31个模型（包括基于编码器和解码器的嵌入模型，以及基础大语言模型）在30个MTEB任务上的表现，揭示了仅从最后一层表示无法察觉的架构和任务级差异。随后，我们将LRD用于两项应用：无标签模型选择和推理时层剪枝。在模型选择方面，所有三种模型级得分均与下游MTEB性能呈正相关，其中端到端子空间位移（d_{0,L}）相关性最强，且在较小规模的基础LLM MMLU面板上呈现相同趋势。在剪枝方面，GFMI是唯一在15%和20%预算下优于随机剪枝的测量引导规则，并且在所有预算下具有最佳中位变化值。Frenet仅在最低预算下有效，而NRS无法从模型选择迁移至剪枝任务。这些结果表明，逐层结构信息既能用于模型解释，也能指导实际部署决策。

</details>


### [117] [Scaling Laws for Mixture Pretraining Under Data Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12715)
*Anastasiia Sedova,Skyler Seto,Natalie Schluter,Pierre Ablin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型预训练中稀缺目标数据与通用数据的混合策略，发现重复使用目标数据15-20次是可行的，并提出了一个考虑重复影响的混合缩放定律，用于指导数据约束下的最优混合配置。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着语言模型规模扩大，数据需求急剧增长，但低资源语言或专业领域等目标数据源本身规模有限。常用的混合训练策略面临根本性权衡：目标数据太少会导致模型暴露不足，太多则造成重复过拟合。现有研究缺乏对这一权衡的系统性理解。

Method: 在超过2000次语言模型训练实验中，涵盖不同模型规模、目标数据大小以及多语言、领域特定、质量过滤等多种数据类型，系统研究了混合训练中重复次数对目标域性能的影响。基于实验结果，提出了一个考虑重复目标token价值递减和通用数据正则化作用的混合缩放定律。

Result: 发现重复是影响目标域性能的核心因素；混合训练能容忍比单源训练高得多的重复率（目标语料可重复使用15-20次）；最优重复次数取决于目标数据大小、计算预算和模型规模。

Conclusion: 通过优化所提出的重复感知混合缩放定律，可以计算有效混合配置，为数据受限条件下的预训练提供实用的混合建议。

Original Abstract: As language models scale, the amount of data they require grows -- yet many target data sources, such as low-resource languages or specialized domains, are inherently limited in size. A common strategy is to mix this scarce but valuable target data with abundant generic data, which presents a fundamental trade-off: too little target data in the mixture underexposes the model to the target domain, while too much target data repeats the same examples excessively, yielding diminishing returns and eventual overfitting. We study this trade-off across more than 2,000 language-model training runs spanning multiple model and target dataset sizes, as well as several data types, including multilingual, domain-specific, and quality-filtered mixtures. Across all settings, we find that repetition is a central driver of target-domain performance, and that mixture training tolerates much higher repetition than single-source training: scarce target corpora can be reused 15-20 times, with the optimal number of repetitions depending on the target data size, compute budget, and model scale. Next, we introduce a repetition-aware mixture scaling law that accounts for the decreasing value of repeated target tokens and the regularizing role of generic data. Optimizing the scaling law provides a principled way to compute effective mixture configurations, yielding practical mixture recommendations for pretraining under data constraints.

中文摘要翻译: 随着语言模型规模的扩大，它们所需的数据量也在增长——然而许多目标数据源，如低资源语言或专业领域，在规模上天然受限。一种常见的策略是将这些稀缺但宝贵的目标数据与丰富的通用数据混合，这带来了一个基本的权衡：混合中目标数据太少会导致模型对目标领域暴露不足，而目标数据太多则会过度重复相同样本，导致收益递减并最终过拟合。我们通过超过2000次语言模型训练实验系统研究了这一权衡，实验涵盖了多种模型规模和目标数据集大小，以及多种数据类型，包括多语言、领域特定和质量过滤混合。在所有设置中，我们发现重复是影响目标域性能的核心驱动因素，且混合训练对重复的容忍度远高于单源训练：稀缺目标语料可重复使用15-20次，最优重复次数取决于目标数据大小、计算预算和模型规模。接下来，我们引入了一个考虑重复影响的混合缩放定律，该定律兼顾了重复目标token价值递减以及通用数据的正则化作用。优化该缩放定律为计算有效混合配置提供了理论基础，从而得到数据约束条件下预训练的实用混合建议。

</details>


### [118] [Before the Last Token: Diagnosing Final-Token Safety Probe Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12726)
*Shravan Doda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现仅基于最终token隐藏状态的LLM安全探测器会遗漏分布在用户token序列前部的攻击痕迹，并提出了基于PCA-HMM轨迹模型的方法来弥补这一缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有final-token安全探测器在预填充阶段会遗漏分布于早期用户token表示中的越狱提示不安全证据，导致漏检大量越狱攻击。

Method: 在三个指令微调LLM上训练SafeSwitch式探测器；通过子空间分析探测瓶颈宽度的影响；进行token级预填充分析；提出仅用干净数据训练的PCA-HMM轨迹模型来恢复被遗漏的越狱检测。

Result: 探测器对干净有害提示召回率高，但漏检大量越狱且对安全相关良性提示产生误报；子空间分析表明漏检越狱与干净良性提示的差异方向未被探测器充分捕获；token级分析显示不安全证据出现在序列早期但未暴露于最终token读出；PCA-HMM轨迹模型能从用户内容预填充轨迹中恢复漏检，且不会产生naive token池化的灾难性误报。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的隐藏状态分析可作为final-token探测器的有效诊断补充。

Original Abstract: Final-token safety probes monitor a single hidden state after prompt prefill, but jailbreak prompts can contain probe-visible unsafe evidence distributed across earlier user-token representations that is missed by this readout. We study this prefill-time failure mode using SafeSwitch-style probes trained only on clean harmful and benign prompts across three instruction-tuned LLMs. The probes achieve high recall on clean harmful prompts, but miss many jailbreaks and can produce false positives on safety-adjacent benign prompts.
